### [1] [Why Do Few-Step Text Latents Fail When Image Latents Work? Non-Commitment at Sharp Categorical Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30705)
*Zhongyao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 值得关注 (77.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文证明连续文本潜空间上的少步确定性生成失败的根本原因是几何性的而非训练或规模问题：平滑的确定性映射无法在锐利分类读取前解决离散分支选择。论文提出DABI（读取锐度）和CCI（分类承诺度）两个诊断指标，证明连续文本解码器对边界对齐扰动的放大远超图像解码器，并给出两种逃逸机制：自回归解码器的分类承诺和随机重注入（SDE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性少步生成在连续图像潜空间上效果良好，但在连续文本潜空间上却崩溃为无意义文本。现有工作未能解释这一差异的根本原因，本文旨在从几何角度揭示其本质。

Method: 通过理论分析（定理3）证明后验均值终端步骤在决策边界附近的O(s(t))管状区域内以潜空间质量速率翻转token；提出DABI（解码器锐度）和CCI（分类承诺度）两个诊断指标；在多个公开检查点上测量连续文本解码器与图像解码器的差异；分析两种逃逸机制（自回归分类承诺和随机重注入SDE）；在理想分离区域推导匹配的锐度传输定律和维度相图。

Result: 连续文本解码器对边界对齐扰动的放大倍数从5×10²到>10⁵，而图像解码器的DABI≈1。确定性ODE在K=4步时PPL为294，而SDE在相同模型上为50。分离M个模式所需的确定性刚度在潜空间维度Ω(log M)时以Θ(√log M)增长，在固定维度时以M^{1/n}增长。

Conclusion: 确定性-连续类方法中精度-深度-刚度之间存在不可约的权衡，两种逃逸机制（自回归分类承诺和随机重注入）都走出了这一框架。这为理解连续语言模型（如ELF、CoLa）的生成失败提供了理论基础。

Classification reason: 论文研究连续文本潜空间中的少步确定性生成为何失败，核心关注连续语言建模中的几何问题（非承诺性、解码器锐度、分类读取），属于连续语言模型与语言流（continuous_language_flow）这一规范子主题的核心范畴。

Importance reason: Directly addresses the fundamental geometric failure of few-step deterministic generation on continuous text latents, a core challenge in the user's top-priority continuous language model subtopic. Signals: continuous language model, few-step generation failure, geometric cause, decoder sharpness, DABI and CCI diagnostics

Abstract: Deterministic few-step generation succeeds on continuous image latents but collapses to incoherent text on continuous text latents, and we show the cause is geometric rather than a training or scaling deficiency: a smooth, regularity-limited deterministic map cannot resolve a discrete branch choice before a sharp categorical readout, so few-step failure is governed by decoder sharpness, not transport accuracy. In the overlapping regime of real text autoencoders, we prove (Theorem 3) that the posterior-mean terminal step flips tokens at the rate of the latent mass in an $O(s(t))$ tube around decision boundaries. Two diagnostics, DABI (readout sharpness) and CCI (categorical commitment), measured on published checkpoints show that four independently built continuous-text decoders amplify a boundary-aligned perturbation far beyond a norm-matched isotropic one (DABI from $5\times10^{2}$ to $>10^{5}$), while image decoders have DABI $\approx 1$. Two mechanisms escape the continuous bound: categorical commitment (autoregressive decoders succeed despite sharper readouts) and stochastic re-injection (deterministic ODE at $K=4$ gives PPL 294 versus SDE 50 on the same model). In the idealized separated regime we prove matching sharp transport laws, including a dimension phase diagram: the deterministic stiffness needed to separate $M$ modes grows as $\Theta(\sqrt{\log M})$ once the latent dimension is $\Omega(\log M)$ (and as $M^{1/n}$ in fixed dimension), with a depth-$B$ hierarchy giving a $\sqrt{B}$-smaller per-step peak (Theorems 5-7); a coarea identity links these to the overlapping tube (Theorem 17). The result is an accuracy-depth-stiffness tradeoff: within the deterministic-continuous class the cost is irreducible, and both escapes step outside it.

</details>

### [2] [Position: Vision-Language-Action Models Cannot Be Verified to Perform Physical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30686)
*Taozhao Chen, Ian Manchester, Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (71.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，论证当前VLA（视觉-语言-动作）系统的评估协议无法区分语义匹配与真正的物理推理能力，任务成功率指标存在可辨识性缺口，并提出通过受控实验设计分别测量语义和物理泛化的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA系统在机器人操作基准上表现不断提升，但这些提升被普遍解释为语义表征向物理执行泛化的证据。然而，这种解释缺乏独立验证，现有评估协议无法区分语义匹配和真正的物理推理能力，导致对VLA能力的归因存在根本性混淆。

Method: 本文通过将VLA策略分解为语义映射和物理动作决策两个组件，论证任务成功率指标无法区分这两种能力来源。提出通过引入受控变化的评估设计来分别测量语义泛化和物理泛化，从而在不访问模型内部的情况下实现因果归因。

Result: 识别出VLA评估中的"可辨识性缺口"（identifiability gap），指出基准性能提升与多种竞争性解释（语义匹配、分布重叠、真实物理泛化）一致。揭示了"叙事漂移"（narrative drift）现象——后续系统继承并强化了先前对性能提升的解释，而未隔离底层因果机制。

Conclusion: 当前VLA评估协议无法验证模型是否具备真正的物理推理能力。需要设计新的评估方法，通过受控实验分离语义和物理泛化，从而有意义地评估VLM在机器人中的作用——将其视为语义接口而非隐式的物理能力来源。

Classification reason: 该论文讨论VLA（视觉-语言-动作）系统在机器人操作中的物理推理能力验证问题，核心关注具身智能中语义泛化与物理泛化的区分，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向。映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制" (embodied_world_model)。

Importance reason: Directly challenges the validity of VLA evaluation protocols, which is critical for the user's focus on world models and physical reasoning. Signals: world model evaluation, physical reasoning verification, VLA policy decomposition, semantic vs. physical generalization, identifiability gap

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems, built on pretrained vision-language models (VLMs), have shown rapidly improving performance on robot manipulation benchmarks. These gains are commonly interpreted as evidence that semantic representations learned from internet-scale data transfer to physical execution generalization. This position paper argues that the assumption underlying this interpretation -- that semantic generalization is sufficient to support physical action decisions -- has not been independently verified and cannot be tested under current evaluation protocols. We support this claim by decomposing VLA policies into semantic mapping and physical action decision, and showing that task success rate -- the dominant evaluation metric -- cannot distinguish between these two sources of capability. As a result, improvements in benchmark performance are consistent with multiple competing explanations, including semantic matching, distributional overlap, and genuine physical generalization. We further argue that this identifiability gap has been reinforced through narrative drift, whereby successive systems inherit and strengthen prior interpretations of performance gains without isolating the underlying causal mechanism. To address this limitation, we propose a research direction based on evaluation designs that introduce controlled variation to separately measure semantic and physical generalization. Such designs make it possible to causally attribute performance without requiring access to model internals, and to empirically assess the role of VLM backbones as semantic interfaces rather than implicit sources of physical competence. Our goal is not to refute the role of VLMs in robotics, but to clarify the conditions under which claims of physical generalization can be meaningfully evaluated.

</details>


### [3] [DSIP: A Dynamic Coordination Planner for Signal-Free Intersections using Diffusion-Model-Based Multi-Agent Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30694)
*Qian Hu, Haoyang Peng, Songan Zhang, Ming Yang, Hongtei Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DSIP 提出了一种基于扩散模型的多智能体运动规划框架，用于无信号灯交叉口的车辆轨迹协调，将交叉口管理从离散相位控制转变为连续多车轨迹优化，在 SUMO 仿真中显著降低了平均延迟并提高了平均速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通信号控制导致频繁启停，增加延迟并降低通行效率，尤其在交通需求高时。随着网联自动驾驶车辆（CAV）的发展，轨迹级协调有望超越传统的相位管理方式，但现有方法在可扩展性和鲁棒性上仍有不足。

Method: 提出 DSIP 框架，利用生成式扩散过程进行多智能体运动规划。将交叉口管理从离散时间相位调度转变为连续多车轨迹优化，在理想通信和执行条件下评估该协调策略的理论上界性能。

Result: 在 SUMO 平台上对多种四向交叉口配置进行实验，DSIP 相比固定时间信号控制和基于强化学习的控制器，在中高密度交通下显著降低了平均延迟并保持了更高的平均速度。

Conclusion: 基于扩散的轨迹规划为未来自主交叉口管理提供了可扩展且鲁棒的基础，通过软件定义的协调释放交叉口潜在通行能力，为提升城市交通效率提供了无需物理基础设施扩建的经济有效途径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于扩散模型的多智能体运动规划框架，用于无信号灯交叉口的车辆轨迹协调。这属于具身智能/机器人领域的规划控制问题，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Applies diffusion models to multi-agent motion planning for traffic intersections, but is outside the user's core priority subtopics. Signals: diffusion model, multi-agent motion planning, autonomous intersection management, trajectory optimization, SUMO simulation

Abstract: Traffic signal control at urban intersections inherently introduces stop-and-go behavior, resulting in increased delays and reduced traffic efficiency, especially under high traffic demand. With the emergence of connected and automated vehicles (CAVs), trajectory-level coordination has emerged as a high-potential strategy to augment or transcend conventional phase-based management. This paper proposes DSIP (Diffusion-model-based Signal-free Intersection Planner), a multi-agent motion planning framework driven by a generative diffusion process. DSIP shifts the intersection management paradigm from discrete temporal phasing to continuous multi-vehicle trajectory optimization. This work evaluates the theoretical upper-bound performance of this coordination strategy under idealized communication and execution conditions to isolate the core benefits of the diffusion-driven approach. Using the SUMO platform, we evaluate DSIP across diverse four-leg intersection configurations. Experimental results demonstrate that DSIP significantly reduces average delay and maintains higher average speed compared to both fixed-time signal control and state-of-the-art reinforcement-learning-based controllers, particularly in medium- to high-density traffic. These findings suggest that diffusion-based trajectory planning provides a scalable and robust foundation for future autonomous intersection management. By unlocking latent intersection capacity through software-defined coordination, this approach offers a cost-effective pathway to improve urban traffic flow efficiency without requiring physical infrastructure expansion.

</details>


### [4] [Vision-Language Procedural Reasoning for Context-Aware Reward Modeling of Robotic Endovascular Guidewire Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30698)
*Wentong Tian, Jiyuan Zhao, Tianliang Yao, Yuxiang Fan, Zhengyu Shi, Dong Liu, Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (69.76) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VL-PR 框架，利用多模态大语言模型进行程序推理，动态调整奖励函数权重，实现机器人血管介入导丝的上下文感知自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人自主导航方法依赖静态奖励函数，缺乏对解剖上下文和任务进展的显式程序推理，难以处理复杂血管解剖结构中的多阶段导航任务。

Method: 将 MLLM 作为程序推理模块，实时解读视觉观测以推断高层导航上下文（如阶段、风险、目标），据此动态调整奖励函数各分量的权重，使单一策略能处理多阶段竞争目标。

Result: 在物理机器人平台上的多种血管场景实验中，该方法相比静态奖励方法提升了任务可靠性和导航效率。

Conclusion: VL-PR 框架通过 MLLM 驱动的上下文感知奖励调整，有效解决了复杂多阶段机器人血管介入导航中的竞争目标问题，为多任务机器人手术提供可扩展方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出视觉语言程序推理框架用于机器人血管介入导丝导航，属于机器人具身智能中的规划控制任务，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotic endovascular navigation, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Robotic endovascular navigation, MLLM for procedural reasoning, context-aware reward adaptation, physical robotic platform experiments

Abstract: Robotic-assisted endovascular interventions demand accurate, stable, and context-aware guidewire navigation in complex and patient-specific vascular anatomies. Despite recent advances in robotic precision and learning-based control, existing autonomous navigation methods remain limited by their reliance on static reward functions and the lack of explicit procedural reasoning regarding anatomical context and task progression. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a vision-language procedural reasoning (VL-PR) framework for autonomous guidewire navigation. The framework integrates a multimodal large language model (MLLM) as a procedural reasoning module that interprets real-time visual observations to infer high-level navigation contexts. Instead of generating low-level control commands, the inferred procedural insights enable context-aware reward adaptation by dynamically adjusting the importance of reward components across different navigation phases. This approach allows a single policy to resolve competing objectives and handle complex transitions while preserving a consistent global task goal. Experiments on a physical robotic platform across diverse vascular scenarios demonstrate enhanced task reliability and streamlined navigational efficiency, highlighting the advantages over static-reward methods and offering a scalable solution for complex and multi-task robotic endovascular procedures.

</details>


### [5] [Off the Rails: Hijacking the Scoring Head in Generative End-to-End Driving Planners with Safety-Violating Adversarial Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30807)
*Halima Bouzidi, Mboutidem Ekemini Mkpong, Haoyu Liu, Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV, cs.CR

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Derail框架，针对生成式端到端自动驾驶规划器中共享的评分头（scoring head）攻击面，通过对抗扰动使轨迹选择从安全候选翻转为不安全候选，在多种生成式规划器上实现39-80%的评分下降和高达50%的碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式端到端自动驾驶规划器（基于扩散或词汇检索）共享一个共同推理模式：用评分头对固定候选轨迹集打分并选择最高分轨迹。评分头是感知与运动指令之间的唯一屏障，且候选轨迹间的决策边界往往很小，这构成了一个被忽视的攻击面。

Method: 提出Derail对抗攻击框架，针对生成式AD规划器的评分头进行攻击。通过设计安全违反目标（safety-violating objectives）的对抗扰动，使原本安全的候选轨迹得分大幅下降，而原本不安全的候选轨迹被选中，从而翻转轨迹选择结果。

Result: 在多种生成式规划器上，Derail实现39-80%的评分下降和高达50%的碰撞率，在攻击效果上持续优于通用的损失最大化攻击和特征散度攻击。

Conclusion: 安全违反目标主导了对生成式AD规划器的攻击有效性，评分头推理模式本身是一个值得明确防御的反复出现的攻击面。

Classification reason: 该论文研究端到端自动驾驶规划器（生成式AD规划器）中的对抗攻击，属于具身智能中的规划控制与安全领域，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial attacks in autonomous driving, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics. Signals: world model, adversarial attack, autonomous driving, scoring head, generative planner

Abstract: Generative models have recently seen rapid adoption in End-to-End (E2E) autonomous driving (AD), with diffusion-based denoising and vocabulary-based retrieval becoming the dominant trajectory-decoding paradigms. Despite their architectural diversity, current generative AD planners share a common inference pattern: a fixed set of candidate trajectories (anchors, vocabulary entries, or proposal queries) is scored by one or more learned heads conditioned on the Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) features, and the highest-scored candidate is returned as the final trajectory. Under this design, the scoring head is the only barrier between perception and the motion command, and its decision margins between competing candidates are often small. We introduce \textsc{Derail}, an adversarial framework that exploits this scoring-head attack surface. Evaluated on various generative planners, \textsc{Derail} flips the trajectory selection from a safe to an unsafe candidate, with score drops of $39$--$80\%$ and collision rates of up to $50\%$, consistently outperforming generic loss-maximization and feature-divergence attacks. Our analysis suggests that safety-violating objectives govern attack effectiveness against generative AD planners, and that the scoring-head inference pattern itself is a recurring attack surface worth explicit defensive consideration.

</details>


### [6] [Motion Planning in Compressed Representation Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30940)
*Lukas Lao Beyer, Sertac Karaman*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (77.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种生成式框架，通过训练高压缩率的离散分层自编码器，在潜空间中进行运动规划搜索，无需任务特定训练即可实现闭环运动规划和多智能体场景合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在运动规划中表现出色，但基于搜索或优化的模型规划算法仍因其灵活性、高效性和可融入领域知识的能力而不可或缺。本文旨在统一这两种范式，在保持灵活性的同时利用生成模型的先验能力。

Method: 学习一个具有高压缩率和分层离散 token 潜空间的自编码器，利用其降维和粗到细的分层结构，直接在潜空间中进行搜索（如 beam search），优化测试时指定的任意目标函数。

Result: 在 nuPlan 和 Waymo Open Motion Dataset 上评估，潜空间搜索可用于多种引导行为生成任务，在闭环运动规划和多智能体引导场景合成中取得强性能。

Conclusion: 潜空间搜索是一种有效的统一框架，结合了生成模型的先验能力和基于搜索的灵活性，无需任务特定训练即可实现高质量的运动规划与场景合成。

Classification reason: 该论文提出在压缩潜空间中进行运动规划（motion planning），属于机器人/具身智能中的规划与控制问题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes latent-space search for motion planning using compressed discrete tokens, aligning with world model and continuous representation interests but not directly on priority subtopics. Signals: latent space search, discrete hierarchical autoencoder, closed-loop motion planning, generative framework, no task-specific training

Abstract: Deep learning methods have vastly expanded the capabilities of motion planning in robotics applications, as learning priors from large-scale data has been shown to be essential in capturing the highly complex behavior required for solving tasks such as manipulation or navigation for autonomous vehicles. At the same time, model-based planning algorithms based on search or optimization remain an essential tool due to their flexibility, efficiency, and the ability to incorporate domain knowledge via expert-designed algorithms and objective functions. We propose a new generative framework to unify these two paradigms. First, we learn an autoencoder with a high compression ratio and a latent space of hierarchically ordered, discrete-valued tokens. Leveraging both the dimensionality reduction and the hierarchical coarse-to-fine structure learned by this autoencoder, we then perform motion planning by directly searching in the latent space of tokens. This search can optimize arbitrary objective functions specified at test time, providing a large degree of flexibility while maintaining efficiency and producing realistic solutions by relying on the generative capabilities of the highly compressed autoencoder. We evaluate our method on nuPlan and the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, showing how latent space search can be used for a variety of guided behavior generation tasks, achieving strong performance for closed-loop motion planning and multi-agent guided scenario synthesis without requiring any task-specific training.

</details>

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Labimus，一个面向化学实验室中类人灵巧操作的仿真与基准平台，涉及具身智能、机器人操作、动作规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics simulation for chemistry, not on the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Humanoid dexterous manipulation, Chemical lab benchmark, Precision-aware evaluation, Real-to-sim modeling, Robotics simulation

Abstract: Laboratory automation has made remarkable progress through robotic platforms and AI-driven scientific reasoning. However, many laboratory operations (e.g., solid--solid transfer) remain inherently dynamic and require real-time adaptation to different materials and experimental conditions. Such precision-critical manipulations are difficult to standardize, motivating the use of humanoid robots with dexterous hands. Despite this opportunity, no existing benchmark evaluates humanoid manipulation in precision-critical laboratory environments. We present Labimus, to our knowledge, the first benchmark for humanoid dexterous manipulation in organic chemistry laboratories. Labimus reconstructs over 30 functionally faithful assets from real organic chemistry workstations through real-to-sim modeling, collectively covering the core operations of routine organic chemistry experiments. The benchmark integrates articulated laboratory instruments, particle-based powder physics, and closed-loop instrument readouts, enabling a complete manipulation-to-measurement pipeline. It further defines six atomic operations and a seven-step solid-weighing workflow derived from real laboratory standard operating procedures. We introduce a precision-aware evaluation protocol designed to jointly measure task completion, experimental precision, and long-horizon execution. We benchmark three representative policies under procedural layouts and environmental perturbations. Results reveal a precision gap: policies that successfully complete laboratory tasks can still fail to satisfy the quantitative tolerances required by experimental protocols. Our benchmark exposes a fundamental disconnect between task completion and experimental validity, providing a new testbed for developing reliable humanoid robots for scientific laboratories.

</details>

Conclusion: 世界-动作模型可以通过合成先验训练并零样本迁移到真实世界，为减少对昂贵真实演示数据的依赖提供了可行路径，但当前成功率仍有较大提升空间。

Classification reason: 该论文研究将世界-动作模型（world-action model）从仿真环境零样本迁移到真实机器人操作任务，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: First sim-to-real transfer of world-action model for robotic manipulation, directly relevant to world model and video diffusion priorities. Signals: world-action model, sim-to-real transfer, zero-shot, video diffusion, Cosmos Policy

Abstract: Bridging the sim-to-real gap is a core challenge in deploying learned manipulation policies. Sim-to-real learning is attractive because it can replace expensive real robot demonstrations with scalable synthetic data, yet world-action models have not previously been shown to transfer from simulation to real robotic manipulation. We study whether a world-action model can be trained from synthetic priors and deployed zero-shot in the real world. To this end, we build upon Cosmos Policy, a video diffusion model adapted for visuomotor control. We construct simulation environments with extensive domain randomization and generate demonstrations using the AnyTask motion planning pipeline. We evaluate our approach across object lifting, drawer opening, and pick-and-place tasks using ${\sim}800$ synthetic demonstrations per task and no real demonstrations. When deployed zero-shot on a Franka Robot, our policy attains a 35\% average success rate. To our knowledge, this represents the first successful sim-to-real transfer of a world-action model for robotic manipulation.

</details>

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过线性探针和因果干预方法，系统分析了自动驾驶策略（行为克隆和强化学习）内部是否真正具备周围车辆运动预测和自车轨迹规划能力。研究发现，尽管策略在闭环模拟中表现良好，但在近碰撞事件中往往缺乏及时的预测信号，且纠正错误预测可以改善自车规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶策略在名义场景中表现良好，但可能依赖脆弱的启发式规则而非真正的预测与规划能力。现有闭环模拟器的汇总分数无法揭示策略内部的推理缺陷，因此需要更细粒度的分析方法来理解策略的真实能力与局限性。

Method: 使用线性探针（linear probing）从策略网络的内部表征中解码周围车辆的未来轨迹预测和自车规划信号；通过目标扰动（targeted perturbations）和因果干预（causal intervention）验证预测信号对规划的影响；在行为克隆（IL）和强化学习（RL）策略上系统分析规模（数据集大小、训练步数）对内部信号涌现的影响。

Result: 1）策略内部确实存在预测和规划信号，但近碰撞事件中预测信号往往缺失或延迟；2）更大数据集和更长训练带来的闭环性能提升并不一定对应更强的内部预测能力，有时只是更好的行为启发式；3）因果干预表明，纠正错误的预测可以显著改善自车规划轨迹的安全性。

Conclusion: 自动驾驶策略的闭环性能可能掩盖其内部预测能力的不足，线性探针是一种有效的策略诊断工具。未来应关注如何增强策略在关键事件中的预测能力，以提升安全性和鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自动驾驶策略中的预测与规划能力，通过探针分析内部表征是否包含周围车辆运动预测和自车轨迹规划信号。这属于具身智能（自动驾驶）中的世界模型与规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Probing internal prediction and planning in driving policies is methodologically sound but only tangentially touches your priority on world models. Signals: world model probing, autonomous driving, linear probe, causal intervention, prediction-planning link

Abstract: Large-scale datasets and fast simulators have enabled improvements in driving policies that appear safe and robust, yet strong performance in nominal scenarios can still mask flawed reasoning and unsafe heuristics. Summary scores from closed-loop simulators do not give significant insight into the policy, making it difficult to determine whether they truly predict the motion of surrounding vehicles, how the ego vehicle generates future plans, or whether they merely rely on brittle heuristics that happen to succeed in nominal scenarios. To better understand the limits and weaknesses of driving policies, we focus on probing for forms of prediction, i.e., where surrounding vehicles will move next, and planning, i.e., understanding how to generate safe trajectories. We focus on these two capabilities because they reflect behaviors expected of effective driving policies, and use their presence or absence to assess policy quality across data-driven behavior cloning and simulation-driven reinforcement learning policies. To evaluate the presence of these capabilities, we investigate them as a function of scale, asking whether the closed-loop gains from larger datasets and longer simulation training reflect stronger prediction and planning or merely better behavioral heuristics. We use linear probing and targeted perturbations in both imitation learning and reinforcement learning models to track when these internal signals emerge, plateau, or fail. Despite good closed-loop performance, policies often fail to form timely surrounding-vehicle predictions during near-collision events, revealing a limitation in the predictive signals available for ego planning. Finally, causal intervention shows that correcting mistaken predictions improves ego planning toward safer trajectories.

</details>

*Andrew Zou Li, Gokul Swamy, Yonatan Bisk, Andrea Bajcsy*

TL;DR: ELASTIC 提出一种元强化学习方法，学习状态依赖的测试时计算分配策略，在扩散策略和 VLA 模型中自适应调整去噪步数（顺序缩放）和候选样本数（并行缩放），在模拟和真实机器人操作任务中以更少计算达到或超越固定分配策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式控制策略（如扩散策略、VLA模型）在机器人控制中支持测试时计算缩放，但顺序缩放（增加去噪步数）和并行缩放（增加候选样本数）的最优分配高度依赖于状态、任务和策略，难以先验确定。现有方法采用固定分配或单一轴缩放，效率低下。

Method: 将计算分配形式化为一个元马尔可夫决策过程（meta-MDP），其中元策略与冻结的预训练机器人策略交互，在每个去噪迭代中选择顺序步数和并行样本数，以最大化任务成功率并最小化计算开销。使用强化学习训练元策略，无需访问生成策略的训练数据。

Result: 在模拟操作基准测试中，ELASTIC 在相同计算预算下帕累托优于固定分配和单轴缩放基线。在真实机器人操作中，使用 π0.5 VLA 模型，ELASTIC 匹配了 best-of-10 的成功率，同时将墙钟延迟降低了 34%。

Conclusion: ELASTIC 证明了学习状态自适应的测试时计算分配可以有效提升生成式控制策略的计算效率与任务性能，为机器人策略的推理时优化提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器人生成式控制策略（扩散策略、VLA模型）的测试时计算分配，属于具身智能中的机器人规划与控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot control compute scaling, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Test-time compute scaling, diffusion policy, VLA model, meta-RL, robot manipulation

Abstract: Generative control policies (GCPs), such as diffusion policies and flow-based vision-language-action models, enable test-time scaling in robot control. Test-time compute can be allocated along two axes: sequential scaling, which increases denoising steps to refine actions, and parallel scaling, which samples multiple candidate actions to search across modes of the policy distribution. However, the optimal allocation of sequential and parallel compute is hard to know a priori as it is state-, task-, and policy-dependent. For example, early stages of a grasp may benefit from broader parallel exploration, while near-contact phases may require more sequential refinement for precision. We present ELASTIC, an algorithm that learns state-dependent test-time compute schedules for GCPs. We formulate compute allocation as a meta-Markov Decision Process in which a meta-policy interacts with a frozen pretrained robot policy and selects sequential steps and parallel samples at each denoising iteration to maximize task success while minimizing compute. Using reinforcement learning, this meta-policy also learns adaptive compute schedules without access to the GCP's training data. Across simulated manipulation benchmarks with diffusion policies, ELASTIC Pareto-dominates fixed and single-axis scaling baselines at matched compute budgets. On real-world robot manipulation with the $\pi_{0.5}$ vision-language-action model, ELASTIC matches best-of-$10$ success while reducing wall-clock latency by 34%.

</details>

Result: 系统在CMU VLA挑战赛中展示了从自然语言指令到机器人动作的完整流程，能够在时间约束下利用部分地图完成导航任务，验证了模块化VLA框架在室内环境中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该模块化VLA框架有效桥接了人类语言与机器人动作之间的鸿沟，通过语义地图与VLM的协同工作，为室内自主导航提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an integrated system for the CMU Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Challenge, designed to enable an autonomous agent to perform complex tasks based on natural language instructions. Our framework employs a modular architecture that orchestrates environment mapping, question processing, and navigation. The system operates in two parallel streams: a perception pipeline that constructs a semantic voxel map from real-time camera feeds using OwlViT embeddings, and a language pipeline that classifies user commands with a Vision-Language Model. The mapping is time-constrained; the system proceeds with a partial map if a 500-second exploration limit is reached. The classified query is then grounded in the geometric and semantic context of the map to generate a detailed prompt for the VLM. This yields an actionable output, demonstrating a capable solution for bridging the gap between human language and robotic action.

</details>

Abstract: The quest for intuitive and natural human-robot interaction (HRI) remains a significant challenge in robotics. Traditional methods often rely on rigid, pre-programmed commands that limit the robot's expressiveness and adaptability. This paper introduces a novel framework that leverages the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to synthesize complex robotic actions from a rich tapestry of multimodal human inputs: natural speech, hand gestures, and music/sound beats. Our system architecture integrates a speech transcription model, a gesture recognition module, and a signal processing pipeline for beat detection. These processed inputs are contextualized using prompt templates and fed into a LLM. The LLM, informed by a predefined robot action space, reasons over the combined inputs to generate a coherent sequence of actions. This sequence is dispatched to an action queue for execution on a quadruped robot over ROS. The framework has ability to interpret and fuse semantic commands from speech, deictic information from gestures, and rhythmic cues from music. This work represents a step towards creating robots that can interact with humans in a more fluid, creative, and context-aware manner.

Abstract: VLA models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for transferring semantic knowledge from web-scale data to physical robotic control. However, current single-frame architectures suffer from intrinsic limitations: temporal myopia that discards historical dynamics, reasoning gaps between high-level instructions and low-level motor commands, and inference inefficiency due to autoregressive scalar decoding. In this work, we propose MIRTH, a unified framework designed to address these challenges. MIRTH augments a pretrained VLA backbone with three key innovations: (1) dual-scale temporal memory hubs that compress long-term scene evolution and short-term motion trends into compact embeddings; (2) latent reasoning tokens optimized via a mutual-information objective carving out a semantic plan space to align multimodal context with action trajectories; and (3) a parallel action decoding scheme that replaces autoregressive generation with vector-wise prediction to maximize control throughput. Extensive evaluations on the LIBERO simulation benchmark and a real-world LeRobot platform demonstrate that MIRTH achieves state-of-the-art performance and exhibiting emergent error recovery capabilities. The codes and collected datasets are released at .

Method: 提出一个BDDL规范作为共享接口，统一数据构建、计划验证和奖励设计。包含三个组件：(1) 视频到BDDL解析器，从开放世界视频证据自动构建BDDL规范；(2) LLM验证器与轻量符号引擎，提供毫秒级密集反馈；(3) GroupAdapt，一种难度感知长度调度策略，利用批次内分组通过率作为零成本信号，对困难提示给予更宽的长度容限，并随通过率提高自动收紧。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1000基准上，8B规划器达到97.3的Strict-Pass分数，相比Qwen3-8B基线提升25.9%，超过最强大型模型基线3.5%，同时将响应长度压缩79%至207个token。

Classification reason: 该论文研究具身任务规划（embodied task planning），将自然语言指令转化为可执行的动作序列，属于机器人/具身智能中的规划与控制问题，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong embodied planning method but tangential to user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: Embodied task planning, Symbolic RL with BDDL, BEHAVIOR-1000 benchmark, GroupAdapt length scheduling, Efficient 8B planner

Abstract: Embodied task planning asks an agent to turn a natural-language instruction into an executable sequence of actions in a physical scene, and is a building block for household, assistive, and service robots. Recent prompting-based and reinforcement-learning planners generate fluent action text but lack a cheap deterministic check that the produced plan is valid in the target world, while high-fidelity simulation is too slow to serve as an inner-loop training signal. The general problem is therefore how to obtain verifiable supervision and rewards for embodied planners without relying on string-level matching or full simulation. Here we show that a single BDDL specification, automatically constructed from open-world video evidence or curated tasks, can serve as a shared interface for data construction, plan verification, and reward design. A video-to-BDDL parser, an LLM verifier, and a lightweight symbolic engine together supply dense feedback at millisecond latency. We further introduce GroupAdapt, a difficulty-aware length schedule that uses the in-batch group pass rate as a zero-cost signal so that hard prompts get wider length tolerance and automatically tighten as their pass rate improves. Under the guidance of the proposed verifier and GroupAdapt schedule, the 8B planner attains a Strict-Pass score of 97.3 on BEHAVIOR-1000, yielding a 25.9 percent relative improvement over the Qwen3-8B baseline. This result exceeds the strongest large-model baseline by 3.5 percent, while simultaneously compressing the response length by 79 percent to 207 tokens, demonstrating both effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>

TL;DR: 3D HAMSTER 提出一种层次化视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，通过让VLM直接预测3D末端执行器轨迹来桥接规划与控制，解决了2D轨迹引导在3D点云策略中因缺乏深度信息导致的几何失真问题，在仿真和真实机器人操作任务中优于2D引导基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层次化VLA模型使用VLM预测2D末端执行器轨迹作为下游策略的显式引导，但下游策略在3D点云空间操作，2D轨迹缺乏深度信息，导致每个路径点被赋予其下方场景表面的深度，产生几何失真的轨迹。作者希望让规划器直接输出度量可靠的3D轨迹，弥合2D规划与3D控制之间的差距。

Method: 3D HAMSTER 在VLM中增加专用深度编码器和密集深度重建目标，使VLM能够预测3D路径点序列。这些3D轨迹直接集成到基于点云的低层策略中。框架包含：(1) 深度增强的VLM规划器，输出3D末端执行器轨迹；(2) 基于点云的低层策略，接收3D轨迹作为引导；(3) 密集深度重建损失作为辅助训练目标。

Result: 在3D轨迹预测、仿真和真实世界操作任务中，3D HAMSTER 一致优于闭源VLM和2D引导基线。在表观变化、未见语言指令、空间和视觉条件等泛化场景下，性能提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 3D HAMSTER 通过让VLM规划器直接输出3D轨迹，有效弥合了层次化VLA框架中高层规划与低层控制之间的几何鸿沟，在多种泛化场景下展现出更强的鲁棒性和操作性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出层次化VLA框架，通过3D轨迹引导将高层规划与低层控制结合，用于机器人操作任务，属于具身智能中的规划与控制，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]（CANONICAL）。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous multimodal priorities via 3D trajectory planning for embodied VLA, but lacks direct continuous token or video generation focus. Signals: world model, hierarchical VLA, 3D trajectory guidance, robot manipulation, depth-aware planning

Abstract: Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models decouple high-level planning from low-level control to improve generalization in robot manipulation. Recent work in this paradigm uses 2D end-effector trajectories predicted by a Vision-Language Model (VLM) as explicit guidance for a downstream policy. However, state-of-the-art low-level policies operate in 3D metric space on point clouds, and feeding them 2D guidance that lacks depth forces each waypoint to be assigned the depth of whatever scene surface lies beneath it, producing geometrically distorted trajectories. We propose 3D HAMSTER, a hierarchical framework that closes this gap by having the planner directly output metrically reliable 3D trajectories. We augment a VLM with a dedicated depth encoder and a dense depth reconstruction objective to predict 3D waypoint sequences, which are directly integrated into a pointcloudbased low-level policy. Across 3D trajectory prediction, simulation, and real-world manipulation, 3D HAMSTER consistently outperforms proprietary VLMs and 2D-guided baselines, with the largest gains under appearance-altering shifts and unseen language, spatial, and visual conditions. The project page is available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程机器人操作任务中，强化学习面临奖励稀疏和延迟的问题，而人工设计密集奖励信号成本高且对环境变化脆弱。因此需要一种能从无结构专家视频中自动学习密集奖励的方法。

Method: STDR框架包含：(1) 阶段结构推断——利用语义理解从演示中自动推断任务阶段结构；(2) 阶段转换奖励——提供目标导向的奖励信号；(3) 阶段内进度奖励——提供细粒度的阶段完成进度指导；(4) OOD检测机制和抓取调节模块——增强鲁棒性并防止奖励黑客行为。

Result: 在MetaWorld、ManiSkill和Franka Kitchen的14个操作任务上，STDR一致提升了样本效率和成功率，在多个挑战性任务上匹配或超越了手工设计的密集奖励。真实机器人实验表明STDR能分配稳定、与进度对齐的奖励。

Conclusion: STDR是一种有效的视觉奖励学习框架，能够从专家演示中自动生成逻辑上合理的密集奖励信号，显著提升长程机器人操作任务的RL训练效果，且对视觉噪声具有鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出STDR框架，利用专家视频为机器人操作任务学习密集奖励信号，用于强化学习训练。这属于具身智能中机器人操作、奖励设计与规划控制的范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics RL reward design but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: world model, dense reward, robotic manipulation, reinforcement learning, stage-transition

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for long-horizon robotic manipulation is often limited by sparse and delayed rewards, while manually designing dense shaping signals is costly and brittle to changes in environments and object configurations. This work proposes Stage-Transition Dense Reward (STDR), a visual reward-learning framework that converts unstructured expert videos into logically grounded dense rewards for training RL agents from scratch. STDR leverages semantic understanding to infer a task's stage structure from demonstrations, and delivers two complementary learning signals during online training: (i) stage-transition feedback that provides goal-directed reward, and (ii) within-stage progress feedback that supplies fine-grained guidance toward completing each stage. Furthermore, an out-of-distribution (OOD) detection mechanism and a grasping regulation module are integrated to enhance robustness and prevent reward hacking. Experiments on 14 manipulation tasks across MetaWorld, ManiSkill, and Franka Kitchen show that STDR consistently improves sample efficiency and success rates over multiple baselines, and matches or surpasses handcrafted dense rewards on several challenging tasks. Real-robot evaluations further indicate that STDR assigns stable, progress-aligned rewards on successful executions while producing appropriately low rewards for failures, suggesting robustness to visual noise and better-calibrated reward assignment across settings.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽然性能强大，但参数量巨大且对剪枝极度敏感。现有范式认为剪枝后的性能下降不可避免，需要通过微调或低秩修正来恢复。本文质疑这一假设，试图探究被剪除的参数是否真正冗余，并揭示VLM到VLA适配过程中参数演化的机制。

Method: 首先量化VLM到VLA适配过程中参数差异的空间分布，揭示不同模块间的结构化模式。然后引入受控剪枝作为诊断探针，通过比较移除不同参数子集对VLA性能的直接（无微调）影响，建立适配诱导的差异信号与功能贡献之间的因果联系。基于发现的模块异质性，设计多模块联合剪枝方案。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，该方法将OpenVLA和π0.5的参数减少12%-30%，在不进行任何剪枝后恢复的情况下保持约90%的原始性能。相比之下，现有参数剪枝标准在相同的无恢复约束下会导致性能完全崩溃。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了VLA适配中的参数演化机制，为在资源受限环境中部署高效、鲁棒的机器人策略提供了新路径。研究表明，通过理解适配过程中的参数差异模式，可以在不依赖后训练恢复的情况下实现有效的模型压缩。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM到VLA（视觉-语言-动作模型）的适配与参数剪枝，面向具身智能和机器人策略部署，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向。论文在LIBERO具身基准上评估，关注机器人策略模型的高效部署，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides a principled pruning method for VLA models, relevant to efficient inference and world models, but focuses on robotics rather than core multimodal generation. Signals: VLA model pruning, parameter redundancy analysis, efficient inference, world model application, no fine-tuning recovery

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have made significant strides in embodied intelligence by integrating the powerful representations of pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs). However, the massive parameter scale of VLAs imposes a heavy computational burden, and these models exhibit extreme sensitivity to parameter pruning. Current paradigms often treat the resulting performance degradation as inevitable, relying on fine-tuning or low-rank corrections to recover efficacy. We challenge this convention by questioning whether the removed parameters are truly redundant if VLA pruning necessitates performance recovery to be effective, or if this paradigm masks the indiscriminate pruning of critical parameters. We revisit parameter redundancy through the lens of VLM-to-VLA adaptation, first quantifying the spatial distribution of parameter divergence during adaptation to reveal structured patterns across different modules. Subsequently, we introduce controlled pruning as a diagnostic probe: by comparing the direct impact of removing different parameter subsets on VLA performance without any fine-tuning, we establish a causal link between adaptation-induced divergence signals and functional contributions. Based on the discovered modular heterogeneities, we design a multi-module joint pruning scheme. Evaluations on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that our approach reduces the parameters of OpenVLA and $\pi_{0.5}$ by 12\%--30\% while maintaining approximately 90\% of the original performance without any post-pruning recovery. In contrast, existing parameter pruning criteria result in total performance collapse when evaluated under the same recovery-free constraints. Our study reveals the parameter evolution mechanism in VLA adaptation and provides a new path for deploying efficient, robust robotic policies in resource-constrained environments.

</details>

Abstract: Visual signals play a crucial role in policy learning by enabling models to capture object motion and interaction dynamics. Just as humans reason about actions using both past experience and anticipated outcomes, effective policies should integrate past interactions with future predictions. However, existing visuomotor policies typically model either historical context or future dynamics in isolation, lacking a unified temporal representation of interaction dynamics. In this work, we introduce \textbf{ChronoFlow}, a temporally unified representation that captures \textbf{past, current, and future} interaction dynamics through sparse 3D keypoints of both objects and the gripper. Based on this representation, we propose \textbf{ChronoFlow-Policy}, a diffusion-based visuomotor policy that jointly learns ChronoFlow and action sequences through a co-training objective. Experiments on 14 simulated tasks and 5 real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that ChronoFlow-Policy consistently outperforms strong diffusion-policy baselines and improves robustness in long-horizon and non-Markovian manipulation scenarios.

</details>

*Wen Jiang, Hanfang Liang, Li Wang, Kangyao Huang, Wang Xu, Wei Fan, Jinyuan Liu, Shaoyu Liu, Hongwei Duan, Bin Xu, Xiangyang Ji, Huaping Liu*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在 OpenUAV UAV-VLN 基准的 Test Unseen Full 分割上，DynFly 相比最强基线提升了 4.69 NDTW、2.40 SDTW、2.14 SR 和 4.87 OSR，同时 NE 降低了 4.51 米。

Conclusion: DynFly 有效弥合了高层导航推理与可执行无人机运动之间的鸿沟，生成的连续轨迹在导航性能和轨迹质量上均优于现有方法，且可无缝集成到现有 UAV-VLN 框架中。

Classification reason: 该论文研究无人机视觉语言导航（UAV-VLN）中的连续轨迹生成，将高层导航意图映射为可执行的无人机运动轨迹，属于具身智能中的规划控制问题，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）这一规范子主题。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large models have significantly improved UAV vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) by enhancing high-level perception and reasoning. However, existing methods mainly focus on predicting discrete actions, local targets, or sparse waypoints, while the continuous transition from navigation intent to executable UAV motion remains weakly modeled. This motion-interface gap limits the continuity, stability, and executability of generated UAV trajectories. To address this gap, we propose DynFly, a dynamic-aware continuous trajectory generation framework that bridges high-level navigation reasoning and executable UAV motion. DynFly bridges high-level navigation intent and continuous UAV motion through a lightweight trajectory generation layer. Specifically, it represents expert trajectories in B-spline control-point space and employs a Spline-DiT generator to learn conditional trajectory generation via flow matching. Furthermore, we introduce UAV-oriented dynamic-aware supervision over position, finite-difference velocity, finite-difference acceleration, heading consistency, and local target alignment, enabling the generated trajectories to better satisfy UAV motion characteristics. And our trajectory generation framework can also be integrated with an existing UAV-VLN framework while preserving its original visual-language reasoning pipeline. Extensive experiments on the OpenUAV UAV-VLN benchmark show that DynFly improves both navigation performance and trajectory quality. On the Test Unseen Full split, DynFly improves the strongest baseline by 4.69 NDTW, 2.40 SDTW, 2.14 SR points and 4.87 OSR points, while reducing NE by 4.51 m.

</details>

TL;DR: HABIT 是一个面向人机共存环境的大规模机器人操作演示数据集，包含 Collaborator、Coworker、Supervisor 三种交互角色，共 60 个任务、超 10K 条轨迹和 160 小时数据。实验表明，基于人机共存数据训练的策略能产生人类感知行为（时空同步、避让、手势理解），而纯机器人数据无法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模机器人演示数据集均在无人环境中采集，导致训练出的策略虽然能独立完成任务，但缺乏人类感知行为（如与人协作时的时空同步、共享空间中的避让、理解人类手势指令等），无法适应人机共存的实际场景。

Method: 构建 HABIT 数据集，设计三种人机交互角色（Collaborator 协作、Coworker 共事、Supervisor 监督），涵盖 60 个任务、超 10K 条演示轨迹。基于该数据集训练机器人策略，并与纯机器人数据训练的策略进行对比实验。

Result: 在 Collaborator 任务中实现了时空同步协作，在 Coworker 任务中学会了避让行为，在 Supervisor 任务中实现了手势指令理解。此外，基于 HABIT 训练的策略能快速适应新的人机交互任务。

Conclusion: 将人类存在作为数据集多样性的新维度，能够有效扩展机器人策略到人机共存环境，弥补纯机器人数据在人类感知行为方面的不足。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了面向人机交互场景的大规模机器人操作数据集，属于具身智能中机器人策略学习与行为建模的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on human-aware robot manipulation, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Human-robot interaction dataset, robot manipulation, human-aware behavior, world model application, not aligned with core priority subtopics

Abstract: Large-scale demonstration datasets have been central to recent progress in general-purpose robot policies. However, existing datasets are collected in human-absent settings, and policies trained on such data may perform tasks competently in isolation but fail to exhibit human-aware behaviors. To address this gap, we introduce HABIT, a large-scale robot demonstration dataset for human-present environments. We organize tasks into three roles capturing distinct modes of human-robot interaction: Collaborator, where human and robot jointly accomplish a task; Coworker, where they pursue separate tasks in a shared space; and Supervisor, where the human directs the robot. The dataset comprises over 10K episodes and over 160 hours across 60 tasks. Our experiments show that training on human-present data elicits human-aware behaviors that robot-only data fails to produce: spatiotemporal synchronization in Collaborator tasks, yielding in Coworker tasks, and gesture grounding in Supervisor tasks. Moreover, training on HABIT enables rapid adaptation to new human-robot interaction tasks. By introducing human presence as a new axis of dataset diversity, HABIT extends robot policies to environments shared with humans.

</details>

Conclusion: RoboTacDex 提供了大规模、多模态、多样化的灵巧操作数据，有助于推动人形机器人灵巧操作和模仿学习研究，数据集将开源。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了人形机器人灵巧操作的大规模多模态数据集（含视觉、触觉、动作轨迹），属于具身智能/机器人学习领域，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。虽然论文本身是数据集而非模型，但其为具身世界模型和模仿学习提供了基础数据支撑，属于该子主题范畴。

Importance reason: Dataset focuses on robotics manipulation, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: dexterous manipulation dataset, humanoid robot, multi-modal (visual-tactile), imitation learning evaluation, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: In the field of robot learning, large-scale and diverse demonstration trajectories provide the fundamental basis for enhancing robotic manipulation ability. We introduce RoboTacDex, a large, multi-modal, and diverse dataset of dexterous manipulation behaviors performed with a humanoid robot. Built on the publicly accessible humanoid robot Unitree G1, RoboTacDex consists of 6k trajectories covering 19 tasks, 23 skills, and interactions with 22 objects. RoboTacDex provides comprehensive records including multi-view RGB and depth information, tactile feedback, and detailed semantic annotations. Furthermore, the dataset features a variety of relatively challenging tasks that can only be completed by dual arms and dexterous hands, aiming to mimic human-like operational logic and simulate real-world manipulation complexity. To ensure data collection quality, we develop an improved multi-camera synchronization system to enable millisecond data synchronization and recording of modalities. In our experiments, we evaluate three representative imitation learning models on our dataset, analyzing their performance as well as their respective strengths and limitations across different task categories. Successful trial results and a moderate level of generalization capabilities across a suite of tasks indicate the effectiveness and diversity of the collected dataset. Our dataset will be open-sourced soon.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略大多依赖行为克隆或监督微调（SFT），无法从自身失败中学习改进。作者希望通过强化学习后训练来提升流式 VLA 模型的性能，且仅使用公开数据，不依赖私有演示数据。

Method: 基于 π₀.₅ 流式 VLA 模型，采用任务级 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）策略。结合共享前缀 rollout 构建、树状轨迹分支、完成感知奖励校准，以及 VLM 与动作专家的选择性联合训练，提升在线优化的效率和稳定性。

Result: 在全部 24 个 RoboCasa 任务上，平均成功率达 80.6%，相比 SFT 初始化提升 13.2 个百分点，超越已发表的最优模型。

Conclusion: 系统性的 GRPO 后训练可以显著提升流式 VLA 策略的性能，且无需额外的私有演示数据，为机器人操作策略的强化学习优化提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究面向机器人操作的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过强化学习（GRPO）后训练提升流式VLA策略的性能，属于具身智能中的机器人策略学习与规划控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong RL post-training for VLA but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: GRPO for VLA, flow-based policy, RoboCasa benchmark, RL post-training, robotic manipulation

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising framework for robotic manipulation by connecting language instructions, visual observations, and continuous control. However, most existing policies remain limited by behavior cloning or supervised fine-tuning (SFT) from fixed demonstrations, which provides limited opportunity to improve from the policy's own failures. In this paper, we present Z-1, a reinforcement learning (RL) post-training framework for flow-based VLA models. Built on top of $\pi_{0.5}$, Z-1 uses only publicly released RoboCasa demonstrations for SFT and then applies a task-wise Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) strategy across $24$ standard RoboCasa tasks. To improve the efficiency and stability of online optimization, Z-1 combines shared-prefix rollout construction, tree-structured trajectory branching, completion-aware reward calibration, and selective joint training of VLM and Action Expert. Across all $24$ RoboCasa tasks, Z-1 achieves an average success rate of $80.6\%$, improving over its SFT initialization by $13.2\%$ points and outperforms the published sota models. These results show that systematic GRPO post-training can substantially improve flow-based VLA policies without additional private demonstrations.

</details>

*Felipe Tommaselli, Francisco Affonso, Arthur Pompeu, Gianluca Capezzuto, Arun Narenthiran Sivakumar, Girish Chowdhary, Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

TL;DR: LeCropFollow 提出一种潜空间规划框架，通过自监督语义热图与 MBRL 规划器 TD-MPC2 结合，在连续潜流形上优化轨迹，实现农业机器人在非结构化作物行间的零样本迁移导航，在种植间隙场景中语义失败率降低 2.4 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于几何的方法在非结构化农业场景（如不规则种植、作物行间断）中常失败，因为它们将高维视觉数据压缩为确定性空间参考，丢弃了导航歧义地形所需的不确定性和语义上下文。作者希望绕过显式几何建模，利用学习到的潜表征来保留语义信息，提升导航鲁棒性。

Method: 将自监督语义热图提取器与 TD-MPC2（基于模型的强化学习规划器）集成，直接在潜流形上优化轨迹。框架在未压缩的热图信号上运行，保留了几何方法丢弃的语义上下文，支持从简化仿真到真实世界的零样本迁移。

Result: 在晚期玉米田的实地实验中，LeCropFollow 在非结构化行间与基线方法持平，但在种植间隙场景中显著优于基线，相比基于关键点的方法实现了 2.4 倍的语义失败率降低。

Conclusion: 潜空间规划为异构农业环境中的导航提供了一种稳健的替代方案，可替代传统的几何估计方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LeCropFollow，将自监督语义热图提取器与 TD-MPC2（基于模型强化学习规划器）结合，在潜空间中优化农业机器人导航轨迹，属于面向机器人具身智能的规划与控制方法，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Strong robotics planning work but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics on continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: latent space planning, MBRL planner, zero-shot transfer, agricultural navigation, semantic heatmap

Abstract: Unstructured navigational features, such as irregular planting or discontinuities, remain the primary failure mode for under-canopy agricultural robots. Existing geometric approaches often fail in these scenarios because they compress high-dimensional visual data into deterministic spatial references, effectively discarding the uncertainty and semantic context required to navigate ambiguous terrain. To address this, we present LeCropFollow, a visual navigation framework that bypasses explicit geometric modeling in favor of a learned latent representation. By integrating a self-supervised semantic heatmap extractor with TD-MPC2, a Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) planner, our system optimizes trajectories directly within a latent manifold. The framework operates over the uncompressed heatmap signal, preserving the semantic context that geometric reductions discard. We demonstrate that this representational shift enables zero-shot transfer from simplified simulation to the physical world without fine-tuning. Extensive field experiments in late-stage corn fields show that LeCropFollow matches state-of-the-art baselines in unstructured rows but significantly outperforms them in plantation gaps, achieving a 2.4x reduction in semantic failures compared to keypoint-based methods. These results suggest that latent planning offers a robust alternative to geometric estimation for operations in heterogeneous agricultural environments. Code, models, and data available: .

</details>


### [24] [OopsieVerse: A Safety Benchmark with Damage-Aware Simulation for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31993)
*Arnav Balaji, Arpit Bahety, Sriniket Ambatipudi, Daniel Lam, Junhong Xu, Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: OOPSIEVERSE 是一个面向机器人操作安全的统一仿真框架与基准，通过 DAMAGESIM 模块将接触力、温度、液体等物理信号转化为可量化的损伤信号，支持在 OmniGibson 和 RoboCasa 两种仿真器中进行损伤感知的演示收集、策略学习、安全评测和 sim-to-real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作能力虽快速进步，但物理安全性仍是部署家用机器人的主要障碍——任务成功不足以确保安全。现有仿真器缺乏通用的损伤检测、量化和表征机制，无法系统性地评估和训练安全策略。

Method: 提出 OOPSIEVERSE 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) DAMAGESIM，一个仿真器无关的损伤检测与量化框架，将接触力、温度变化、液体交互等物理信号转化为机械损伤、热损伤和流体损伤；(2) 一套家用操作任务套件，用于评估常见损伤模式并区分任务完成与安全执行。DAMAGESIM 在 OmniGibson (Nvidia Omniverse) 和 RoboCasa (MuJoCo) 两个不同物理引擎的仿真器中实例化。

Result: 展示了 OOPSIEVERSE 在多个用例中的有效性：(1) 通过实时损伤反馈引导更安全的演示收集；(2) 通过损伤条件模仿学习和强化学习学习更安全的操作策略；(3) 对最先进的视觉语言动作(VLA)策略进行安全基准测试；(4) 提升 sim-to-real 迁移策略的真实世界安全性。

Conclusion: OOPSIEVERSE 为安全机器人操作研究提供了一个开源、系统化、可扩展的基础框架，能够将损伤作为显式的物理信号纳入策略学习和评估流程，有望推动家用机器人安全部署。

Classification reason: 该论文关注机器人操作中的物理安全仿真，提出了一个损伤感知仿真框架 DAMAGESIM 和基准 OOPSIEVERSE，用于安全策略学习、模仿学习、强化学习以及 VLA 策略的安全评测。这属于具身智能/机器人领域的世界模型与安全规划控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot safety simulation, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: robot safety benchmark, damage-aware simulation, world model for manipulation, sim-to-real transfer, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: While robotic manipulation capabilities have advanced rapidly, physical safety remains a major barrier to deploying household robots: task success is insufficient if the robot damages itself or its surroundings. Simulation offers a harm-free alternative to costly and dangerous real-world training and evaluation, yet existing simulators lack general mechanisms to detect, quantify, and represent damage. To address this gap, we introduce OOPSIEVERSE, a unified simulation framework and benchmark for damage-aware household manipulation. OOPSIEVERSE provides damage as an explicit, physically-grounded, and taskagnostic signal by converting sources such as contact forces, temperature changes, and liquid interactions into corresponding mechanical, thermal or fluid damage. OOPSIEVERSE comprises two core elements: (1) DAMAGESIM, a simulator-agnostic framework for detecting and quantifying damage during navigation and manipulation, and (2) a suite of household tasks designed to evaluate common damage modes and distinguish between task completion and safe execution. We demonstrate the generality of our framework by instantiating DAMAGESIM in two simulators with different physics backends, OmniGibson (Nvidia Omniverse) and RoboCasa (MuJoCo). We further showcase the utility of OOPSIEVERSE across multiple use cases, including (1) guiding safer demonstration collection via real-time damage feedback, (2) learning safer manipulation policies through damage-conditioned imitation learning and reinforcement learning, (3) benchmarking the safety of state-of-the-art Vision Language Action policies, and (4) improving real-world safety of sim-to-real transferred policies. Together, our results highlight the potential of OOPSIEVERSE as an open-source foundation for systematic, scalable research on safe robot manipulation. For code and more information, please refer to

</details>


### [25] [Freeform Preference Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32027)

Abstract: Reward design remains a central bottleneck for autonomous robot policy improvement, especially in long-horizon manipulation tasks where sparse success labels provide too little signal and binary preferences collapse many competing notions of quality into one ambiguous signal. We introduce Freeform Preference Learning (FPL), a method for learning robot policies from freeform human preferences. Rather than asking annotators which of two trajectories is better overall, FPL lets them define natural-language preference axes, such as speed, safety, quality of placement, or carefulness, and provide pairwise preferences along each axis. These annotations are used to learn a language-conditioned reward model that maps a trajectory and preference label to an axis-specific reward. We use this model to train a reward-conditioned policy that optimizes across the multiple human-specified dimensions. Across four real-world and two simulated long-horizon manipulation tasks, FPL improves over sparse-reward and binary-preference methods by 38 percentage points. Beyond improved performance, FPL learns dense progress signals without explicit subtask segmentation, shows compositionality of behavior not present in the data, and allows users to steer the policy towards different behaviors at test time without retraining. Abstract: Existing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) systems distribute representation capacity uniformly across a scene, ignoring the fact that many downstream robotic tasks engage only a fraction of the reconstructed geometry. This causes valuable onboard compute to be allocated towards optimizing irrelevant parts of the scene, either limiting online capacity or under-optimizing the most relevant parts of the scene. We introduce GaussLite, a task-driven 3DGS mapping system that conditions its representation density on a natural-language task specification. Given a posed RGB-D stream and a task such as "prepare to pick up the object on the desk," GaussLite uses a one-shot LLM parser to extract target and anchor objects, which are grounded per-frame by an open-vocabulary detector and segmented to produce per-pixel relevance masks in real time. The mapper allocates seeding density, gradient flow and scaling by task relevance. At matched Gaussian budget and real-time mapping at 4 Hz on resource-constrained hardware, GaussLite outperforms baselines on ROI PSNR on the Replica Dataset by an average +2.72 dB and on a real-hardware demonstration in indoor and outdoor settings by +2.23 dB. We further show that two task-specialized agents' maps can be fused into a single shared map via per-voxel voting on active-optimization counts in real time, outperforming concatenation by +3.42 dB while only sharing an average 7.08% of the map.

</details>

Classification reason: 该论文研究具身智能体在未知环境中的语义导航，提出层次化3D场景图（HSG）作为全局状态抽象，并基于HSG模拟器进行有限视野推演以实现全局规划决策。这属于面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的世界模型，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong in embodied world models but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Hierarchical 3D scene graph, zero-shot semantic navigation, belief-based planning, long-distance navigation improvement, embodied world model

Abstract: Semantic navigation is a fundamental task for embodied agents operating in unseen environments, requiring both semantic understanding and long-term decision-making. Recent foundation models have empowered agents with rich semantic priors for this task. However, without structured global representations, decision-making often falls back on local observations and greedy strategies, resulting in inefficient exploration and myopic behaviors, especially in long-distance navigation. To address these challenges, we propose a zero-shot semantic navigation framework. Our method incrementally maintains an online Hierarchical 3D Scene Graph (HSG) to form a multi-granular semantic topology over objects, zones, and regions, serving as a compact state abstraction for global planning. Building on this memory, we introduce a hierarchical belief-based planning framework that fuses semantic priors with exploration evidence on the HSG, and performs finite-horizon rollouts on an HSG-based simulator to explicitly estimate the long-term expected returns of candidate macro-actions. This enables globally consistent decisions and reduces redundant backtracking. Extensive experiments in high-fidelity simulation environments across multiple tasks and datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, particularly in long-distance scenarios, where our approach improves SR and SPL by an average of 9.4\% and 5.0\%, respectively.

</details>

Signals: Multi-UAV simulation, LLM agent benchmark, task planning, not continuous language/video, no generative world model

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide a promising interface for high-level robotic task planning, but their use in multi-UAV collaboration remains difficult to evaluate systematically. Existing UAV simulators mainly emphasize dynamics, perception, or low-level control, while existing LLM-agent benchmarks rarely capture aerial-robotics constraints such as partial observability, spatial coverage, UAV assignment, and multi-vehicle coordination. To bridge this gap, we present MultiUAV-Plat, a lightweight, easy-to-use, LLM-agent-oriented simulation platform for multi-UAV collaborative task planning. The platform exposes concise RESTful APIs, agent-facing observations, role-based information access, hidden validation logic, and optional 2D/3D visualization, allowing agents to solve missions through realistic tool interaction rather than privileged simulator access. Built on this platform, the MultiUAV-Plat Benchmark contains 75 mission sessions, 1500 natural-language tasks, and 9396 validation checks across target assignment, area search, and area assignment and patrol scenarios. We further propose Agent4Drone, a task-specific LLM agent framework that structures multi-UAV behavior into memory, observation, task understanding, planning, execution, and verification. In a full paired benchmark comparison, Agent4Drone achieves a 57.9% task pass rate, a 74.6% average task check pass rate, and a 72.0% global check pass rate, substantially outperforming a ReAct baseline at 30.6%, 47.9%, and 43.1%, respectively. Agent4Drone also reduces the total failed task rate from 32.4% to 12.9%. These results demonstrate that MultiUAV-Plat and MultiUAV-Plat Benchmark provide a reproducible foundation for studying LLM-driven multi-UAV autonomy under realistic information and execution constraints.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期交互式交通仿真面临两大挑战：一是多智能体持续进出场景导致动态 token 数量变化，二是长时程 rollout 评估中智能体对应关系随时间衰减。现有方法难以同时保证短期精度和长期稳定性。

Method: RosettaSim 将场景拓扑、智能体状态和生成意图投影为可变长度的结构化自回归流，利用小规模冻结 LLM 的注意力机制可迁移性和运动 token 与自然语言的分布一致性进行交通建模。同时提出 RTE（检索式交通评估），通过检索语义相似的真实场景作为上下文参考锚点来评估长时程仿真质量。

Result: 在 Waymo Open Sim Agent Challenge 上，RosettaSim 在短期和长期仿真中均达到 SOTA 性能。RTE 与标准指标的相关性（r=0.83）优于现有方法（r=0.74），表明其更好地对齐了长时程仿真保真度。

Conclusion: 本文证明了小规模冻结 LLM 在交通仿真中的有效性，结构化自回归建模能够同时实现短期精度和长期稳定性，RTE 为长时程仿真评估提供了更可靠的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究面向自动驾驶的交互式交通仿真，属于世界模型在具身智能（自动驾驶）中的应用。RosettaSim 将场景拓扑、智能体状态和生成意图建模为结构化自回归流，实现长时程多智能体仿真，这与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong world model contribution for autonomous driving but outside user's priority subtopics and uses negative keyword traffic. Signals: world model, autonomous driving, long-term simulation, structured autoregressive modeling, LLM for traffic

Abstract: Interactive traffic simulation is a vital world model for autonomous driving. A central challenge in long-horizon simulation is modeling sustained multi-agent interactions, which is further exacerbated by dynamic token cardinality as agents continuously enter and exit the scene. In this work, we propose that the solution lies in the synergy between the architectural inductive biases and statistical priors of large-scale sequence models, e.g., Large Language Models (LLMs). Our probing experiments reveal that the transferability of attention mechanisms and the distributional consistency between motion tokens and natural language enable small-scale, heavily frozen LLMs to rapidly adapt to traffic modeling. Building on this insight, we introduce RosettaSim, a unified framework that projects scene topology, agent states, and spawning intents into a structured autoregressive stream with variable length, achieving both strong short-term accuracy and stable long-horizon simulation fidelity. Furthermore, evaluating extended rollouts presents yet another hurdle, as one-to-one agent correspondence inevitably fades over time. To address this, we introduce Retrieval-based Traffic Evaluation (RTE), which retrieves semantically similar real-world scenarios as context-aware reference anchors. Experiments on the Waymo Open Sim Agent Challenge (WOSAC) demonstrate that RosettaSim achieves state-of-the-art performance in both short- and long-term simulation. Furthermore, RTE exhibits a stronger correlation with standard metrics ($r=0.83$) than existing approaches ($r=0.74$), indicating improved alignment with long-horizon simulation fidelity.

</details>

Abstract: Autonomous aerial inspection of marine infrastructure is frequently compromised by stochastic sea states, introducing risks of high-kinetic impacts, post-landing toppling, and sensory occlusion. This paper proposes a decoupled, multi-vehicle landing framework synchronizing an Unmanned Surface Vehicle (USV) equipped with a 3-RPU stabilized platform with a robust Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV). The architecture utilizes two independent Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) agents: a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agent providing high-frequency wave-motion compensation for the landing deck, and a multimodal RL agent for the UAVs final approach. Evaluated in high-fidelity maritime simulations, the system achieved a 100% landing success rate across 15 trials in wave states varying from calm to rough. Results show a mean stabilization efficacy of 87.8%, maintaining the landing surface within 1 degree of the horizontal plane for 96% of the mission duration in rough conditions, effectively contributing to safer landings.

*Xinyuan Song, Zekun Cai*

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: 本文发现长时域语言智能体的隐式世界模型存在类似相变的崩溃现象：在临界边界附近，状态负载或推理步长的微小变化会导致世界模型突然崩溃，表现为世界状态保真度先于动作有效性失效。通过大规模网格搜索，作者绘制了包含"已解决平台区-窄过渡带-崩溃底限"的相图，并证明更强的模型只能平移临界边界而无法消除这一质变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域语言智能体在复杂任务中需要维护隐式世界模型，但其崩溃机制尚不明确。作者希望探究世界模型崩溃是否像水的沸腾一样存在相变临界点，从而量化这一瓶颈。

Method: 在确定性任务族中，通过精确的每步金标准状态进行大规模网格搜索，变化状态基数、依赖密度、时域长度、分支因子、观测模式和变异率等参数，绘制相图并分析每步迹线。

Result: 发现了清晰的相图结构：已解决平台区、窄过渡带和崩溃底限。每步迹线显示世界状态保真度先于动作有效性失效，说明智能体并非选择了错误动作，而是基于被破坏的世界状态做出决策。更强的模型只能平移临界边界，无法消除质变。

Conclusion: 世界模型崩溃是长时域智能体的可量化瓶颈，具有相变特性，无法通过单纯增强模型能力消除，需要新的架构或训练策略来应对。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长时域语言智能体（language agents）的隐式世界模型崩溃现象，属于世界模型在规划与控制中的瓶颈分析。虽然不直接涉及机器人具身，但"具身世界模型与规划控制"（embodied_world_model）是世界模型方向最宽泛的CANONICAL子主题，涵盖面向智能体规划控制的世界模型研究，且该论文明确讨论"world-model collapse"对long-horizon agents的影响，与规划控制密切相关。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model collapse in long-horizon agents, a critical bottleneck for world model research, with strong empirical phase transition analysis. Signals: world model collapse, phase transition, long-horizon agents, implicit world models, state fidelity failure

Abstract: Water looks unchanged as it warms, then at a critical point it boils. We ask whether long-horizon language agents show an analogous transition in their implicit world models. In some parameter settings, changing state load by a small amount, or adding a single step of horizon, leaves behavior nearly unchanged; near a critical boundary, the same small change causes a sudden world collapse. We study this effect in a deterministic task family with exact per-step gold state. A large grid search over state cardinality, dependency density, horizon, branching, observation mode, and mutation rate reveals a phase diagram: a solved plateau, a narrow transition band, and a collapse floor. Per-step traces show the mechanism: world-state fidelity fails before action validity, so the agent is not merely choosing a bad action; it is acting from a corrupted world. Stronger models translate the critical boundary but do not remove the qualitative transition. These results make world-model collapse a measurable bottleneck for long-horizon agents.

</details>

*Xinyuan Song, Zekun Cai*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预算受限的环境探测机制（Budgeted Probing），允许语言智能体在执行任务动作前，主动向环境询问某个信念字段并更新其世界模型，从而在错误发生前修复模型漂移。通过类型分层分析（程序性信念 vs. 空间性信念）和受控实验，证明了结构化探测策略能有效降低终端世界模型误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程语言智能体在决策过程中会维护一个私有的世界模型，但该模型可能随时间漂移，导致后续决策在错误动作执行前就已注定失败。现有方法缺乏一种直接、高效的修复机制来在规划过程中校准世界模型。

Method: 提出 Budgeted Probing 操作符，将环境交互视为稀缺的校准资源。智能体在提交下一个任务动作前，可以选择向环境询问一个信念字段并用答案更新世界模型。论文对信念类型进行分层分析（程序性信念如工具依赖 vs. 空间性信念如物体位置），并设计受控实验验证不同探测策略的效果。

Result: 受控实验表明，在规划过程中引入环境证据可以有效降低终端世界模型误差，且探测策略应遵循任务结构——程序性信念可通过定向检查修复，空间性信念更依赖结构线索，智能体自身的置信度在环境变化时不可靠。

Conclusion: 环境交互不仅是推进任务的手段，更是一种稀缺的校准资源。通过预算受限的结构化探测，语言智能体可以在错误发生前主动修复世界模型漂移，提升长程任务的可靠性。

Classification reason: 论文研究语言智能体在长程任务中通过预算受限的环境探测来校准世界模型，属于世界模型在规划与控制中的应用，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model calibration for language agents, a core subtopic with high weight, and introduces a novel budgeted probing mechanism. Signals: world model calibration, budgeted environment probing, procedural vs spatial beliefs, agent belief drift, structured belief tables

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents do not only choose actions; they carry a private model of the world from one decision to the next. When that model drifts, a later failure can be decided before the failing action is ever taken. We study a direct repair mechanism: before committing to the next task action, an agent may ask the environment about one belief field and write the answer back into its world model. This makes environment interaction a scarce calibration resource, not merely a way to advance the task. We introduce \method, a budgeted probing operator for structured belief tables. The useful probes are not the same everywhere. Procedural beliefs, such as tool dependencies, can often be repaired by targeted checks, but those checks spend steps that the task may need. Spatial beliefs, such as object locations and graph edges, rely more on structural cues; the agent's own confidence can be a poor guide when the world changes off-screen. A type-stratified analysis formalizes this probe-action frontier, and controlled experiments show that mid-planning environment evidence reduces terminal world-model error when the probe policy follows the structure of the task.

</details>

*Hongyi Lin, Yang Liu, Jinhua Zhao, Xiaobo Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 本文提出FAT框架，通过任务分解而非模型替换的方式，让基础模型（VLM）执行代理推理任务（选择/验证），专用模型保持结构化预测，在2D/3D检测、轨迹预测和语义分割上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在具身智能中直接进行结构化预测（如几何数值估计）能力不如专用模型，存在能力不匹配问题。作者质疑是否应该用基础模型完全替代专用模型，提出应通过更匹配基础模型优势的任务进行协作。

Method: 提出FAT框架，将结构化预测分解为三个步骤：(1) 专用模型在原生输出空间生成几何和物理上合理的假设；(2) 信息空间重建，将假设转化为多模态候选表示；(3) 基础模型执行有界的代理任务（如选择或验证）。具体实例化为ProxySelect，使用VLM对重建的多模态候选进行选择。

Result: 在2D目标检测、3D目标检测、轨迹预测和语义分割四个任务上，ProxySelect一致性地提升了专用基线模型的性能，且显著优于直接使用基础模型进行回归的方法，同时计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通用协作原则：专用模型保留任务特定结构，基础模型通过上下文代理推理来优化假设。这表明基础模型与专用模型的协作应基于任务分解而非模型替代。

Classification reason: 论文提出FAT框架，将基础模型（VLM）与专用预测模型协作用于具身智能中的结构化预测任务（2D/3D检测、轨迹预测、语义分割），属于具身智能中基础模型与专用模型协作的规划控制范式，映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel collaboration framework (FAT/ProxySelect) that aligns with world model and efficient inference priorities, but the subtopic (embodied world model) is not explicitly prioritized. Signals: Foundation model collaboration, proxy task reasoning, task decomposition, structured prediction, vision-language model

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly integrated into embodied intelligence systems, but directly assigning them structured prediction tasks requires precise geometric and numerical estimation, where specialized models often remain stronger. This capability mismatch raises a key question: should foundation models replace task-specific predictors, or should they collaborate through tasks better aligned with their strengths? We propose FAT, a foundation-model-augmented task-specific reasoning framework that treats collaboration as task decomposition rather than model replacement. FAT decomposes structured prediction into specialist prediction, information-space reconstruction, and foundation-model proxy reasoning. The specialist generates geometrically and physically valid hypotheses in the native output space, while the foundation model performs a bounded proxy task, such as selection or verification, over reconstructed multimodal candidates. We instantiate this principle as ProxySelect with a vision--language model. Across 2D object detection, 3D object detection, trajectory prediction, and semantic segmentation, ProxySelect consistently improves specialized baselines and substantially outperforms direct foundation-model regression at lower computational cost. These results suggest a general collaboration principle: specialized models preserve task-specific structure, while foundation models refine their hypotheses through contextual proxy reasoning.

</details>


### [34] [AdaJEPA: An Adaptive Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32026)
*Ying Wang, Oumayma Bounou, Yann LeCun, Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: AdaJEPA 提出一种自适应潜世界模型，在模型预测控制闭环中通过测试时自适应（自监督信号）持续更新模型参数，显著提升分布偏移下目标到达任务的规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜世界模型在测试时通常保持冻结，当环境分布发生偏移时预测会变得不准确，导致规划失败。作者希望让世界模型在测试时也能自适应调整，以应对分布偏移。

Method: AdaJEPA 在训练后，于 MPC 闭环中执行：规划并执行第一个动作块，利用观测到的下一状态转换作为自监督适应信号更新模型参数，然后重新规划。每次 MPC 重规划步骤仅需一个梯度步即可完成自适应。

Result: 在多种目标到达任务中，AdaJEPA 显著提升了规划成功率，且仅需每个 MPC 重规划步骤一个梯度步即可实现显著改进。

Conclusion: 测试时自适应是提升潜世界模型在分布偏移下鲁棒性的有效策略，闭环自监督更新无需额外专家演示即可持续校准世界模型。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AdaJEPA，一种在模型预测控制（MPC）闭环中进行测试时自适应的潜空间世界模型，用于目标到达任务中的规划与控制。这与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配，因为论文核心关注世界模型在规划控制中的自适应与闭环更新。

Importance reason: AdaJEPA advances world model adaptation for planning under distribution shift, aligning with user's high-priority world model and efficient inference subtopics. Signals: world model, test-time adaptation, model predictive control, self-supervised learning, distribution shift

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning from high-dimensional observations by predicting future states in a compact latent space. However, these models are typically kept frozen at test time: when their predictions become inaccurate, planning can fail, especially under test-time distribution shift. To address this, we propose AdaJEPA, an adaptive latent world model that performs test-time adaptation within the closed loop of model predictive control (MPC). After training, AdaJEPA plans and executes the first action chunk, uses the observed next-state transition as a self-supervised adaptation signal, and replans with the updated model. This closed-loop update continuously recalibrates the world model without additional expert demonstrations. Across a range of goal-reaching tasks, AdaJEPA substantially improves planning success with as few as one gradient step per MPC replanning step.

</details>

### [35] [Bridging Local Observation and Global Simulation in Closed-Loop Traffic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31844)
*Ziyan Wang, Tan Xiang, Peng Chen, Xintao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CRAFT通过自监督故障发现和偏好引导的测试时对齐，缓解闭环交通模拟中局部观测与全局模拟之间的上下文不匹配问题，在不重新训练基础模拟器的情况下减少31.2%的碰撞和33.2%的交通违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归交通模拟器在基于自车中心驾驶日志训练时，自车有丰富的局部观测，而周围智能体因感知限制和遮挡仅被部分观测。这种局部-全局上下文不匹配在日志训练中隐藏，但在闭环推演中暴露，导致异常停车、不安全交互和违规行为。

Method: CRAFT将基础模拟器作为全局可观测沙盒，从记录的初始状态生成多样化的假设推演以暴露上下文引起的故障；将这些故障与人类对齐的驾驶先验结合，转化为偏好监督信号，训练上下文偏好评估器（CPE）；推理时CPE作为即插即用的对齐模块，在完整场景上下文中对候选动作评分并重新加权自回归解码，实现全局一致的行为。

Result: 在不重新训练基础模拟器的情况下，碰撞减少31.2%，交通违规减少33.2%。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过测试时对齐有效缓解了局部-全局上下文不匹配问题，为闭环交通模拟提供了一种无需重新训练即可提升行为真实性的轻量级方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出CRAFT框架，用于闭环交通模拟中的局部-全局上下文不匹配问题，属于交通场景下的交互式模拟器/环境动力学建模，与[可交互与物理世界建模]（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model and neural simulator priorities with a novel test-time alignment method for closed-loop traffic simulation. Signals: world model, neural simulator, closed-loop simulation, test-time alignment, self-supervised failure discovery

Abstract: A local-to-global context mismatch arises when autoregressive traffic simulators trained on ego-centric driving logs are deployed in globally observable closed-loop environments. In such logs, the ego vehicle has rich local observations, while surrounding agents are only partially observed due to perception limits and occlusions. As a result, simulators may learn incomplete context--action mappings that remain hidden in log-based training but emerge during closed-loop rollouts, leading to unrealistic behaviors such as abnormal stops, unsafe interactions, and rule violations. We propose CRAFT, a Contextual pReference Alignment Framework for Traffic Simulation, to mitigate this mismatch via self-supervised failure discovery and preference-guided test-time alignment. CRAFT treats the base simulator as a globally observable sandbox, generating diverse what-if rollouts from logged initial states to expose context-induced failures. These failures are grounded with human-aligned driving priors and converted into preference supervision for training a Contextual Preference Evaluator (CPE). At inference time, CPE acts as a plug-in alignment module that scores candidate actions under complete scene context and reweights autoregressive decoding toward globally coherent behaviors. CRAFT mitigates this local-to-global contextual bias, reducing collisions by 31.2\% and traffic violations by 33.2\% without retraining the base simulator.

</details>

*Ting-Bing Xu, Jiacheng Sui, Zhe Gao, Kewei Shi, Wenjin Yang, Zhicheng Liu, Zhaoxu Sun, Mingchao Sun, Hongyu Pan, Fan Jiang, Mu Xu, Qi Fan, Yong Li, Baoquan Chen*
