<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式世界模型（IWM）评测仅停留在轨迹级别的动作跟随评估，忽略了记忆保持和交互物理一致性等关键能力，缺乏系统性的长程稳定性评测方法。

Method: 提出 WorldRoamBench 基准，包含 600+ 测试用例，覆盖 Nature/Urban/Indoor 场景，支持第一/第三人称视角和 WASD 10-60s 连续交互。在四个维度分别设计创新度量：动作维度采用逐帧度量避免跨模型语义尺度差异；视觉维度采用分段漂移度量捕捉中序列非单调崩溃；物理维度采用可控性门控评估力学、光学和3D一致性；记忆维度采用动作解耦协议，通过过渡局部化3D点云重建评估场景记忆，通过跟踪+VLM推理评估主体记忆。

Result: 评测了 10+ 个开源和闭源模型，发现没有任何模型能在所有维度上可靠满足要求，即使最佳模型也仅达到中等分数。

Conclusion: WorldRoamBench 揭示了现有 IWM 在长程稳定性、物理一致性、记忆忠实性方面的显著不足，为未来构建可部署于真实应用的稳定 IWM 提供了系统性的评测框架和方向指引。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 WorldRoamBench，一个用于评测交互式世界模型（IWM）长程稳定性的开放世界基准，涵盖动作、视觉、物理、记忆四个维度。这属于可交互与物理世界建模（interactive_physical_world）范畴，该 canonical 子主题定义包含"交互式模拟器"和"环境动力学建模"，与该基准评测目标高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model stability and evaluation, a high-priority subtopic with strong alignment to user's world_model direction and boost keywords. Signals: world model benchmark, long-horizon stability, interactive world model, physics evaluation, memory evaluation

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in interactive world models (IWMs), existing benchmarks evaluate action following only at trajectory level and ignore memory and interaction physics. We introduce WorldRoamBench, an open-world benchmark for long-horizon stability across four dimensions, each with tailored innovations: (i) Action: per-frame action metric bypassing cross-model semantic scale disparity and exposing failures hidden by trajectory; (ii) Vision: segment-based drift metric capturing non-monotonic mid-sequence collapse missed by start-vs-end comparisons; (iii) Physics: controllability-gated evaluation over mechanics, optics, and 3D consistency, scoring plausibility under faithful action execution; (iv) Memory: action-decoupled protocol evaluating scene memory via transition-localized 3D point-cloud reconstruction and subject memory via tracking-plus-VLM reasoning. The benchmark comprises 600+ test cases across Nature, Urban, and Indoor scenes in first/third-person views with WASD 10-60s continuous interaction. Evaluating 10+ open/closed-source models reveals none reliably satisfies all dimensions; even the best achieves only moderate scores. Advances on WorldRoamBench are steps toward IWMs that are stable, physically grounded, memory-faithful, and deployable in real-world applications.

</details>


### [37] [World Narrative Model for Highly Controllable Video Generation: A Paradigm Shift from Pixel Sampling to Physical World Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31946)
*Ye Chen, Xuanhong Chen, Yupeng Zhu, Liming Tan, Zhewen Wan, Yuxuan Xiong, Tielong Wang, Jinfan Liu, Wuze Zhang, Xiongzhen Zhang, Feifei Li, Xianglin Luo, Zhehan Zhao, Zhifan Zhang, Laisheng Kou, Zhujing Liang, Yugang Chen, Muchun Chen, Xu Miao, Yijing Zhang, Xiaojie Sheng, Qiang Hu, Jialiang Chen, Weimin Zhang, Wenjun Zhang, Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (94.4) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 World Narrative Model (WNM)，一种将视频生成从端到端像素采样范式转变为结构化物理世界编排的新范式。WNM 通过协作智能体将多模态输入（文本、参考视频、草图）转化为可编辑的 4D 世界表征（场景几何、物体布局、骨架运动、轨迹、相机运动、光照），再以此驱动现有视频基础模型作为神经着色器渲染最终视频，大幅提升可控性，减少"抽卡"循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型将视频视为像素分布采样问题，缺乏对几何、运动、相机参数、光照等物理量的显式可控性，导致专业内容创作陷入低效的"抽卡"循环。作者旨在通过解耦"渲染什么"（结构化物理叙事）与"如何渲染"（像素生成），实现工业级可控视频生成。

Method: WNM 包含三个核心组件：(1) 协作智能体系统，将稀疏多模态输入（文本、参考视频、草图）转化为完整的 4D 世界表征，包括场景几何、物体布局、角色骨架运动、轨迹、相机运动、光照等；(2) 确定性结构化蓝图，作为物理级精确的中间表示；(3) 适配器模块，将蓝图注入现有视频基础模型（冻结或轻量微调），使其作为神经着色器忠实渲染最终视频。整个框架支持自动世界生成与预可视化，并提供导演控制台供人工精调。

Result: 实验表明，WNM 大幅减少了概率性"抽卡"调用次数，生成的视频在布局、运动、摄影风格上紧密遵循创作者意图，实现了定量可控的视频生成。

Conclusion: WNM 提出了一种从像素采样到物理世界编排的范式转变，通过结构化 4D 世界表征解耦内容规划与像素渲染，显著提升了视频生成的可控性。框架开放模块化，各组件可独立改进，为工业级视频生成提供了新路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 World Narrative Model (WNM)，将视频生成解耦为结构化物理叙事（4D场景几何、物体布局、骨架运动、轨迹、相机运动、光照等）与像素渲染两个阶段，属于可交互与物理世界建模（interactive_physical_world）范畴。同时其视频生成部分也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Proposes a paradigm shift from pixel sampling to structured 4D world orchestration for highly controllable video generation, directly aligning with world model and video generation priorities. Signals: world model, video generation, controllable generation, 4D representation, neural shader

Abstract: The fundamental obstacle to industrial grade video generation is the lack of controllability: existing models treat video as a pixel distribution sampling problem, bypassing the explicit, instance level $4D$ $(3D + T)$ physical world. Consequently, content creators cannot specify geometry, motion, camera parameters, or lighting in a deterministic, quantitative way, leading to the infamous ''gacha'' loop that makes professional content creation prohibitively inefficient and expensive. To address this, we introduce the World Narrative Model (WNM), a paradigm that decouples what to render -- the structured physical narrative -- from how to render -- the pixel generation process. WNM replaces end-to-end black-box sampling with orchestrated $4D$ pre-visualization for media generation. Collaborative agents translate sparse multimodal inputs, including text, reference videos, and sketches, into a fully editable world representation with scene geometry, object layouts, character/animal skeleton motion, trajectories, camera motion, and lighting at quantitative, physically meaningful granularity. This representation acts as a deterministic structural blueprint that drives existing video foundation models, either frozen or lightly adapted, to render final footage, turning the base model into a faithful neural shader. Built on this engine, our human-AI platform supports automatic world generation and pre-visualization aligned with professional filmmaking pipelines, while director consoles enable seamless human refinement. Experiments show that WNM greatly reduces probabilistic ``gacha'' calls and produces videos whose layout, motion, and cinematography closely follow creator intent. The framework is open and modular, allowing each component, such as world representation, control agents, and adapters, to be independently improved. Project website: .

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出流式高斯编码器（Streaming Gaussian Encoding），通过维护一组持久化的潜空间高斯查询来实现4D全景占据跟踪（4D-POT），利用自运动补偿和置信度引导的预算约束进行传播与刷新，并通过深度监督塑造高斯不透明度作为可见性代理，在Occ3D扩展的nuScenes和Waymo数据集上取得了新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于掩码的4D-POT方法在每个时间步重新计算体积表示，导致几何时序一致性不足，尤其在遮挡和静态场景元素下表现不佳。本文旨在通过维护持久化的场景表示来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出流式高斯编码器，将场景建模为一组固定大小的潜空间高斯查询，通过自运动补偿进行传播，并在置信度引导的预算约束下刷新。利用深度监督塑造高斯不透明度作为可见性代理，使置信度作为时序聚合的持久场景支持度量。结合基于warmup的多帧训练策略，实现表示级别的时序一致性。

Result: 在Occ3D扩展的nuScenes和Waymo数据集上，相机4D-POT任务取得新SOTA，在几乎无额外计算开销的情况下显著提升了跟踪一致性，且与现有基于掩码的流水线完全兼容。

Conclusion: 流式高斯编码器通过持久化的场景表示有效提升了4D全景占据跟踪的时序一致性，为动态场景理解提供了一种高效且兼容现有框架的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出流式高斯编码器用于4D全景占据跟踪，维护持久化的体积场景表示，通过高斯查询的传播与更新实现时空一致的场景建模，属于动态4D场景中的世界建模，因此映射到世界模型方向下的[空间3D/4D世界模型]规范子主题。

Importance reason: Novel streaming Gaussian representation for 4D occupancy tracking, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous language/multimodal models and generation. Signals: 4D panoptic occupancy tracking, streaming Gaussian encoder, persistent volumetric representation, ego-motion compensation, confidence-guided budget

Abstract: Camera-based 4D panoptic occupancy tracking (4D-POT) is a promising paradigm for holistic scene understanding from multi-view imagery, enabling joint reasoning about geometry, semantics, and object identities across time. Recent mask-based pipelines achieve strong performance by propagating instance queries across frames. However, their underlying volumetric representations are typically recomputed at each timestep, limiting geometric temporal consistency, particularly under occlusion and for static scene elements. To address this limitation, we propose a streaming Gaussian encoder that maintains a persistent volumetric scene representation for 4D-POT. Our method models the scene as a fixed-size set of latent Gaussian queries that are propagated via ego-motion compensation and refreshed under a confidence-guided budget constraint. Crucially, we shape Gaussian opacities through depth-based supervision to serve as proxy for visibility, enabling confidence to accumulate as a temporally aggregated measure of persistent scene support. Together with a warmup-based multi-frame training strategy, this yields representation-level temporal coherence beyond decoder-only tracking. Extensive experiments on Occ3D-extended nuScenes and Waymo establish a new state-of-the-art for camera-based 4D-POT, improving tracking consistency with negligible computational overhead while remaining fully compatible with existing mask-based pipelines. We provide code and models at .

</details>

*Junhao Chen, Boran Zhang, Mingjin Chen, Henghaofan Zhang, Saining Zhang, Congcong Zhu, Hao Zhao, Ruqi Huang, Zhihao Li, Yufei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OVOW是首个无需训练的、从单目视频重建实例级可仿真4D网格场景的系统。它通过VLM实例发现、类别感知重建、迭代渲染匹配优化恢复度量尺度与6-DoF位姿、物理约束组装四阶段流水线，生成可直接用于物理仿真和编辑的水密网格场景，并建立了首个结构化Video-to-4D评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D重建方法（隐式场、高斯原语、点云）虽然渲染质量高，但输出缺乏水密拓扑、实例分离和标准化物理接口，无法直接用于物理仿真器和具身AI。作者希望填补这一空白，实现从单目视频到可直接用于物理仿真的结构化4D网格场景的转换。

Method: OVOW采用四阶段流水线：(1) VLM实例发现：用视觉语言模型检测、标注并分类所有实例的运动类型（刚性/可变形）；(2) 类别感知重建：对刚性物体重建每实例网格，对可变形物体重建拓扑一致的网格序列；(3) 迭代渲染-匹配-优化：通过渲染-匹配-优化循环恢复度量尺度和6-DoF位姿轨迹；(4) 物理约束组装：施加地面接触和物体间支撑约束。所有运动（刚性和非刚性）均通过直接顶点变形建模，无需类别先验或骨架绑定。

Result: 在两个合成基准（静态和4D）上，OVOW在布局和几何精度上取得最佳结果，光度误差和语义误差最低；在真实单目视频上运行速度比基线快一到两个数量级；下游物理仿真验证了其物理稳定性。

Conclusion: OVOW首次实现了从单目视频到实例级、可仿真4D网格场景的无训练重建，弥合了高质量4D重建与物理仿真/具身AI之间的鸿沟。该方法速度快、物理稳定，并可作为合成视频到4D仿真数据的可扩展引擎，为未来4D世界模型和具身AI提供支持。

Classification reason: 该论文从单目视频重建实例级、可仿真的4D网格场景，属于动态3D/4D场景中的世界建模，与[空间3D/4D世界模型]（spatial_3d_4d_world_model）高度匹配。同时其输出可用于下游物理仿真和具身AI，也与世界模型方向相关。此外，论文还涉及4D生成与重建，可匹配multimodal_generation下的[3D生成与重建]（three_d_generation）。

Importance reason: Pioneers training-free 4D mesh reconstruction from monocular video with direct physics simulation readiness, aligning with world model and video generation priorities. Signals: training-free 4D reconstruction, instance-level watertight mesh, physics simulation ready, VLM-driven instance discovery, iterative render-match-optimize

Abstract: We introduce \textbf{OVOW}, the first training-free system that reconstructs \emph{instance-level, simulation-ready} 4D mesh scenes from a single monocular video. Recent 4D reconstruction achieves impressive rendering quality, but its outputs (\eg, implicit fields, Gaussian primitives, or point clouds) lack the watertight topology, instance separation, and standardized physical interfaces required by physics simulators and embodied AI. OVOW closes this gap with a four-stage pipeline: a vision-language model discovers, labels, and motion-classifies all instances; category-aware reconstruction yields per-instance meshes for rigid objects and topology-consistent mesh sequences for deformable ones; an iterative render-match-optimize procedure recovers metric scale and 6-DoF pose trajectories; and physics-grounded assembly enforces ground contact and inter-object support. Crucially, we model all motion, rigid and non-rigid, through direct vertex deformation without category-specific priors or skeleton rigging, producing watertight mesh scenes ready for downstream physics simulation and editing. We further establish the first benchmark for \emph{structured Video-to-4D} evaluation, with metrics for geometric correctness, instance separation, and physical plausibility beyond visual fidelity; the same pipeline doubles as a scalable engine for \emph{synthesizing} paired video-to-4D simulation data for future 4D world models and embodied AI. Across two synthetic benchmarks (static and 4D), OVOW attains the best overall layout and geometry accuracy and the lowest photometric and semantic error among all baselines, and on monocular video runs one to two orders of magnitude faster than the baselines, while downstream physics simulation confirms its physical stability.

</details>

### [40] [DVG-WM: Disentangled Video Generation Enables Efficient Embodied World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32028)
*Ziyu Shan, Zhenyu Wu, Xiaofeng Wang, Zheng Zhu, Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DVG-WM 提出一种解耦视频生成世界模型框架，将世界模型显式分解为动力学学习（生成中间视觉状态序列）和视觉合成（高保真视频渲染）两个模块，并引入级联机制通过流匹配将动力学映射到视频潜空间，配合潜退化机制恢复接触细节。在 LIBERO 和真实机器人平台上实现最高 3.97 倍加速，验证了解耦范式在机器人操作世界模型中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的具身世界模型存在根本性纠缠：精确建模动力学需要低层级时序推理，而生成高分辨率帧需要高层级语义驱动的视觉合成。这种纠缠导致要么推理速度慢（不适合迭代规划），要么预测过于粗糙（丢失接触丰富的细节）。

Method: DVG-WM 将世界模型解耦为两个阶段：(1) 动力学学习阶段——以初始观测和语言指令为条件，首先生成合理的中间视觉状态序列来预览物理交互；(2) 视觉合成阶段——对中间状态进行细化以获得高保真视频。提出高效级联机制：使用流匹配（flow matching）将动力学直接映射到视频潜空间，并引入潜退化机制（latent degradation mechanism）来恢复接触丰富的细节。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准和真实机器人平台上，DVG-WM 在提升视频质量的同时实现了最高 3.97 倍的推理加速，验证了解耦视频生成作为高效具身世界模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 解耦视频生成范式能够有效解决世界模型中动力学建模与视觉合成之间的纠缠问题，在保持高质量预测的同时显著提升推理效率，为机器人操作任务中的迭代规划提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出解耦视频生成世界模型（DVG-WM），用于机器人操作中的未来状态预测和规划，属于视频生成式世界模型范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题「视频世界模型」。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and video generation for robotics with a novel disentangled framework and significant speedup. Signals: world model, video generation, flow matching, efficient inference, robotic manipulation

Abstract: Video-based embodied world models provide an appealing substrate for robotic manipulation by predicting future states, yet current approaches remain limited by a fundamental entanglement: accurately modeling dynamics typically requires low-level temporal reasoning, while producing high-resolution frames demands expansive visual synthesis according to high-level semantics. This entanglement results in slow inference speed for iterative planning or too coarse predictions to retain contact-rich details. To solve this dilemma, we present Disentangled Video Generation World Model (DVG-WM), an efficient framework that explicitly decomposes world modeling into dynamics learning and visual synthesis. Conditioned on an initial observation and a language instruction, our model first generates a plausible sequence of intermediate visual states to preview the physical interaction and refines them to obtain high-fidelity videos. Furthermore, an efficient cascading mechanism is proposed, where DVG-WM uses flow matching to directly map the dynamics to video latents, and introduces a latent degradation mechanism to regenerate contact-rich details. Experiments on LIBERO and real-world platforms demonstrate improved video quality with up to 3.97 times acceleration, validating that disentangled video generation can be an efficient embodied world model for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [41] [Delta-JEPA: Learning Action-Sensitive World Models via Latent Difference Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31232)
*Zhenghao Zhang, Yuanxiang Wang, Zhenyu Guan, Yujia Yang, Bingkang Shi, Tianyu Zong, Hongzhu Yi, Guoqing Chao, Xingchen Chen, Tiankun Yang, Chenxi Bao, Tao Yu, Jingjing Zhou, Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Delta-JEPA 提出了一种免重建的世界模型方法，通过潜差异动作解码器（LDAD）从连续观测的潜位移中重建动作，从而防止联合嵌入目标崩溃为动作不敏感的表征，在视觉连续控制任务中提升了规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的免重建联合嵌入世界模型（如JEPA）在学习潜空间动态时，容易产生动作不敏感的表征——即不同动作导致相似的潜嵌入，从而无法有效支持基于rollout的规划。作者希望在不依赖像素重建的前提下，让潜空间保持对动作的敏感性。

Method: 提出 Delta-JEPA，核心组件是 Latent Difference Action Decoder (LDAD)。LDAD 不采用传统的将端点嵌入拼接后推断动作的方式，而是从连续观测的潜位移（latent displacement）中重建执行的动作。这种位移级监督直接正则化了潜空间的转移几何：相邻嵌入不能坍缩，否则会丢失动作信息；不同动作被鼓励产生可区分的潜变化。模型仅使用潜预测和动作重建损失，避免像素重建和分布匹配正则化器。

Result: 在四个视觉连续控制任务上，Delta-JEPA 在规划性能上优于基于 JEPA 和表征学习的基线方法。消融实验表明，基于位移的动作解码始终优于端点拼接方式；动作敏感性分析显示 Delta-JEPA 产生了更清晰的动作条件潜响应。

Conclusion: 监督潜差异是一种简单且有效的机制，能够学习抗坍缩且动作敏感的世界模型，为免重建世界模型提供了一种实用的改进方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Delta-JEPA，一种基于潜空间差异解码的动作敏感世界模型，用于视觉连续控制任务中的规划。这属于世界模型方向下的视频世界模型（video_world_model）子主题，因为其核心是学习视觉世界模型的潜动态，进行未来预测和基于rollout的规划。

Importance reason: Directly advances action-sensitive world models for planning, a core priority subtopic, with a novel latent difference decoding method that prevents representation collapse. Signals: world model, action-sensitive latent dynamics, latent difference decoding, visual continuous control, JEPA extension

Abstract: Learning visual world models for planning requires compact latent dynamics that remain sensitive to actions, yet reconstruction-free joint-embedding objectives can collapse to action-insensitive representations. We propose Delta-JEPA, an end-to-end reconstruction-free world model that augments latent forward prediction with a Latent Difference Action Decoder (LDAD). Unlike inverse decoders that infer actions from concatenated endpoint embeddings, LDAD reconstructs the executed action from the latent displacement between consecutive observations. This displacement-level supervision directly regularizes transition geometry: adjacent embeddings cannot collapse without losing action information, and different actions are encouraged to induce distinguishable latent changes for rollout-based planning. Delta-JEPA uses only latent prediction and action reconstruction, avoiding pixel reconstruction and distribution-matching regularizers. Across four visual continuous-control tasks, Delta-JEPA improves planning over JEPA-based and representation-learning world model baselines. Ablations show that displacement-based action decoding is consistently more effective than endpoint concatenation, and action-sensitivity analyses show clearer action-conditioned latent responses. These results indicate that supervising latent differences is a simple and effective mechanism for collapse-resistant and action-sensitive world model learning.

</details>


### [42] [Learning Video Dynamics with Predictive Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31050)
*Yujin Tang, Tian Zhou, Xin Lin, Cheng Tan, Yifan Hu, Rong Jin, SouYoung Jin, Liang Sun*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 值得关注 (72.52) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Predictive Differentiable Rendering (PDR)，一种基于可微2D高斯渲染的视频预测新范式。通过PredGS适配器将现有像素空间预测器与连续2D高斯表征桥接，结合CUDA加速的可微渲染器predgsplat，以L1+SSIM损失替代MSE，在TaxiBJ、WeatherBench、KTH、Human3.6M等基准上显著提升了视频预测的细节保真度和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性视频预测模型在离散像素空间中操作，主要使用逐像素MSE损失优化，导致预测结果过度平滑、缺乏细粒度视觉细节。作者希望弥合离散与连续表征之间的鸿沟，实现高保真的未来帧预测。

Method: 提出PDR框架：1) PredGS——基于2D高斯表征的轻量即插即用适配器，可无缝集成到现有像素空间预测器中；2) predgsplat——CUDA加速的可微2D高斯渲染器，支持任意通道，每个高斯由5+C个可学习参数（位置、尺度、旋转、C通道振幅）定义，渲染速度比基线快10倍；3) 使用L1+SSIM联合损失替代MSE损失进行优化。

Result: 在TaxiBJ、WeatherBench、KTH、Human3.6M四个真实世界基准上，PDR一致超越现有方法，在细节保持、视觉保真度和预测精度方面均取得更优结果。

Conclusion: PDR通过可微2D高斯渲染将视频预测从离散像素空间提升到连续表征空间，有效克服了MSE损失导致的模糊问题，为高保真视频预测提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出PDR（Predictive Differentiable Rendering）用于视频未来帧预测，属于视频世界模型中的未来预测与视频动力学建模。与[world_model]方向下的[视频世界模型]（video_world_model）高度匹配，该子主题涵盖视频生成式世界模型、未来预测等。

Importance reason: Bridges continuous 2D Gaussian representation with video prediction, directly aligning with user's high-priority continuous multimodal and world model interests. Signals: continuous 2D Gaussian representation, world model, video prediction, differentiable rendering, L1+SSIM loss

Abstract: How to accurately predict a high-fidelity future world? While the visual world is inherently continuous, existing deterministic video prediction models operate in discrete pixel space and are mainly optimized with pixel-wise mean squared error (MSE), which often leads to over-smoothed predictions and a lack of fine-grained visual details. To address these limitations, we propose Predictive Differentiable Rendering (PDR), a novel end-to-end video prediction paradigm that bridges the gap between discrete and continuous representations. Inspired by recent progress in 3D reconstruction with 3D Gaussian Splatting, we introduce PredGS, a lightweight and plug-and-play adapter based on 2D Gaussian representation, which could be seamlessly integrated with existing pixel space predictors, significantly improving spatial detail preservation with negligible computational overhead. Furthermore, we develop predgsplat, a CUDA-accelerated differentiable 2D Gaussian renderer supporting arbitrary channels. Each Gaussian is defined by 5 + C learnable parameters (position, scale, rotation, and C channel amplitudes) and achieves up to 10x faster rendering than the baseline. Optimized by a combined L1 and SSIM loss, PDR overcomes the inherent blurring tendencies of MSE Loss, significantly enhancing the prediction performance. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world benchmarks, including TaxiBJ, WeatherBench, KTH, and Human3.6M, demonstrate that PDR consistently surpasses existing methods, delivering superior detail preservation, visual fidelity, and predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [43] [ForgeDrive: Bidirectional Cross-Conditioning for Unified Visual-Action Generation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31226)
*Xuchang Zhong, He Zheng, Chenxu Zhao, Tianxiong Lv, Hangqi Fan, Bohua Wang, Yushan Liu, Zhihao Liao, Leigang Luo, Congyang Zhao, Yang Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 值得关注 (70.91) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ForgeDrive 提出了一种面向自动驾驶的统一自回归扩散框架，通过"先行动后想象"（act-then-imagine）范式实现视觉与动作的双向交叉条件生成，在 NAVSIM 上超越现有规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型采用"先想象后行动"（imagine-then-act）范式，视觉生成阶段的误差会级联传播到动作规划中。作者希望打破这种单向依赖，让动作生成与视觉生成相互促进。

Method: 将未来建模为逐时间步的帧-动作对序列，训练时解耦两种模态的扩散时间步并引入 UniDiffuser 风格噪声调度器，实现双向条件生成；推理时采用 act-then-imagine 范式，先生成动作再用动作辅助生成未来帧，并加入未来自车状态预测增强规划能力。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 基准上，ForgeDrive 统一了驾驶模拟、规划和视觉里程计任务，在无需后训练策略的情况下超越了现有强规划器。

Conclusion: ForgeDrive 验证了动作生成与视觉生成可以相互促进，打破了传统级联范式的局限，为自动驾驶世界模型提供了新的统一框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向自动驾驶的统一视觉-动作生成框架，核心是动作条件视频生成与未来场景预测，属于世界模型方向中的视频世界模型（video_world_model）[CANONICAL] 范畴。同时其统一生成框架也涉及多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Novel act-then-imagine paradigm for world models with bidirectional cross-conditioning, but focused on autonomous driving rather than general video generation. Signals: world model, autoregressive diffusion, bidirectional cross-conditioning, act-then-imagine, autonomous driving

Abstract: World-model-based autonomous driving endows the model with the ability to understand scene evolution. Yet this promise is undermined by the prevailing imagine-then-act paradigm, which allows errors from the more challenging visual generation stage to cascade into action planning. We introduce ForgeDrive, a unified autoregressive diffusion framework with visual-action cross-conditioning that closes this gap through act-then-imagine paradigm. ForgeDrive factorizes the future as a sequence of per-timestep frame-action pairs, intertwining each action with its corresponding visual observation. During training, we decouple the diffusion timesteps of the two modalities and introduce a UniDiffuser-style noise scheduler to get the ability to infer either modality from its counterpart and deepen understanding of relationships between images and actions. At inference, we propose a novel act-then-imagine inference paradigm, and find that at each step, action generation is a capability internalized during training, requiring no clean future frame as a prerequisite at inference time; instead, the generated action can improve the accuracy of future frame generation, which in turn enhances the quality of the next action. Additionally, we augment each step with future ego-status prediction, further sharpening planning ability. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM demonstrate that ForgeDrive not only unifies driving simulation, planning, and visual odometry into a single model, but also outperforms existing strong planners without any post-training strategy.

</details>


### [44] [MemLearner: Learning to Query Context memory for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31734)
*Jiwen Yu, Jianxiong Gao, Jianhong Bai, Yiran Qin, Kaiyi Huang, Quande Liu, Xintao Wang, Pengfei Wan, Kun Gai, Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: MemLearner 提出一种基于学习的自适应上下文查询方法，利用视频生成模型自身的视觉先验来检索历史帧作为记忆，解决视频世界模型在长时生成中场景不一致的问题，在遮挡和动态物体场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型在长时生成中缺乏记忆机制，导致生成的场景随时间推移出现不一致。现有基于规则的上下文帧检索方法在场景遮挡和动态物体等复杂情况下泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出 MemLearner，使用可学习的查询 token 桥接上下文帧与预测帧，利用预训练视频生成模型自身的视觉先验进行上下文查询，无需额外训练独立模块。同时收集了带相机位姿标注的长视频数据集，并提出多数据集训练策略，结合标注的渲染视频与未标注的真实世界视频进行训练。

Result: 在多个基准上，MemLearner 在场景一致性和记忆保持方面显著优于先前的视频世界模型，尤其在遮挡和动态物体等挑战性场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 基于学习的自适应上下文查询方法能有效提升视频世界模型的长时生成一致性，利用生成模型自身的先验知识进行记忆检索是一种高效且可泛化的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MemLearner，一种基于学习的自适应上下文查询方法，用于视频世界模型中的长时场景一致性建模。论文核心是视频世界模型中的记忆与上下文检索问题，属于世界模型方向下的视频世界模型（video_world_model）子主题，该子主题为 [CANONICAL] 条目，直接复用。

Importance reason: Directly advances video world models with a learning-based memory mechanism, a top priority subtopic. Signals: video world model, learning-based context query, scene consistency, long video generation, occlusion handling

Abstract: Video World Models are interactive video generation models that predict future world states based on user actions and history video frames. A critical challenge in video world models is the lack of memory, causing inconsistent generated scenes over extended durations. Previous methods explored rule-based context frame retrieval as memory, but they fail to generalize in scenarios with scene occlusions and dynamic objects. We propose MemLearner, a learning-based adaptive context query method using query tokens to bridge context and predicted tokens. By leveraging the video generation model itself for context querying, MemLearner exploits pre-trained visual priors without training additional modules from scratch, and incorporates efficient strategies for training and inference. We collect a dataset of long videos with scene occlusions and dynamic objects, paired with camera pose annotations, and propose a multi-dataset training strategy leveraging both annotated rendered and unannotated real-world videos. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MemLearner significantly outperforms prior video world models in terms of scene consistency and memory, particularly under challenging occlusion and dynamic scenarios.

</details>

### [45] [CasaMaestro: Multi-View Panoramas for House-Scale 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31086)
*Yuzhou Ji, Xiaotian Yang, Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: CasaMaestro 是一种前馈式模型，仅需20-50张稀疏多视角室内全景图即可直接预测度量深度和相机位姿，实现房屋尺度的快速点云3D重建，支持闭环仿真中的室内资产获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭部署的具身AI系统需要快速、度量的住宅空间3D重建以支持导航、交互和长程任务执行。传统针孔相机3D重建管线视野有限，覆盖多房间需要数千张图像且存在累积漂移问题。

Method: CasaMaestro 采用前馈式架构，以20-50张稀疏多视角室内全景图为输入，直接预测度量深度和相机位姿，无需增量对齐，从而避免长链累积漂移，实现房屋尺度的完整点云重建。

Result: 在真实场景和合成场景上均能鲁棒地提供高质量重建结果，可作为获取房屋尺度3D室内资产的有力基础，应用于闭环仿真。

Conclusion: CasaMaestro 是首个支持多视角全景图进行房屋尺度重建的模型，为家庭具身AI系统的3D环境建模提供了高效、准确的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于多视角全景图的房屋尺度3D重建方法，属于3D场景重建，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel feedforward multi-view panorama 3D reconstruction, but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: 3D reconstruction, multi-view panoramas, house-scale, feedforward model, embodied AI

Abstract: The rise of home-deployed embodied AI systems is driving a growing need for fast, metric 3D reconstruction of residential spaces to support navigation, interaction, and long-horizon task execution. However, the commonly used pinhole-camera 3D reconstruction pipelines struggle to model large indoor residences efficiently due to their limited field of view, to which achieving full coverage across multiple rooms often requires thousands of images and incurs drift from long chains of incremental alignment. In this work, we present CasaMaestro (Spanish words meaning ``house'' and ``master''), a feedforward model that can take only twenty to fifty sparse multi-view indoor panoramas as input and directly predicts metric depth along with camera poses, allowing fast point-cloud reconstruction of the entire house with full coverage. CasaMaestro is the first model that supports house-scale reconstruction with multi-view panoramas. Experiments show that CasaMaestro can robustly provide high quality results in both real-world and synthetic scenes, which can serve as a strong foundation for acquiring house-scale 3D indoor assets to be applied in close-loop simulation.

</details>


### [46] [Horizon3D: Sparse Radar-Camera Fusion for Long-Range 3D Perception in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31096)
*Geonho Bang, Geunju Baek, Dongyoung Lee, Wonjun Jeong, Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (46.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Horizon3D 提出一种稀疏雷达-相机融合框架，用于自动驾驶中的远距离3D目标检测。该方法结合高斯原语与稀疏BEV特征，通过关键点引导的高斯初始化、以目标为中心的稀疏融合和双路径时序融合，在TruckScenes数据集上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达-相机融合方法在远距离场景下性能有限：BEV方法计算量大且丢失细粒度目标细节，查询方法缺乏场景上下文。同时，时序融合需要同时处理稀疏远距离观测的多帧累积和快速移动目标的运动建模。

Method: Horizon3D 包含三个核心模块：(1) 关键点引导的高斯初始化（Keypoint-Guided Gaussian Initialization），在雷达和相机估计的目标关键点处初始化高斯原语；(2) 以目标为中心的稀疏融合（Object-Centric Sparse Fusion），通过稀疏融合细化高斯原语，并将其溅射到BEV平面以融合目标级细节与稀疏雷达BEV上下文；(3) 双路径时序融合（Dual-Path Temporal Fusion），通过BEV路径进行场景级累积，通过高斯路径进行目标级运动传播。

Result: 在TruckScenes验证集上，Horizon3D 以+3.0 NDS和+1.6 mAP超越此前最佳方法，同时保持有竞争力的推理速度。

Conclusion: Horizon3D 通过高斯原语与稀疏BEV特征的结合，以及双路径时序融合设计，有效解决了远距离雷达-相机3D检测中的场景上下文与目标细节兼顾问题，为自动驾驶远距离感知提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文提出基于雷达-相机融合的3D目标检测框架，属于3D感知与重建方向，与[3D 生成与重建]（three_d_generation）中的3D场景感知与重建主题相关。虽然该论文更偏向3D感知而非生成，但3D生成与重建子主题涵盖3D场景理解与重建方法，且无更精确匹配的现有子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving radar-camera fusion, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Sparse radar-camera fusion, Long-range 3D detection, Gaussian primitives, BEV features, TruckScenes dataset

Abstract: Long-range 3D object detection is critical for safe autonomous driving at highway speeds, yet existing radar-camera fusion methods remain limited at extended ranges. BEV-based methods capture scene-level context but incur rapidly growing computation and often lose fine-grained object detail, while query-based methods are efficient but provide limited scene-level context. Temporal fusion further requires both multi-frame accumulation for sparse distant observations and object-level motion modeling for fast-moving objects. We propose Horizon3D, a sparse radar-camera fusion framework for long-range 3D object detection that combines Gaussian primitives with sparse BEV features. Horizon3D initializes Gaussian primitives at radar- and camera-estimated object keypoints using Keypoint-Guided Gaussian Initialization, refines them through Object-Centric Sparse Fusion, and splats them onto the BEV plane to fuse object-level detail with sparse radar BEV context. It further introduces Dual-Path Temporal Fusion, which aggregates temporal cues through a BEV path for scene-level accumulation and a Gaussian path for object-level motion propagation. Experiments on TruckScenes show that Horizon3D achieves state-of-the-art radar-camera 3D detection performance. On the validation set, it outperforms the previous best method by +3.0 NDS and +1.6 mAP while maintaining competitive inference speed.

</details>


### [47] [AugSplat: Radiance Field-Informed Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31556)
*Lorenzo Lazzaroni, Riccardo Bollati, Daniel Barath, Michael Niemeyer, Keisuke Tateno*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AugSplat 提出一种结合 NeRF 与 3DGS 的框架，在稀疏视角场景下先用 NeRF 合成新视角图像，再作为辅助监督信号优化 Gaussian Splatting，从而在保持实时渲染速度的同时显著提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting 在稀疏视角设置下对初始几何质量高度敏感，有限的观测导致点云重建不完整或有噪声，影响最终渲染质量。而 NeRF 虽能从稀疏视角鲁棒重建，但推理速度慢。作者希望结合两者优势，在稀疏视角下实现高质量实时渲染。

Method: 提出 AugSplat 框架，包含两个变体：(1) Staged AugSplat：先用 NeRF 合成视图进行初始优化阶段，再切换到真实图像优化；(2) Dual AugSplat：联合训练真实与合成视图，合成损失权重随时间衰减。核心思路是利用 NeRF 从稀疏输入视图中合成附近新视角图像，增加有效视角覆盖，为 3DGS 优化提供额外监督。

Result: 在稀疏视角 mip-NeRF 360 场景上，AugSplat 相比标准 3DGS 显著提升了重建质量。Staged AugSplat 取得最佳平均性能，Dual AugSplat 性能接近且在整个训练过程中保持真实图像监督。两种变体在推理时均保持实时渲染速度。

Conclusion: AugSplat 有效结合了 NeRF 的鲁棒重建能力与 3DGS 的实时渲染优势，为稀疏视角 3D 场景重建提供了一种简单而有效的解决方案。该方法无需修改 3DGS 核心架构，仅通过数据增强方式即可提升性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AugSplat，利用 NeRF 合成视图辅助稀疏视角下的 3D Gaussian Splatting 优化，属于 3D 场景重建与渲染方法，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D reconstruction with sparse views, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, NeRF, sparse-view reconstruction, view augmentation, real-time rendering

Abstract: Generating high-quality novel views at real-time frame rates remains a central challenge in 3D vision, particularly in sparse-view scenarios. Neural radiance fields have demonstrated robust reconstruction from limited observations, but their reliance on volumetric rendering leads to high computational cost and slow inference. In contrast, Gaussian Splatting methods achieve real-time rendering through rasterization, but their optimization is highly sensitive to the quality of the initial geometry. This sensitivity becomes especially problematic in sparse-view settings, where limited observations often lead to incomplete or noisy point-cloud reconstructions. In this work, we present AugSplat, a simple framework for improving Gaussian Splatting in sparse-view regimes using radiance-field-based view augmentation. We first train a radiance field on the sparse input views and use it to synthesize additional images from nearby novel viewpoints, increasing the effective view-space coverage available for supervision. These synthetic views are then used as auxiliary supervision during Gaussian Splatting optimization. We study two variants: Staged AugSplat, which uses synthetic views for an initial optimization phase before switching to real images, and Dual AugSplat, which jointly trains on real and synthetic views with a decaying synthetic loss weight. Experiments on sparse-view mip-NeRF 360 scenes show that AugSplat improves reconstruction quality over standard Gaussian Splatting. Staged AugSplat achieves the strongest average performance, while Dual AugSplat provides a closely performing formulation that keeps real-image supervision active throughout training, and both variants preserve real-time rendering at inference.

</details>


### [48] [Mesh BDF: Barycentric Dominance Field for 3D Native Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31777)
*Gaochao Song, Haohan Weng, Luo Zhang, Zibo Zhao, Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (61.58) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Barycentric Dominance Field (BDF)，一种定义在三角网格表面上的连续表示，将顶点拓扑连接性编码为连续表面信号，从而弥合离散网格拓扑与连续扩散生成模型之间的鸿沟，使扩散模型能够生成更高质量、可扩展且鲁棒的原生 3D 网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型在原生 3D 网格生成中取得了进展，但其固有约束导致生成网格面数有限、顶点分辨率受限且难以支持纹理。为了突破这些瓶颈，需要一种能兼容扩散模型连续生成范式的网格表示方法。

Method: 提出 Barycentric Dominance Field (BDF)，一种定义在三角网格表面上的连续表示，通过将顶点拓扑连接性转化为连续表面信号，使离散网格拓扑能够无缝集成到现有的 3D 扩散管道中，无需修改架构。

Result: 实验表明，BDF 使扩散模型在原生网格生成中相比最先进的自回归方法实现了显著更高的质量、更强的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BDF 作为一种连续网格表示，有效弥合了离散网格拓扑与连续扩散生成之间的鸿沟，为 3D 原生网格生成提供了一种超越自回归范式的新路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Barycentric Dominance Field (BDF) 用于原生 3D 网格生成，属于 3D 生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Bridges discrete mesh topology and continuous diffusion for 3D native mesh generation, but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: continuous representation on mesh surfaces, bridges discrete and continuous, diffusion-based 3D mesh generation, no autoregressive limitations, no priority subtopic match

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) modeling has recently achieved remarkable progress in native 3D mesh generation, largely due to its natural ability to handle variable-length, discrete data structures. However, the inherent constraints of the AR paradigm severely restrict the generated meshes, leading to limited face counts, bounded vertex resolutions, and difficulties in supporting textures. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose the Barycentric Dominance Field (BDF), a continuous representation defined on triangular mesh surfaces that elegantly encodes vertex topological connectivity. BDF bridges the fundamental gap between discrete mesh topology and continuous diffusion-based generative modeling by transforming connectivity into a continuous surface signal. As an intrinsic mesh property, BDF shares strong similarities with texture maps, enabling its seamless integration into existing 3D diffusion pipelines without requiring architectural modifications. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BDF empowers diffusion models to generate native meshes with significantly higher quality, greater scalability, and stronger robustness compared to state-of-the-art autoregressive methods.

</details>


### [49] [PolyFlow: Continuous Topology Embedding Flow Matching for Artist-style Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30673)
*Chunshi Wang, Haohan Weng, Junliang Ye, Biwen Lei, Yang Li, Zibo Zhao, Zeqiang Lai, Kaiyi Zhang, Yunhan Yang, Zhuo Chen, Chunchao Guo, Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PolyFlow 提出了一种将离散网格拓扑嵌入到连续每顶点状态空间的方法，并基于 Transformer 流匹配框架实现全并行网格生成，在 Toys4K 基准上超越自回归基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 Transformer 能生成高质量网格拓扑，但顺序解码计算开销巨大，比并行生成模型慢数个数量级。而连续扩散/流匹配方法无法直接应用于网格，因为网格连接性是离散的，与标准连续噪声注入和去噪操作不兼容。

Method: 提出紧凑的拓扑嵌入器（topology embedder），将离散网格顶点位置和法线投影到连续每顶点嵌入中，通过时空距离阈值可忠实恢复原始离散邻接信息。预训练并冻结该嵌入器后，任意原始网格可转换为统一的连续每顶点状态空间（位置、法线、隐式拓扑属性）。在此基础上，构建基于 Transformer 的流匹配框架 PolyFlow，以提取的点云特征为条件，实现全并行顶点状态去噪。推理时通过 ODE 求解器快速生成，并可通过指定目标顶点数精确控制输出网格分辨率。

Result: 在 Toys4K 基准上，PolyFlow 在 Chamfer Distance 和 Hausdorff Distance 两个指标上均超越了最先进的自回归基线方法。

Conclusion: PolyFlow 通过将离散网格拓扑嵌入到连续空间，成功将流匹配这一高效并行生成范式应用于网格生成，在保持生成质量的同时大幅提升效率，并支持显式的分辨率控制。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于流匹配的网格（mesh）生成方法，属于 3D 物体生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel continuous mesh representation for parallel generation, but 3D generation is not a priority subtopic. Signals: continuous representation for discrete data, flow matching for 3D, parallel mesh generation, artist-style mesh, Toys4K benchmark

Abstract: Autoregressive Transformers dominate high-quality mesh generation by producing artist-worthy topologies, yet their inherent sequential decoding induces substantial computational overhead, falling orders of magnitude slower than parallel generative models. On the other hand, while continuous diffusion and flow-matching methods support efficient parallel synthesis across a variety of domains, they cannot be directly applied to meshes: mesh connectivity is inherently discrete and incompatible with standard continuous noise injection and denoising operations. To resolve this fundamental incompatibility, we introduce a compact topology embedder that projects discrete mesh vertex positions and normals into continuous per-vertex embeddings, where the original discrete adjacency information can be faithfully recovered via spacetime distance thresholding. After pretraining and freezing this embedder, any raw mesh can be fully converted into a continuous per-vertex state space unifying position, normal, and implicit topological attributes. Built upon this novel continuous mesh representation, we present PolyFlow, a Transformer-based flow-matching framework that achieves fully parallel vertex state denoising conditioned on extracted point-cloud features. During inference, our model completes generation rapidly via an ODE solver, and supports explicit, precise control over output mesh resolution by directly specifying the target vertex count. Extensive evaluations on the Toys4K benchmark demonstrate that PolyFlow surpasses state-of-the-art autoregressive baselines in both Chamfer Distance and Hausdorff Distance.

</details>

### [50] [PhotoQuilt: Training-Free Arbitrary-Resolution Photomosaics via Bootstrapped Tiled Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30968)
*Koorosh Roohi, Javad Rajabi, Andrew Fleet, Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (51.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PhotoQuilt 提出了一种无需训练的框架，通过引导式分块去噪（bootstrapped tiled denoising）生成任意分辨率的光马赛克图像，在保持全局构图的同时确保每个局部图块独立逼真，避免了高分辨率扩散模型的计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成高分辨率光马赛克图像需要同时满足全局构图连贯和局部图块独立逼真两个尺度，现有方法要么直接生成高分辨率图像（成本高且趋向平滑而非马赛克），要么分块生成（保持局部细节但丢失全局结构），缺乏兼顾两者的高效方案。

Method: PhotoQuilt 采用引导式分块去噪流程：首先在低分辨率下生成全局构图以固定布局，然后在潜空间上采样并重新注入噪声以恢复生成能力；去噪过程在固定图块内进行，每个图块独立形成自己的图像，同时共享全局结构保持整体布局一致。由于图块生成独立处理，可扩展到大幅画布而无需二次注意力开销。

Result: 实验表明，PhotoQuilt 在全局结构和局部真实性两个维度上均优于现有基线方法，能够生成任意分辨率的高质量光马赛克图像。

Conclusion: PhotoQuilt 通过引导式分块去噪策略有效解决了高分辨率光马赛克生成中全局与局部尺度兼顾的难题，提供了一种无需训练、可扩展的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出PhotoQuilt，一种无需训练的任意分辨率光马赛克生成框架，通过分块去噪生成全局一致且局部细节丰富的图像。这属于图像生成与编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的可控编辑、风格化生成方向相关，因此复用该分类。

Importance reason: Novel training-free tiled denoising for high-res photomosaics, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: training-free, arbitrary resolution, photomosaics, bootstrapped tiled denoising, diffusion models

Abstract: Photomosaics are large images whose local regions are seen as independent tiles while their overall arrangement forms a coherent scene. Generating them at high resolution, with every tile convincing in its own right, is computationally expensive, since the canvas must hold many detailed tiles at once. We present PhotoQuilt, a training-free framework that generates photomosaics at arbitrary resolution. Diffusion models struggle to satisfy both scales at once, as direct high-resolution generation is costly and tends toward one smooth image rather than a mosaic, while patch-based tiling keeps local detail but loses global structure. PhotoQuilt resolves this with a bootstrapped tiled denoising procedure. We first produce a global composition at low resolution to fix the layout, then upscale it in latent space and re-inject noise to restore generative capacity. Denoising proceeds within fixed tiles, so each forms its own image while the shared global structure holds them in one layout. Because tile generation is handled separately, PhotoQuilt scales to large canvases without quadratic attention cost. Experiments show that PhotoQuilt outperforms current baselines on both global structure and local realism.

</details>


### [51] [WarpI2I: Image Warping for Image-to-Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31018)
*Shen Zheng, Anurag Ghosh, Gaurav Parmar, Srinivasa Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (46.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 WarpI2I，一种基于显著性引导的图像变形-逆变形框架，在编码前将空间表示重新分配到显著区域，从而在不增加潜空间分辨率的情况下更好地保留结构细节，适用于人像重光照、驾驶场景重光照等图到图翻译任务，且可自然扩展到视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的紧凑型潜扩散模型（LDM）在编码高分辨率输入时会压缩空间信息，导致细粒度结构细节丢失。作者希望在不增加潜空间分辨率的前提下，更好地保留图像中的结构信息。

Method: 提出显著性引导的 warp-unwarp 框架：先检测图像中的显著区域，对图像进行空间变形（warp）将更多像素分配给显著区域，然后送入原始扩散模型处理，最后通过逆变形（inverse warp）恢复原始空间布局。此外，提出基于 outpainting 的合成数据生成管线，用于产生高质量的人像重光照配对数据。

Result: 在人像重光照、驾驶场景重光照和翻译任务上，该方法在结构保持、光照忠实度和图像质量方面均优于基线方法，且可逐帧应用于视频，具有良好的时间稳定性。

Conclusion: WarpI2I 是一种模型无关、无需修改架构、计算开销极小的图到图翻译增强框架，能有效提升 LDM 在结构细节保持方面的表现，并自然支持视频应用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于图像变形（warp）的图到图翻译框架，应用于人像重光照、驾驶场景重光照等任务，属于图像编辑与可控编辑范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题「图像与视频编辑」。

Importance reason: Novel warp-unwarp framework for I2I translation but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Image-to-image translation, saliency-guided warp, latent diffusion models, model-agnostic, video extension

Abstract: Image-to-image (I2I) translation has achieved strong results in tasks like human relighting and driving scene translation using latent diffusion models (LDMs). However, compact LDMs often struggle to preserve fine-grained structures because the encoder compresses high-resolution inputs into a spatially downsampled latent space. To address this issue, we propose a simple saliency-guided warp-unwarp framework that reallocates spatial representation toward salient regions before encoding, enabling better preservation of structural details without increasing latent resolution. The warped image is processed by the original diffusion model and then mapped back via an inverse warp. In addition, we propose a simple and efficient outpainting-based synthetic data generation pipeline to produce high-quality paired data for image relighting. Our method is model-agnostic, requires no architectural modification, and introduces negligible computational overhead. Experiments on human relighting, driving scene relighting, and translation demonstrate improved structural preservation, lighting faithfulness, and image quality, with our framework extending naturally to video via frame-by-frame application with good temporal stability. Project Webpage:

</details>

*Bo Wei, Xianhui Lin, Yi Dong, Zhongzhong Li, Zonghui Li, Zirui Wang, Jiachen Yang, Xing Liu, Hong Gu, Xiaoming Li, Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ART（Anchoring on Reality Makeup Transfer），一种两阶段化妆迁移框架。第一阶段用伪目标初始化模型建立语义对齐，第二阶段通过可微循环将监督从伪目标切换到真实参考图像，从素颜版本重建参考图以惩罚遗漏细节和伪影。同时发布了首个2K分辨率野外化妆数据集 MakeupFaces2K（8573张图像）。实验表明该方法在复杂化妆风格下实现了优越的化妆保真度、背景稳定性和身份保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化妆迁移任务缺乏真实的成对训练数据，现有方法依赖弱先验或大规模编辑模型生成的合成伪目标，导致细粒度细节退化、合成伪影和身份漂移。作者希望突破伪目标监督的天花板，实现更真实、细节更丰富的化妆迁移。

Method: 提出两阶段框架 ART：Stage I 使用伪目标初始化模型，建立基本的语义对齐和全局化妆放置；Stage II 将监督从伪目标切换到真实参考图像，通过可微循环从参考图像的素颜版本重建参考图，该循环会惩罚任何遗漏的细节并覆盖合成伪影。同时构建了 MakeupFaces2K 数据集，包含8573张2K分辨率野外化妆肖像图像。

Result: 大量实验表明，ART 在复杂化妆风格下实现了优越的化妆保真度、强大的背景稳定性和鲁棒的身份保持，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将监督锚定在真实参考图像上而非伪目标，ART 有效突破了伪目标监督的性能上限，为化妆迁移任务提供了更可靠的解决方案。MakeupFaces2K 数据集也为该领域提供了高质量资源。

Classification reason: 该论文研究化妆迁移（Makeup Transfer），属于图像编辑/风格化任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Makeup transfer is a niche image editing task with limited relevance to the user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: makeup transfer, pseudo-target ceiling, two-stage framework, differentiable cycle, MakeupFaces2K dataset

Abstract: Makeup transfer applies a reference cosmetic style to a source face while preserving its identity and geometry. However, this task is severely hindered by the lack of real paired training data. Current methods rely on either weak priors or synthetic pseudo-targets from large-scale editing models. These paradigms provide suboptimal guidance, often leading to degraded fine-grained details, synthetic artifacts, and identity drift. To this end, we propose Anchoring on Reality Makeup Transfer (ART), a two-stage framework with a reality-anchored refinement cycle. In Stage I, the model is initialized with pseudo-targets to establish basic semantic alignment and global makeup placement. Crucially, Stage II shifts supervision from pseudo-targets to the real reference, reconstructing it from its bare-skin counterpart through a differentiable cycle that penalizes any omitted detail and overrides synthetic artifacts. Furthermore, we introduce MakeupFaces2K (MF2K), the first 2K-resolution in-the-wild makeup portrait dataset comprising 8,573 images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior makeup fidelity, strong background stability, and robust identity preservation, especially for complex makeup styles.

</details>


### [53] [Editing Everything Everywhere All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31278)
*Fabio Quattrini, Carmine Zaccagnino, Enis Simsar, Marta Tintoré Gazulla, Rita Cucchiara, Alessio Tonioni, Silvia Cascianelli*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (55.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MICE（Multi-Instance Concurrent Editing），一种无需训练的多实例并发图像编辑方法。通过修改多模态扩散 Transformer 中联合注意力的加性偏置，MICE 能够调控实例级编辑指令、潜变量和上下文 token 之间的交互，实现单次前向传播中对图像多个区域同时进行编辑，有效防止属性泄漏和语义干扰，在视觉质量和编辑忠实度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑方法通常一次只编辑一个区域，多轮编辑效率低且难以保证全局协调。当同时编辑多个区域时，不同编辑指令之间会产生语义干扰，导致属性泄漏和编辑解耦效果差。本文旨在实现单次前向传播中高效、可扩展的多实例并发图像编辑。

Method: MICE 基于多模态扩散 Transformer（MMDiT），通过修改联合注意力的加性偏置（additive bias）来调控 token 间的交互。具体包括：(1) 允许同一实例内部的注意力交互；(2) 惩罚相邻区域 token 之间的交互；(3) 抑制无关的跨实例注意力。用户提供分割掩码标识不同区域，MICE 据此构建注意力偏置矩阵，实现实例级编辑指令与对应区域 token 的绑定，同时保持全局视觉一致性。

Result: 在 LoMOE-Bench 和新提出的 MICE-Bench（平均每图 8.5 个并发编辑）上评估，MICE 在视觉质量保持和编辑指令忠实度方面均优于强基线方法和近期竞争对手。实验表明该方法能有效处理大量并发编辑任务，且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: MICE 是一种无需训练的多实例并发图像编辑方法，通过调控注意力机制有效解决了多编辑指令间的语义干扰问题，实现了高效、可扩展的并发编辑。该方法为多区域图像编辑提供了实用且有效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出多实例并发图像编辑方法 MICE，属于图像编辑方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑" (visual_editing)。

Importance reason: Focuses on image editing, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Training-free multi-instance editing, Multimodal Diffusion Transformer, Attribute leakage prevention, MICE-Bench benchmark, No direct link to autoregressive generation or world models

Abstract: Editing multiple elements of an image in a single forward pass is a practical alternative to multi-turn image manipulation, offering improved efficiency and potentially better harmonization. However, when several instructions target different regions, semantic interference often leads to attribute leakage and poor edit disentanglement, especially as the number of edits increases. In this work, we propose MICE (Multi-Instance Concurrent Editing), a training-free strategy for scalable multi-instance image editing with Multimodal Diffusion Transformers. MICE modifies the additive bias of joint attention to regulate interactions between instance-specific edit instructions, latent, and context tokens identified via user-provided segmentation masks. Specifically, MICE allows intra-instance attention, penalizes interactions between neighboring region tokens, and suppresses unrelated cross-instance attention. As a result, our method enforces attribute binding while preserving global visual consistency. We evaluate MICE on LoMOE-Bench and introduce MICE-Bench, a more challenging benchmark with an average of 8.5 concurrent edits per image. The experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms strong baselines and recent competitors in terms of visual quality preservation and faithfulness to the editing instructions.

</details>


### [54] [Language-Assisted Super-Resolution from Real-World Low-Resolution Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31363)
*Joonkyu Park, Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (51.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出LA-SR框架，利用真实图像中不同深度区域天然存在的分辨率差异提取真实低分辨率（LR）块，并通过视觉语言模型在语言空间中定义无配对超分辨率（SR）损失，实现无需人工合成降质的真实图像超分辨率重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超分辨率方法依赖人工合成降质（如手写核函数或相机ISP调整）来构造HR-LR配对数据，但这些合成降质无法模拟真实低分辨率图像的复杂退化模式，导致模型在真实场景中泛化能力差。作者观察到单张高质量图像中不同深度区域天然存在分辨率差异（远处为LR，近处为HR），可从中提取真实退化LR块，但缺乏配对HR，因此需要无配对SR方法。

Method: 提出LA-SR框架，核心思想是将无配对SR问题重新定义到语言空间中。使用视觉语言模型将图像投影到同时表征内容和质量的语义空间，设计两种语言引导损失：1）语言内容损失（linguistic content loss）保持语义保真度；2）语言质量损失（linguistic quality loss）增强感知真实感。通过这种语言空间对齐，实现真实LR输入的超分辨率重建。

Result: 在真实低分辨率图像上，LA-SR相比基于合成数据训练的SR方法生成更真实、更高质量的HR输出，有效克服了合成数据训练方法的泛化局限。

Conclusion: 利用真实图像中天然存在的分辨率差异提取真实LR块，结合视觉语言模型在语言空间中进行无配对超分辨率，是一种有效的真实场景SR范式，突破了传统合成降质方法的局限。

Classification reason: 该论文研究单图像超分辨率（Super-Resolution），属于图像增强/重建任务，与"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）这一规范主题下的图像修复、增强类工作高度相关。超分辨率可视为一种图像编辑/增强操作，因此复用该规范主题。

Importance reason: Novel unpaired SR approach using real-world LR patches and language-space losses, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: unpaired super-resolution, real-world low-resolution patches, vision-language model loss, language-guided SR, no synthetic degradation

Abstract: Single image super-resolution aims to reconstruct high-resolution (HR) images from low-resolution (LR) inputs. Training SR models typically requires paired HR-LR data, which is difficult to obtain in reality. As a result, most methods synthesize LR images by artificially degrading HR images with handcrafted kernels or camera ISP adjustments. However, these synthetic degradations fail to capture the complexity of real LR images, leading to poor generalization in practice. To address this, we observe that even within a single high-quality image, regions at different depths exhibit varying resolutions, where distant regions act as LR patches and closer ones as HR patches. This allows the extraction of real, degradation-induced LR patches from real images. Since these LR patches lack paired HR counterparts, we propose LA-SR (Language Assistant for SR), a novel framework for unpaired SR. The key idea of LA-SR is to redefine unpaired SR in the language space, using vision-language models to bridge the LR-HR gap. LA-SR projects images into a semantically rich space representing both content and quality, and applies two language-guided losses: linguistic content loss to preserve semantic fidelity, and linguistic quality loss to enhance perceptual realism. With this alignment, LA-SR effectively super-resolves real LR inputs, producing realistic outputs that overcome the limitations of synthetic-data-trained methods.

</details>


### [55] [Histogram-constrained Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31683)
*Haoming Liu, Yuanhe Guo, Yijia Cao, Shenji Wan, Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (73.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出直方图约束图像生成（HIG），将用户指定的分布约束（如颜色直方图或潜token分布）建模为最优传输问题，在扩散采样过程中施加精确的分布级引导，实现介于全局文本提示与局部密集条件之间的中等粒度控制，并展示了在颜色约束生成、潜空间约束生成和高容量信息嵌入等应用中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型控制机制存在粒度鸿沟：文本提示提供全局语义控制但缺乏局部精度，ControlNet等密集条件提供精确局部结构但控制粒度单一。作者希望填补中间粒度的空白，提出一种分布级（distribution-level）的控制机制，在保持全局一致性的同时提供精确的统计约束。

Method: 将用户指定的分布约束（如颜色直方图、潜token分布）建模为最优传输（OT）问题，在扩散采样过程中通过显式引导变换（guidance transformation）驱动扩散轨迹与目标直方图对齐。该方法与现有控制机制完全兼容，可作为插件式模块集成到任意扩散模型中。

Result: 在多种应用场景中验证了HIG的有效性：颜色直方图约束生成可精确匹配目标颜色分布；潜token分布约束可实现风格化控制；基于直方图编码的高容量信息嵌入展示了分布级控制的信息密度优势。实验表明该方法在保持生成质量的同时实现了精确的分布约束。

Conclusion: 分布级控制是一种灵活且可解释的控制方案，填补了全局语义控制与局部密集条件控制之间的粒度空白，与现有控制机制完全兼容，为可控图像生成提供了新的混合策略方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出直方图约束的图像生成（HIG），通过最优传输在扩散采样过程中施加分布级约束（如颜色直方图、潜token分布），实现精确可控的图像生成。这属于可控图像编辑/生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的可控编辑方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Novel distributional control via optimal transport, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: histogram constraint, optimal transport, diffusion sampling, controllable generation, color/latent distribution

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as a dominant paradigm in generative modeling, enabling high-fidelity sampling from complex data distributions. Despite impressive capabilities, controlling diffusion models to produce outputs aligned with user intent remains an open challenge, especially when balancing global coherence with local precision. Existing control mechanisms vary in the granularity of their conditioning signals. For example, textual prompts guide generation globally through high-level semantics, while ControlNet-like approaches secure precise local structure via dense conditions. In this work, we introduce Histogram-constrained Image Generation (HIG), a novel control mechanism that falls into the middle ground of control granularity. Our framework enforces user-specified distributional constraints (e.g., color histograms or latent token distributions) during the generation process with exact precision. We model such control as an optimal transport (OT) problem and apply explicit guidance transformations during sampling, thereby driving the diffusion trajectory to align with the desired histogram. We demonstrate the versatility of HIG across diverse applications, including constrained generation via color/latent histograms and high-capacity information embedding through histogram-level encoding. Our findings underscore the promise of distributional control, a flexible and interpretable control scheme that is fully compatible with existing control mechanisms, diversifying the hybrid strategies for controllable image generation. Our project page is available at: .

</details>

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在视觉真实感和几何一致性上优于现有基线，为可扩展的自动驾驶场景增强提供了鲁棒工具。

Conclusion: DriveWeaver 通过视频修补替代传统 3D 资产插入，有效解决了光照不一致和泛化性差的问题，为自动驾驶仿真中的可控车辆插入提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于点云条件进行视频修补（video inpainting）以在自动驾驶仿真中插入可控车辆，核心任务是视频修补与可控编辑，属于多模态生成方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing），该 subtopic 明确包含 inpainting 和可控编辑。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving simulation and video inpainting, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's configured direction weights. Signals: video inpainting, autonomous driving simulation, point cloud conditioning, 3D Gaussian rendering, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: A pivotal step in autonomous driving simulation involves inserting foreground vehicles with predefined trajectories into simulated scenes. This process enhances scene diversity and facilitates the creation of various corner cases for testing and improving autonomous driving models. However, existing methods often rely on pre-reconstructed 3D assets, which frequently lead to lighting inconsistencies between the inserted foreground and the background. Moreover, the reliance on limited, manually-curated 3D assets hinders large-scale deployment. To address these challenges, we propose DriveWeaver, a novel framework for controllable vehicle insertion in autonomous driving simulation. Specifically, for a masked target insertion area, DriveWeaver performs video inpainting conditioned on vehicle point clouds to generate high-quality, temporally consistent vehicles. This video-inpainting-based approach ensures seamless blending between the foreground and background, while the readily available point cloud conditions enable superior generalization. To support long-term generation, we further design a global-to-local hierarchical inpainting strategy, ensuring the consistent identity and appearance of the inserted vehicles. Meanwhile, we extract explicit 3D Gaussian representations of the inserted vehicles through an urban reconstruction pipeline to enable real-time rendering for autonomous driving simulation. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms existing baselines in visual realism and geometric consistency, providing a robust tool for scalable autonomous driving scene augmentation.

</details>


### [57] [InstanceControl: Controllable Complex Image Generation without Instance Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31924)
*Xiaoyu Liu, Huan Wang, Fan Li, Zhixin Wang, Jiaqi Xu, Ming Liu, Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (79.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: InstanceControl提出了一种无需人工实例标注的多实例可控图像生成方法，利用VLM自动解析文本中的实例描述并预测实例掩码，结合自适应掩码细化策略，在复杂多实例场景中实现了优于现有方法的实例级控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控图像生成方法（如ControlNet）在复杂多实例场景中容易出现属性混淆问题，而近期方法需要人工标注实例信息，成本高昂。作者希望消除对实例标注的依赖，实现自动化的多实例可控生成。

Method: InstanceControl利用VLM自动从文本提示中解析实例描述，并基于视觉条件（如深度图）预测实例掩码，建立文本与视觉区域之间的实例级对应关系。同时引入自适应掩码细化策略，在生成过程中动态优化预测的实例掩码以应对噪声。

Result: 大量实验表明，InstanceControl在保真度和实例级控制精度上均优于现有最先进方法，无需人工标注即可实现精确的多实例可控生成。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过VLM自动建立文本-视觉实例对应关系可以有效解决多实例可控生成中的属性混淆问题，消除了对人工实例标注的需求，为复杂场景下的可控图像生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出InstanceControl，一种无需实例标注的多实例可控图像生成方法，属于可控图像生成/编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的可控编辑方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong controllable generation method but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or world models. Signals: Controllable generation, Multi-instance generation, VLM-based instance parsing, No instance labeling needed, Adaptive mask refinement

Abstract: Controllable image generation methods, such as ControlNet, have demonstrated a remarkable capacity to introduce visual conditions(e.g., depth maps) to guide image generation. However, these methods often struggle with complex multi-instance scenes, frequently leading to attribute confusion among instances. While recent approaches attempt to mitigate this via manual instance labeling, such requirements are labor-intensive. In this paper, we propose InstanceControl, a novel multi-instance controllable generation method that eliminates the need for instance labeling. We identify the primary bottleneck in existing methods as the inability to accurately associate instance descriptions with their corresponding regions within visual conditions. To address this, we leverage the Vision-Language Model (VLM) to establish instance-level correspondences between text prompts and visual conditions. Specifically, the VLM automatically parses instance descriptions from the text prompts and simultaneously predicts instance masks based on the visual conditions. Furthermore, since the predicted masks may contain noise, we introduce an adaptive mask refinement strategy that dynamically refines these instance masks during the generation process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior fidelity and precise instance-level control.

</details>


### [58] [AnyBokeh: Physics-Guided Any-to-Any Bokeh Editing with Optical Fingerprint Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31959)
*Xinyu Hou, Xiaoming Li, Zongsheng Yue, Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AnyBokeh提出了一种物理引导的任意到任意bokeh编辑框架，通过估计源图像的符号弥散圆图和视差图，提取光学指纹并迁移到目标光圈/焦点设置，实现无需全聚焦重建的灵活bokeh编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有bokeh编辑方法通常假设输入为全聚焦图像，或先恢复全聚焦图像再渲染新bokeh，这会丢弃源图像中的有用模糊线索并传播重建伪影。实际摄影中，用户可能在任何光圈和焦点设置下拍摄，需要一种能够处理任意输入到任意输出的bokeh编辑方法。

Method: AnyBokeh首先估计源图像的符号弥散圆（CoC）图和视差图，利用符号CoC与视差差值的线性关系提取源图像特有的光学指纹。然后将源光学特征迁移到目标光圈和焦点设置下，生成目标CoC图。最后，一个以源和目标CoC图为条件的生成编辑器执行相对模糊合成，实现空间自适应的去模糊、保留和散焦渲染。同时构建了一个高保真合成数据集用于物理监督学习。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，AnyBokeh在任意到任意bokeh编辑、全聚焦到bokeh渲染和散焦去模糊任务中均实现了忠实且可控的编辑效果，避免了现有方法常见的全聚焦重建和测试时bokeh级校准需求。

Conclusion: AnyBokeh通过物理引导的光学指纹迁移和相对模糊合成，实现了灵活、可控的任意到任意bokeh编辑，为后捕获景深控制提供了一种更实用的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究单图像景深控制与bokeh编辑（虚化效果编辑），属于图像编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel physics-guided bokeh editing framework, but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords. Signals: physics-guided, bokeh editing, optical fingerprint, any-to-any, generative editor

Abstract: Depth-of-field control is a fundamental tool in photography, yet post-capture bokeh editing from a single image remains challenging. A practical editor should handle images captured under arbitrary focus and aperture settings. Existing methods typically assume an all-in-focus input, or first recover an all-in-focus image before rendering new bokeh. Such pipelines can discard useful blur cues from the source image and propagate reconstruction artifacts into the final edit. We introduce AnyBokeh, a physics-guided framework for any-to-any bokeh editing. Instead of treating source blur merely as a degradation to be removed, AnyBokeh estimates the source blur state with a signed circle-of-confusion map and a disparity map. By modeling the linear relation between signed circle of confusion and disparity difference, AnyBokeh estimates a source-specific optical fingerprint and transfers the source optical characteristics to the desired focus and aperture setting. A generative editor conditioned on both source and target circle-of-confusion maps then performs relative blur synthesis, enabling spatially adaptive deblurring, preservation, and defocus rendering. To support physically supervised learning, we further construct a high-fidelity synthetic dataset with accurate depth, focus distance, and full EXIF metadata. Experiments on real-world benchmarks show that AnyBokeh achieves faithful and controllable editing across any-to-any bokeh editing, all-in-focus-to-bokeh rendering, and defocus deblurring, while avoiding all-in-focus reconstruction and test-time bokeh-level calibration commonly required by existing approaches. The code and dataset will be available at .

</details>

TL;DR: 本文将本征分解（将图像颜色分解为漫反射albedo和shading）扩展到3D高斯泼溅辐射场，通过独立的高斯基元集建模各层、数据驱动预测引导优化实现多视图照片的分解，并支持用户通过编辑单张图像的albedo来修改平面纹理，在任意视角下以合理光照重渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征分解在图像编辑中历史悠久，允许修改物体颜色和纹理而不改变光照。然而，现有方法尚未将这一分解扩展到3D高斯泼溅辐射场，限制了3D场景中独立编辑纹理与光照的能力。

Method: 1) 将本征分解建模为独立的高斯基元集（albedo集、shading集、残差集），每集自适应各自层的特性；2) 利用数据驱动预测（预训练模型提供先验）引导优化过程，从多视角照片中解耦出上述本征层；3) 提供编辑工作流：用户修改单张图像中平面表面的albedo，编辑被捕获到本征辐射场中，实现任意视角下光照合理的重渲染。

Result: 在多个真实场景上展示了纹理编辑效果，能够修改物体颜色/纹理而保持光照不变，并在任意新视角下生成光照一致的渲染结果。

Conclusion: 本文成功将本征分解引入3D高斯泼溅，实现了可编辑的辐射场表示，为3D场景的纹理编辑提供了灵活且光照一致的工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出对3D高斯泼溅辐射场进行本征分解（albedo/shading分离），并基于此实现纹理编辑（修改物体颜色/纹理而不改变光照）。这属于3D场景的可控编辑，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）高度相关，因为该subtopic涵盖可控编辑、风格化等，且3DGS编辑是其自然延伸。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D Gaussian splatting intrinsic decomposition for editing, which is not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian splatting, intrinsic decomposition, image editing, not autoregressive, not continuous language model

Abstract: Intrinsic decomposition which expresses image colors as the product of diffuse albedo and shading, possibly augmented with view-dependent residuals has a long history in image editing as it enables the modification of object colors and textures without altering lighting. We extend intrinsic decomposition to radiance fields represented with Gaussian splatting by proposing solutions to three key aspects of such decomposition. First, we describe how to model the intrinsic decomposition as independent sets of Gaussian primitives, which allows each set to adapt to the characteristics of the layer it represents. Second, we present an optimization procedure guided by data-driven predictions to disentangle multi-view photographs of a scene into the aforementioned intrinsic sets. Finally, we provide an editing workflow where users modify the texture of planar surfaces simply by modifying the albedo of that surface in one image. Capturing this edit within the intrinsic radiance field allows re-rendering of the edited scene with plausible lighting under arbitrary viewpoints.

</details>

### [60] [SyncCache: Exploiting Asymmetric Dynamics for Fast Audio-Driven Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30849)
*Juncheng Ma, Yuxuan Du, Yanan Sun, Zhening Xing, Changlin Li, Zhenyu Tang, Bo Li, Peng-Tao Jiang, Li Yuan, Daquan Zhou, Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.CV, eess.AS

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 值得关注 (67.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SyncCache 是一种针对 DiT 音频驱动肖像动画的无训练缓存加速方法，通过空间非对称探测和模态解耦缓存利用动态区域与静态背景的不对称性，实现最高 4.12x 加速且保持视觉保真度和唇音同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT 在音频驱动肖像动画中计算成本极高，推理延迟大。现有无训练缓存加速方法主要针对文生图任务，忽略了音频驱动肖像动画中空间（人脸区域 vs 背景）和模态（音频条件 vs 视觉）的不对称动态特性。

Method: 提出 SyncCache，包含三个核心组件：1) 空间非对称探测（Spatially-Asymmetric Probing）：优先关注动态人脸区域的误差敏感性；2) 模态解耦缓存（Modality-Decoupled Caching）：跳过重 DiT 块复用稳定的块间残差，同时持续计算轻量音频块以保持唇音同步；3) 缓存比率控制与离线动态规划实现内存自适应缓存选择。

Result: 在 HunyuanVideo-Avatar 上实现 4.12x 加速，在 Wan-S2V 上实现 3.75x 加速，视觉保真度接近无损，音频对齐精确。

Conclusion: SyncCache 有效利用音频驱动肖像动画中的不对称动态特性，在加速推理的同时保持高质量输出，为 DiT 模型的高效部署提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究音频驱动的肖像动画（audio-driven portrait animation），属于数字人/说话头/面部重演范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "数字人与动作生成" (avatar_motion_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on caching acceleration for audio-driven portrait animation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks direct alignment with continuous language/multimodal models or video generation world models. Signals: Training-free caching, DiT acceleration, audio-driven portrait animation, asymmetric dynamics, spatial and modality decoupling

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have significantly advanced audio-driven portrait animation, but their high computational cost leads to substantial inference latency. Although training-free diffusion caching accelerates inference significant, existing methods are primarily developed for text-conditioned generation and overlook the spatial and modality imbalances inherent in audio-driven portrait animation. In this paper, we propose SyncCache, a training-free caching acceleration method tailored for DiT-based portrait animation that explicitly exploits asymmetric dynamics. Specifically, high-frequency dynamics driven by audio conditions and concentrated in human regions are more challenging and critical to cache and reuse than the low-frequency visual background in portrait animation. First, we introduce Spatially-Asymmetric Probing to prioritize error sensitivity in dynamic human region. Second, through Modality-Decoupled Caching, we bypass heavy DiT block by reusing stable inter-block residuals, while continuously recomputing lightweight audio blocks to preserve precise lip synchronization. Furthermore, we introduce a cache ratio to control cache capacity and formulate memory-adaptive cache selection as an offline dynamic programming problem without online overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SyncCache achieves superior speed-quality trade-offs, delivering up to 4.12x acceleration on HunyuanVideo-Avatar and 3.75x on Wan-S2V with near-lossless visual fidelity and precise audio alignment.

</details>


### [61] [Towards Flexible, Natural, Efficient Interaction for Conversational Talking Face Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31088)
*Baiqin Wang, Sen Chen, Jiankuo Zhao, Xiangyu Liu, Zhen Lei, Xiangyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 InterTalk，一个面向多轮对话式说话人脸生成的实时交互框架。通过基于运动的架构、显式建模多轮对话动态、迭代生成策略以及面部组件解耦（唇同步、眨眼等），在保持 30 FPS 实时性能的同时实现了灵活、自然、高效的多人对话交互。还构建了多人物对话数据集并采用 3D 人脸数据增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式说话人脸生成方法在灵活性（支持任意数量参与者的多轮对话）、自然性（连贯运动与非语言反馈）和效率（实时生成与低计算开销）三者之间难以平衡，缺乏一个同时满足所有要求的统一框架。

Method: InterTalk 基于运动架构，显式建模多轮对话动态，支持任意数量的参与者；引入多参与者运动反馈和迭代生成策略以增强交互自然性；将运动解耦为多个面部组件（如嘴唇、眼睛），实现针对性优化（精确唇同步、真实眨眼）；构建多人物对话数据集并采用 3D 人脸数据增强。

Result: 大量实验表明 InterTalk 在交互质量上优于现有方法，同时保持 30 FPS 的实时生成性能。

Conclusion: InterTalk 成功实现了灵活、自然、高效的对话式说话人脸生成，为多轮多人交互场景提供了可行的解决方案，在实时性和生成质量之间取得了良好平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文研究对话式说话人脸生成（conversational talking face generation），属于数字人/说话头/肖像动画范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "数字人与动作生成" (avatar_motion_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on conversational talking face generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's configured direction weights. Signals: real-time 30 FPS, motion-based architecture, multi-round dialogue, 3D face data augmentation, iterative generation strategy

Abstract: Conversational talking face generation has recently attracted increasing attention, aiming to synthesize interactive talking videos where characters speak, listen, and respond dynamically to each other. This task presents three core challenges: 1) Flexibility: enabling multi-round dialogues with an arbitrary number of participants; 2) Naturalness: maintaining coherent motion and appropriate non-verbal feedback throughout the interaction; and 3) Efficiency: achieving real-time generation and low computation overhead for long-term continuous online conversation. Despite recent advances, existing methods still fall short in balancing all three requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce InterTalk, a novel and efficient framework designed for highly interactive conversational talking face generation. Built upon a motion-based architecture, InterTalk supports real-time conversation synthesis. Our method achieves strong flexibility by explicitly modeling multi-round conversational dynamics among each participant, eliminating constraints on their numbers. To enhance interactivity, we incorporate motion feedback from multiple participants and introduce an iterative generation strategy for more natural behaviors. Besides, we disentangle motion into several facial components, enabling targeted refinements for natural response such as precise lip sync and realistic eye blinking. Finally, we construct a new multi-person conversational dataset and enrich it with 3D face-based data augmentation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that InterTalk achieves superior interaction quality while maintaining real-time performance at 30 FPS.

</details>


### [62] [JacobianAvatar: Temporally Consistent Semi-rigid Avatar Reconstruction from a Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31115)
*Changyeon Won, Min-Gyu Park, Seonghwan Park, Ju Hong Yoon, Hae-Gon Jeon*

TL;DR: JacobianAvatar 利用神经 Jacobian 场（NJF）表示半刚性形变，通过自监督学习预测 Jacobian 矩阵并求解泊松方程，从单目视频中重建时序一致、几何连贯的人体虚拟人。引入约束泊松求解器、符号距离 Jacobian 正则化和形变引导残差光流损失，有效处理自遮挡和不可见区域，在基准和野外视频上超越 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频中重建具有复杂运动（如服装动力学）的真实人体虚拟人需要建模全局和局部形变，但现有方法在自遮挡区域和不可见表面处理上存在困难，难以保持时序一致性。

Method: 基于神经 Jacobian 场（NJF）表示半刚性形变，训练自监督神经网络预测与姿态相关的 Jacobian 矩阵，通过求解泊松方程得到形变。提出三个关键组件：约束泊松求解器（抑制边界伪影）、符号距离 Jacobian 正则化（恢复频繁遮挡区域如腋窝和大腿）、形变引导残差光流损失（强制时序一致性）。

Result: 在基准数据集和野外视频上的实验表明，该方法生成的虚拟人具有时序稳定性和几何连贯性，在定量和定性指标上均优于现有 SOTA 方法。

Conclusion: 神经 Jacobian 场结合所提出的三个关键组件能有效解决单目视频中人体虚拟人重建的自遮挡和时序一致性问题，为复杂运动下的虚拟人重建提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文从单目视频重建具有时序一致性的半刚性人体虚拟人（avatar），涉及人体动作、服装形变与几何重建，属于数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on avatar reconstruction from monocular video, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: monocular avatar reconstruction, neural Jacobian field, semi-rigid deformation, temporal consistency, self-occlusion handling

Abstract: Generating realistic human avatars in complex motions--such as clothing dynamics--requires modeling of global and local deformations which remains challenging in monocular settings. We address this problem by leveraging neural Jacobian fields (NJFs) for representing semi-rigid deformations. We train self-supervised neural networks for predicting Jacobian matrices that give the pose-dependent deformations, by solving a Poisson equation. However, monocular input presents several difficulties such as self-occluded regions and invisible surfaces. To address these issues, we introduce three key components: a constrained Poisson solver, signed distance-based Jacobian regularization, and a deformation-guided residual flow loss, which together suppress boundary artifacts, recover frequently occluded regions such as armpits and thighs, and enforce temporal consistency during motion. Experiments on benchmark and in-the-wild videos demonstrate that our method generates temporally stable and geometrically coherent avatars, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>

### [63] [Quality-Aware Modulation for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30934)

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

TL;DR: 本文提出 Quality Representation Module (QRM)，一个轻量级 Transformer 模块，通过学习质量感知表示来调整 DiT 中的 adaptive LayerNorm 调制，从而在不改变采样调度或扩散主干的情况下提升生成图像的质量、对齐度和视觉一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DiT 模型仅依赖 timestep 或 prompt embedding 来调制去噪强度，缺乏质量感知信息，导致生成图像存在不对齐、视觉不一致和保真度不足的问题。

Method: 提出 Quality Representation Module (QRM)，一个轻量级 Transformer 模块，基于现有模型输入学习质量感知表示，生成一组向量 M_qrm，用于调整 DiT 块内的 adaptive LayerNorm 调制，从而将质量敏感信号注入去噪参数。

Result: 消融实验验证了 QRM 训练损失和架构设计的有效性，实证结果表明在基于 DiT 的模型上持续提升了图像质量。

Conclusion: QRM 是一种简单有效的即插即用模块，通过注入质量感知信息显著改善 DiT 生成图像的质量，且无需改变采样过程或扩散主干。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Quality Representation Module (QRM) 改进 Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 的调制机制，属于文本到图像扩散模型的基础方法改进，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"文生图与图像扩散"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a quality-aware modulation module for DiT, but focuses on image generation, not the user's high-priority continuous multimodal or video generation topics. Signals: Diffusion Transformer, quality-aware modulation, image generation, lightweight module, adaptive LayerNorm

Abstract: Modern text-to-image diffusion models, such as diffusion transformers (DiT), rely on timestep or prompt embeddings to modulate the strength of the denoising process in each timestep. While this modulation communicates the current noise level, it does not provide any quality-aware information, which can lead to generated images that are unaligned, visually inconsistent, and lacking in fidelity. In this paper, we propose the Quality Representation Module (QRM), a lightweight transformer module that learns a quality-aware representation based on existing model inputs, and produces a set of vectors $M_{qrm}$. These vectors adjust the adaptive LayerNorm modulation within the DiT transformer blocks, thereby injecting a quality-sensitive signal into the denoising parameters. The QRM introduces no significant changes to the sampling schedule or diffusion backbone. Experiments include ablations on QRM training losses and architectures, as well as empirical results demonstrating consistent image quality improvements over baseline DiT-based models.

</details>

Main category: cs.CV
