TL;DR: TerraDiT-Ω 提出统一空间控制框架，支持从任意地理空间基元（多边形、折线、边界框、点）直接生成卫星图像，通过几何感知局部注意力机制将几何信息注入扩散 Transformer 的注意力空间，在多种条件格式下均优于密集控制和稀疏控制基线，并可用于可控数据增强提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星图像生成面临独特挑战：卫星场景由空间复杂且语义不同的几何结构组成。现有方法要么使用密集栅格（如分割图）需要昂贵标注，要么使用稀疏提示（如文本）丢失几何保真度，且与地理信息系统中常用的矢量基元不兼容。需要一种统一框架，能直接利用各种原生地理空间基元进行可控卫星图像生成。

Method: 提出 TerraDiT-Ω 框架，基于扩散 Transformer (DiT) 架构。核心创新是 Geometry-Aware Local Attention (GALA)，一种将显式几何线索注入注意力空间的条件机制。GALA 将空间条件（多边形、折线、边界框、点等基元）编码为几何感知的注意力偏置，使模型在生成过程中能感知空间结构。该框架统一支持多种地理空间基元，从精确的多边形/折线到粗略的边界框/点，适应不同标注预算。

Result: 在所有条件格式下，TerraDiT-Ω 在 FID、KID 等指标上一致优于密集控制（如 ControlNet）和稀疏控制（如文本提示）基线。该框架还可用于可控合成数据增强，在土地覆盖分割、目标检测、道路图提取和场景分类等下游任务中显著提升性能。

Conclusion: TerraDiT-Ω 证明了统一空间控制框架在卫星图像生成中的有效性，能够兼容多种地理空间基元并保持高质量生成。该方法不仅适用于城市规划等设计任务，还能与端到端 GeoAI 工作流自然兼容，为遥感领域的可控生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TerraDiT-Ω，一个基于扩散 Transformer (DiT) 的卫星图像生成框架，属于文本/条件到图像生成方向，与 [CANONICAL] 文生图与图像扩散 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong technical contribution in satellite image synthesis but low alignment with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: Satellite image synthesis, Unified spatial control, Diffusion Transformer, Geometry-Aware Local Attention, Controllable data augmentation

Abstract: Generative models have achieved remarkable progress, yet applying them to satellite imagery remains challenging. Unlike natural imagery, satellite scenes are structured by spatially complex and semantically distinct geometries. Prior work addresses this complexity by adapting natural image frameworks using dense rasters or sparse prompts, trading off annotation cost and fidelity while breaking compatibility with vector primitives commonly used to represent geographic information. We introduce TerraDiT-$\Omega$, a unified spatial control framework that generates satellite imagery directly from any native geospatial primitive. By jointly leveraging precise annotations (polygons, polylines) and coarser ones (bounding boxes, points), the model supports controllable layouts across varying annotation budgets, broadening applicability to design tasks such as urban planning while remaining naturally compatible with end-to-end GeoAI workflows. To effectively leverage these primitives during generation, we propose Geometry-Aware Local Attention, a conditioning mechanism that injects explicit geometric cues into the attention space. Across all conditioning formats, our approach consistently outperforms both dense-control and sparse-control baselines. Furthermore, this flexibility enables controllable synthetic data augmentation using a single generative model, improving downstream performance on land-cover segmentation, object detection, road graph extraction, and scene classification. Code, data, and weights are available at .

</details>


### [65] [WaterGen: Decoupling Scene and Medium in Underwater Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31147)
*Jiayi Wu, Tianfu Wang, Tianyi Xiong, Dehao Yuan, Xiaomin Lin, Md Jahidul Islam, Cornelia Fermuller, Christopher Metzler, Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (68.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WaterGen 提出了一种将场景内容与水介质效果解耦的水下图像生成方法，利用潜扩散模型先生成无退化的水下场景潜编码，再通过物理建模的条件解码过程施加水介质效果，实现了大规模、多样、真实且可控的水下图像生成，并验证了生成数据对水下恢复和分割任务的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下计算机视觉任务（检测、恢复、分割等）受限于大规模多样化训练数据的匮乏。现有水下图像生成方法要么可控但缺乏真实性和多样性，要么真实但无法独立控制水介质效果。因此需要一种既能生成多样真实场景、又能精确可控地模拟水介质效果的方法。

Method: 将水下图像合成解耦为两个阶段：(1) 使用无退化的水下图像微调潜扩散 U-Net，使其学习生成多样且真实的无退化水下场景潜编码；(2) 将物理精确的水介质退化合成建模为条件解码过程，作用于这些潜编码上。这种解耦设计使得模型既能生成多样场景，又能完全控制水下外观效果。

Result: WaterGen 能够生成大规模、场景结构多样、水介质效果精确的水下合成数据集。在下游任务（水下图像恢复和语义分割）中，使用 WaterGen 生成的数据训练能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 场景生成与水介质建模可以在潜扩散框架内解耦，从而实现多样场景生成与精确可控水下外观的兼顾。该方法为水下视觉任务提供了有效的数据增强手段。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 WaterGen，基于潜扩散框架（latent diffusion）进行水下图像生成，属于图像生成方法。虽然任务特定（水下），但核心方法是 latent diffusion 框架下的条件图像生成，与 [CANONICAL] 文生图与图像扩散 主题高度相关。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on underwater image generation, which is a niche application not aligned with the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Underwater image generation, Latent diffusion, Scene-medium decoupling, Downstream task improvement, Not autoregressive

Abstract: Underwater computer vision tasks, such as detection, restoration, and segmentation, are limited by the scarcity of large-scale and diverse training data. We introduce WaterGen, a method for generating large-scale, realistic, and diverse underwater images that provides independent control of the scene and water medium conditions. Our approach treats underwater image generation as the decoupled control of two factors: realistic and diverse scene content (what is in the image), and accurate and controllable water medium effects (what the water does to the image). Existing methods generally achieve only part of this objective: they either provide controllability with limited realism or diversity, or generate realistic scenes without accurately and independently modeling water-medium effects. Our key insight, that allows us to avoid this compromise, is that scene generation and medium modeling can be decoupled within a latent diffusion framework, enabling diverse scene generation together with accurate and controllable underwater appearance. To do this, we decompose underwater image synthesis into two stages. First, we fine-tune the latent diffusion U-Net using degradation-free underwater images so that it learns to generate diverse and realistic latent embeddings of underwater scene content without medium-induced degradation. Second, we formulate the physically accurate medium degradation synthesis as a conditional decoding process applied to these latent embeddings. This decoupled design allows our model to generate diverse scenes with full control of underwater appearance. We leverage WaterGen to build large-scale synthetic underwater datasets that are diverse in scene structures and accurate in water effects and pseudo-labels. We demonstrate that our synthetic data consistently improve downstream performance in underwater restoration and semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [66] [AC3S: Adaptive Conditioning for 3D-Aware Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31204)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的合成数据生成方法使用边缘图等视觉提示引导生成，但容易出现过条件化（over-conditioning）问题，导致图像真实感下降、多样性受限，难以同时保证3D结构对齐和图像质量。

Method: 提出 AC3S 框架：(1) 自监督视觉提示调制器（self-supervised visual prompt modulator），动态调整 ControlNet 条件强度，防止过条件化；(2) 多智能体 VLM 框架，自动生成与3D几何结构对齐的详细语义提示，增强多样性和语义一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，AC3S 显著提升了生成图像的质量和下游任务效用，有效缓解了过条件化伪影，同时保持了精确的3D结构对齐。

Conclusion: AC3S 通过自适应条件控制策略，在3D结构对齐与图像真实感之间取得了更好的平衡，为可扩展的高质量合成数据生成提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于扩散模型的3D感知合成数据生成框架，核心是改进ControlNet条件控制以避免过条件化，属于文生图/图像扩散方向下的可控生成技术，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与图像扩散"。

Importance reason: Novel adaptive conditioning for 3D-aware synthetic data, but not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: diffusion-based image generation, ControlNet over-conditioning, self-supervised visual prompt modulator, multi-agent VLM for 3D-aware prompts, synthetic data with 3D annotations

Abstract: Synthetic data generation has emerged as a powerful tool for improving data scalability in computer vision. Recent diffusion-based pipelines have demonstrated strong photorealism. However, how to enforce precise 3D structure and pose consistency in generated images remains challenging. Existing methods leverage visual prompts such as edge maps to guide diffusion models, but often suffer from over-conditioning artifacts that degrade image realism and limit dataset quality. In this paper, we present a diffusion-based image generation framework that enforces 3D structural alignment while preserving photorealism through adaptive conditioning. Our framework, Adaptive Conditioning for 3D-Aware Synthetic Data Generation (AC3S), introduces a self-supervised visual prompt modulator that dynamically adjusts the strength of ControlNet conditioning, preventing over-conditioning and enabling the diffusion model to retain its generative expressiveness. To further enhance diversity and semantic consistency, we develop a multi-agent vision language model framework that composes detailed and 3D-aware prompts aligned with the underlying geometric structure. Together, these components enable the scalable generation of high-quality synthetic datasets with accurate 2D and 3D annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly improves image quality and downstream utility.

</details>


### [67] [SpheRoPE: Zero-Shot Optimization-Free 360 Panorama Generation with Spherical RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32033)
*Or Hirschorn, Aaron Olender, Eli Alshan, Ianir Ideses, Lior Fritz, Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文提出SpheRoPE，一种零样本、免训练、免优化的360°全景图像与视频生成框架。通过将标准RoPE替换为球面位置编码（Spherical RoPE）并引入语义失真CFG，在预训练扩散Transformer（Flux.1, Flux.2, LTX-Video）中直接注入球面先验，无需微调或优化即可生成高质量全景内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有360°全景生成方法要么依赖稀缺全景数据的昂贵微调（限制泛化性），要么使用多步优化导致推理延迟过高。作者观察到预训练生成模型已具备一定的全景先验，但不足以满足等距柱状投影（ERP）的严格拓扑约束，因此需要一种零样本、免训练的方法来在推理时解决这些约束。

Method: 1) Spherical RoPE：将标准RoPE的低频通道重参数化为3D笛卡尔坐标以编码球面流形，高频通道进行谐波量化以强制执行精确周期性。2) 语义失真CFG：显式引导几何结构，避免重训练并继承预训练模型的全部创作广度。该方法适用于多种骨干网络（Flux.1, Flux.2, LTX-Video）和多种360°生成模态。

Result: 在文本到全景图生成任务上，使用Flux.1、Flux.2和LTX-Video骨干网络，达到了与基线方法竞争的性能，同时保持免训练特性。实验表明该方法能泛化到不同的骨干网络和360°生成模态。

Conclusion: SpheRoPE通过简单的推理时位置编码替换和CFG引导，无需任何训练或优化即可实现高质量的360°全景生成，为全景内容创作提供了一种高效、通用的零样本解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出零样本、免训练、免优化的360°全景图像/视频生成方法，基于预训练扩散Transformer（Flux.1, Flux.2, LTX-Video）注入球面先验。核心属于文生图/视频扩散模型范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与图像扩散"（text_to_image_diffusion）高度匹配，同时涉及视频生成，但主要贡献在图像扩散模型的改进上。

Importance reason: Novel zero-shot spherical position encoding for 360° generation, but tangential to user's core continuous language/multimodal and autoregressive video generation priorities. Signals: zero-shot panorama generation, spherical position encoding, diffusion transformer, training-free, 360 video generation

Abstract: We present a zero-shot, training-free and optimization-free framework for generating 360 panoramic images and videos by directly injecting spherical priors into pre-trained diffusion transformers. Existing methods either rely on costly fine-tuning on scarce panoramic data that limits generalization, or leverage multi-step optimization that incurs prohibitive inference latency. We observe that contemporary generative models natively exhibit some panoramic priors from large-scale training. However, these emergent capabilities are insufficient, as the models fundamentally fail to satisfy the rigorous topological constraints imposed by equirectangular projection (ERP). We introduce a zero-shot and optimization-free approach that resolves these constraints at inference time. Spherical RoPE replaces standard rotary position embeddings: low-frequency channels are re-parameterized as 3D Cartesian coordinates to natively encode the spherical manifold, while high-frequency channels are harmonically quantized to enforce exact periodicity. Coupled with complementary Semantic Distortion classifier-free guidance (CFG) that explicitly steers geometry, we avoid retraining and inherit the full creative breadth of state-of-the-art models. Our approach generalizes across diverse backbones and 360 generation modalities. We demonstrate this across text-to-panorama using Flux.1, Flux.2, and LTX-Video backbones, achieving competitive performance against baselines, all while remaining training-free. Project page:

</details>

### [68] [InfiniVerse: Occupancy Guided Unbounded Scene Generation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31109)
*Xiaoyu Ye, Leheng Li, Xinyu Ji, Yingjie Cai, Hongda He, Xu Yan, Guanyi Zhao, Ying-Cong Chen, Bingbing Liu, Shuguang Cui, Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

TL;DR: InfiniVerse 提出一个统一管线，从单帧多视图输入重建3D占据表示，沿任意轨迹自回归扩展场景，再通过视频扩散模型生成时空一致的动态城市场景视频，并引入分层草图-精炼范式实现2D-3D跨模态对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中生成真实、可控且时序连贯的城市场景是一个关键但未解决的挑战。现有方法在长距离场景生成、2D-3D对齐和可控性方面存在不足。

Method: 首先从输入的多视图帧重建3D占据表示；然后基于该表示沿任意轨迹自回归扩展场景；接着使用视频扩散模型将粗占据网格转化为时空一致的逼真视频序列；最后提出分层草图-精炼范式，将生成视频反投影为图像条件反馈以增强3D占据表示，实现视觉与空间域的跨模态对齐与相互增强。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset和nuScenes上达到SOTA，FID为6.4，FVD为67.97，在生成时长和稳定性上显著超越现有基准。

Conclusion: InfiniVerse通过3D占据引导与视频扩散结合的方式，实现了长距离、2D-3D对齐、可控的动态城市场景生成，为自动驾驶场景生成提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文使用视频扩散模型生成动态城市场景视频，属于视频生成方法，映射到 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题。

Importance reason: Directly advances controllable video generation and world model for autonomous driving, aligning with high-priority video generation and world model subtopics. Signals: autoregressive scene extension, video diffusion model, 3D occupancy representation, controllable generation, world model for driving

Abstract: Generating realistic, controllable, and temporally coherent urban environments is a critical yet unresolved challenge in the autonomous driving community. In this paper, we introduce InfiniVerse, a unified pipeline for long-range, 2D-3D-aligned, and controllable synthesis of dynamic urban scenes from a single frame. In practice, our approach first reconstructs a 3D occupancy representation from the input multi-view frame. This representation serves as a foundation for autoregressive scene extension along arbitrary trajectories. Subsequently, a video diffusion model translates the coarse occupancy grid into realistic, spatiotemporally consistent video sequences. Moreover, we propose a hierarchical sketch-and-refine paradigm, in which the generated videos are re-projected as image-conditioned feedback to enhance the 3D occupancy representation, establishing cross-modal alignment and mutual enhancement between the visual and spatial domains. Extensive evaluations on the Waymo Open Dataset and nuScenes demonstrate that InfiniVerse achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a FID of 6.4 and FVD of 67.97, significantly outperforming existing benchmarks in both duration and stability.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文从变分视角出发，对生成式机器学习中的随机微分方程（SDE）进行了自包含的导论性介绍，推导了ELBO，并将扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配统一为变分方法的不同参数化形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配等方法在图像、视频和生物分子生成中取得了显著成功，但这些方法通常从不同视角被介绍，缺乏统一的数学框架。作者希望提供一个自包含的导论，从变分下界（ELBO）的统一视角来理解和比较这些方法。

Method: 论文从随机微分方程（SDE）和常微分方程（ODE）的基本概念出发，介绍了生成建模中的概率框架和Fokker-Planck方程。推导了变分下界（ELBO），并将其作为统一起点，展示了扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配可以视为该变分框架的不同参数化形式。通过一维密度建模的简单示例比较了不同参数化的效果。

Result: 论文提供了一个统一的变分视角，将扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配联系起来，并通过一维示例直观展示了不同参数化方法的异同。

Conclusion: 扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配都可以从变分下界（ELBO）的统一框架中推导出来，它们本质上是同一变分方法的不同参数化选择。该统一视角有助于更深入地理解这些生成建模方法之间的关系和差异。

Classification reason: 该论文是扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配的综述性导论，从变分下界（ELBO）的统一视角推导和比较这些生成建模方法。这些方法属于生成模型的核心基础，与"生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）中的扩散加速、少步采样等主题密切相关，且该 canonical 子主题涵盖生成模型方法论本身。

Importance reason: Tutorial unifying diffusion/flow matching from variational perspective, but lacks novel results or direct application to user's priority subtopics. Signals: tutorial, variational perspective, diffusion models, flow matching, score matching

Abstract: The use of ordinary and stochastic differential equations has led to substantial progress in generative machine learning with applications to, for example, image, video and biomolecule generation. This paper provides a self-contained and informal introduction to the differential equations, the probabilistic framework for using them in generative modeling and the Fokker--Planck equation that governs the temporal evolution of the marginal distribution of the stochastic variables of the differential equations. The variational lower bound on the log-likelihood (the evidence lower bound, ELBO) is derived and used as a general starting point for a discussion of diffusion models, score matching, and flow matching. All of these approaches may be viewed as specific parameterizations of the most general variational approach. A one-dimensional density modeling problem is used as a simple example to compare different parameterizations.

</details>


### [70] [Accelerated Likelihood Maximization for Diffusion-based Versatile Content Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31323)
*Hyunsoo Lee, Inwoo Hwang, Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

TL;DR: 本文提出加速似然最大化（ALM），一种无需训练的扩散模型采样策略，通过直接优化未观测区域并结合加速技术，实现全局一致且高效的多领域内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在部分给定输入下生成多样、连贯且合理的内容时存在局限：基于训练的方法任务特定但计算昂贵且泛化差；无需训练的方法虽高效但未显式优化未观测变量，导致全局不一致。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出Accelerated Likelihood Maximization (ALM)，在反向扩散过程中直接优化未观测区域的似然，而非通过预生成区域约束间接影响缺失区域。同时引入加速策略，在不牺牲性能的前提下显著提升计算效率。

Result: 在多种数据域和任务上，ALM一致优于现有最先进方法，展示了其在多样化内容生成中的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: ALM为扩散模型提供了一种强大且通用的内容生成范式，兼具无需训练、全局一致和计算高效的优势，可广泛应用于多种生成任务。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的加速采样策略（ALM），用于扩散模型的高效内容生成，核心贡献在于加速反向扩散过程并提升计算效率，属于生成模型加速与高效化方向。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference and acceleration for diffusion models, a high-priority subtopic, with a training-free method that boosts global consistency. Signals: training-free acceleration, diffusion model sampling, global consistency, versatile content generation, efficient inference

Abstract: Generating diverse, coherent, and plausible content from partially given inputs remains a fundamental challenge for diffusion models. Existing approaches face clear limitations: training-based approaches offer strong task-specific results but require costly computation, and they generalize poorly across tasks. Training-free approaches offer better efficiency, but they do not explicitly optimize over unobserved variables, leading to globally inconsistent results. To address these limitations, we introduce Accelerated Likelihood Maximization (ALM), a novel training-free sampling strategy integrated into the reverse diffusion process that significantly extends the applicability of diffusion models beyond simple generation tasks. Unlike previous methods that implicitly influence missing regions through pre-generated region constraints, we directly optimize the unobserved region during the sampling process, enabling globally coherent and plausible generation. Furthermore, we incorporate an acceleration strategy that significantly improves computational efficiency without sacrificing performance. Experimental results demonstrate that ALM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in various data domains and tasks, establishing a powerful paradigm for versatile content generation.

</details>


### [71] [Cross-Space Distillation: Teaching One-Step Students with Modern Diffusion Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32020)
*Anh Nguyen, Ngan Nguyen, Duc Vu, Trung Dao, Viet Nguyen, Quan Dao, Kien Nguyen, Chi Tran, Phong Nguyen, Khoi Nguyen, Cuong Pham, Dimitris Metaxas, Vishal M. Patel, Anh Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (63.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出跨空间蒸馏（Cross-Space Distillation）范式，解决教师与学生模型潜空间不匹配（不同VAE分辨率与参数化）下的知识蒸馏问题。通过轻量级Bridge模块（冻结学生VAE解码器+可学习投影器）将学生潜变量映射到教师空间，实现从SD 3.5/Flux等大模型到SD 1.5等紧凑模型的单步蒸馏，显著提升生成质量（HPSv3从5.4提升至9.4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于时间步蒸馏的单步扩散模型假设教师和学生共享相同的潜空间（Shared-Space），这限制了从现代高容量教师模型（如SD 3.5、Flux）向紧凑、可部署的学生模型（如SD 1.5）的知识迁移，因为它们的潜空间分辨率和VAE参数化不同。

Method: 提出Cross-Space Distillation框架，核心是Bridge模块：冻结学生VAE解码器作为空间先验，结合轻量级可学习投影器，将学生潜变量映射到教师潜空间。训练时使用潜空间重建损失和注意力保真度损失来稳定对齐。蒸馏过程采用基于分布的timestep蒸馏方法。

Result: 在多个现代教师模型上验证，Bridge使紧凑单步学生模型获得显著提升：SD 1.5的HPSv3从5.4提升至9.4，同时保持单步推理、低延迟和广泛的生态兼容性。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级潜空间接口，异构的大容量教师模型可以被蒸馏到高效、可部署的学生骨干中，打破了传统蒸馏对共享潜空间的依赖，为扩散模型的高效部署提供了新路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出跨空间蒸馏（Cross-Space Distillation）方法，通过轻量级Bridge模块将现代大容量扩散教师模型（如SD 3.5、Flux）蒸馏到紧凑的单步学生模型（如SD 1.5）中，属于扩散模型蒸馏与加速方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化"（efficient_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses model efficiency via novel cross-space distillation, aligning with high-priority efficient inference and continuous multimodal model subtopics. Signals: Cross-space distillation, one-step diffusion, model efficiency, latent space alignment, SD 3.5/Flux teacher

Abstract: Modern one-step diffusion models achieve impressive quality through distribution-based timestep distillation. Yet, they rely on a critical assumption: Teacher and Student must inhabit the same latent space. This Shared-Space constraint prevents knowledge transfer from modern high-capacity Teachers (e.g., SD 3.5 and Flux) into compact, deployment-friendly Students such as SD 1.5, whose latent resolution and VAE parameterization differ from the Teacher. We formalize this overlooked regime as Cross-Space Distillation, where Teacher and Student differ in both latent resolution and VAE space. To enable distillation under this mismatch, we introduce the Bridge, a lightweight latent interface that maps Student latents into the Teacher space without modifying the Student backbone. Bridge combines a frozen Student VAE decoder as a spatial prior with a compact learnable projector, and is trained with latent reconstruction and attention fidelity objectives for stable Teacher-space alignment. Across diverse modern Teachers, Bridge enables substantial gains for compact one-step Students; for example, it improves SD 1.5 from 5.4 to 9.4 HPSv3 while preserving one-step inference, low latency, and broad ecosystem compatibility. These results show that heterogeneous large Teachers can be distilled into efficient, deployable backbones through a lightweight latent-space interface.

</details>

### [72] [TAG-DLM: Diffusion Language Models for Text-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31166)
*Lingjie Chen, Yuanchen Bei, Haobo Xu, Yanjun Zhao, Yuzhong Chen, Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL

Importance: 值得关注 (73.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出TAG-DLM，一种基于掩码扩散语言模型的文本属性图学习方法，通过将局部邻域线性化为token序列并注入拓扑注意力掩码，统一了文本推理与图消息传递，在节点分类、链接预测等任务上超越GNN、图Transformer和LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本属性图（TAG）方法通常将文本和图拓扑分开处理：GNN使用浅层文本特征，LLM+图混合方法将语言模型仅作为文本编码器，结构学习交给单独的图模块。缺乏一种真正统一文本推理与图结构学习的方法。

Method: 提出TAG-DLM，基于掩码扩散语言模型（masked diffusion language model），具有双向注意力和生成式解码能力。对每个图实例，将采样的局部邻域线性化为token序列，通过拓扑注意力掩码注入图结构信息，实现图上的消息传递。通过改变prompt即可适配不同任务（节点分类、链接预测、跨数据集迁移），无需目标特定微调。

Result: 在三个TAG基准数据集上的节点分类和链接预测任务中，TAG-DLM超越了GNN、图Transformer和基于LLM的基线方法，相比最强基线提升最多3.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型能够有效统一文本推理与图结构学习，通过拓扑注意力掩码实现图消息传递，且通过prompt适配不同任务具有良好泛化性，为文本属性图学习提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于掩码扩散语言模型（masked diffusion language model）的文本属性图学习方法，属于扩散语言模型在特定任务上的应用。虽然涉及图学习，但其核心方法是扩散语言模型（生成模型的一种），且论文主要贡献在于将扩散语言模型用于文本生成与图结构推理的统一。最接近的现有子主题是"生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment），因为该子主题涵盖扩散模型的可控性与任务适配，且该论文的扩散语言模型通过prompt适配不同任务，体现了生成模型的可控对齐特性。此外，该论文不属于连续语言模型（非连续latent空间）、世界模型、多模态理解与生成等方向。

Importance reason: Novel diffusion language model for graph learning, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: diffusion language model, text-attributed graph, topology attention mask, node classification, link prediction

Abstract: Text-attributed graphs (TAGs), where each node carries a natural language description, require models to jointly reason over text and graph topology. Existing approaches often handle the two modalities separately: graph neural networks operate on shallow text features, while hybrids of LLMs and graphs use the language model mainly as a text encoder and delegate structure learning to a separate graph module. We propose method that unifies textual reasoning and graph message passing within a masked diffusion language model, a language model with bidirectional attention and generative decoding. For each graph instance, method linearises a sampled local neighbourhood into a token sequence and injects graph structure through a topology attention mask, which realises message passing over the graph. Because the diffusion language model can both interpret and generate text, the method adapts to different tasks simply by changing the prompt, supporting node classification, link prediction, and cross-dataset transfer with no target-specific fine-tuning. Experiments show that method outperforms graph neural networks, graph transformers, and LLM-based baselines on all three TAG benchmarks across two tasks, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 3.9 points.

</details>


### [73] [Zero-Shot Quantization for Object Detectors using Off-the-Shelf Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31456)
*Hyunho Lee, Kyomin Hwang, Hyeonjin Kim, Suyoung Kim, Sunghyun Wee, Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (80.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GoodQ 提出利用现成生成模型为目标检测器构建零样本量化训练集，通过信息密集提示生成多实例图像、内在分布感知选择匹配类别分布、教师引导自适应降噪抑制噪声，在低比特（W4A4、W3A3）量化上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测模型在边缘设备部署时需量化，但原始训练数据因隐私等问题不可用。现有零样本量化方法通过噪声优化合成训练集，在低比特区域性能不佳。因此需要利用更强大的生成模型来构建高质量训练集。

Method: GoodQ 包含三个核心策略：1) 信息密集提示（Information-Dense Prompting），生成包含多个实例的密集图像；2) 内在分布感知选择（Intrinsic Distribution-Aware Selection），匹配预训练数据集的类别分布；3) 教师引导自适应降噪（Teacher-guided Adaptive Noise Reduction），在 QAT 过程中抑制伪标签噪声。

Result: 在低比特量化（W4A4）上达到 SOTA 性能，并首次将零样本量化扩展到极端比特宽度（W3A3）。实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: GoodQ 证明了利用现成生成模型可以有效解决零样本目标检测器量化问题，为数据受限场景下的模型部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究目标检测器的零样本量化（Zero-Shot Quantization），利用现成生成模型构建训练集以进行量化感知训练（QAT）。核心是生成模型对齐与可控性优化（信息密集提示、分布匹配、噪声抑制），属于生成模型对齐与奖励（generation_alignment）范畴下的生成数据对齐与可控性主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on object detection quantization, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Zero-shot quantization, Object detection, Generative model for data synthesis, Low-bit quantization, Off-the-shelf models

Abstract: With an increasing number of Object Detection (OD) models being deployed on edge devices, Zero-Shot Quantization for OD (ZSQ-OD) aims to quantize these models when access to the original training data is prohibited. Existing research on Zero-Shot Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) for OD synthesizes training sets through noise optimization. However, this approach struggles to maintain performance in low-bit regions. In this paper, we introduce GoodQ (Generative off-the-shelf models for object detector Quantization), a QAT pipeline that utilizes off-the-shelf generative models to construct a training set. We first identify three challenges that arise when introducing a generative model to the ZSQ-OD task: 1) each image contains dense information with multiple instances, 2) the class-wise distribution in the original dataset is imbalanced, and 3) the pseudo-labels assigned to the generated images can potentially act as noisy signals during QAT. GoodQ addresses these challenges by 1) introducing an Information-Dense Prompting strategy to generate multi-instance images, 2) applying Intrinsic Distribution-Aware Selection to match the pretrained class distribution, and 3) employing Teacher-guided Adaptive Noise Reduction to mitigate noise arising from the QAT process. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance in low-bit ZSQ (W4A4) and extends quantization to extreme bit-widths (W3A3). Furthermore, we conduct an extensive analysis to uncover the underlying factors contributing to the efficacy of GoodQ.

</details>


### [74] [Amplifying Membership Signal Through Chained Regeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31991)
*Wojciech Łapacz, Stanisław Pawlak*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出MADreMIA框架，利用链式再生（chained regeneration）迭代过程放大生成模型对训练数据的记忆信号，实现更有效的成员推断攻击（MIA）和数据集推断（DI），无需训练影子模型，适用于多种模型族（扩散模型、自回归模型、语言模型）和模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推断攻击（MIA）和数据集推断（DI）方法通常依赖单次生成，信号弱且跨模态敏感性有限；而训练影子模型对于大规模生成模型来说计算上不可行。因此需要一种无需影子模型、可扩展且跨模态的隐私审计方法。

Method: 受模型自噬紊乱（MAD）现象启发，提出MADreMIA框架。核心思想是通过链式再生（chained regeneration）迭代过程——每次生成的输出作为下一次的输入——来放大记忆信号。训练样本在迭代再生中表现出更高的连贯性和更慢的退化速度，而非成员样本则快速退化。该方法支持白盒、灰盒和黑盒设置，适用于扩散模型、自回归模型和语言模型等多种生成模型。

Result: 在多种模型族（IARs、扩散模型、语言模型）和模态上进行了全面评估，结果表明MADreMIA在低假阳性率（FPR）下提供了更丰富的成员推断信号，优于传统单次生成方法。初步实验还展示了该方法在音频模型上的潜力。

Conclusion: MADreMIA通过链式再生放大了生成模型的记忆信号，为隐私审计和版权执法提供了一种无需影子模型、跨模态可扩展的有效框架，在多种生成模型上均表现出优越的成员推断能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出MADreMIA框架，通过链式再生（chained regeneration）增强对生成模型（扩散模型、自回归模型、语言模型等）训练数据记忆的检测，属于生成模型隐私审计与成员推断攻击，与生成模型对齐与奖励（generation_alignment）中的安全/可控性主题相关。

Importance reason: Novel MIA framework for generative models, but tangential to user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: membership inference attack, dataset inference, chained regeneration, model-agnostic, diffusion models

Abstract: The tendency of large generative models to memorize training data makes sample verification critical for privacy auditing and copyright enforcement. Current membership (MIA) and dataset inference (DI) attacks often rely on one-shot generations, which yield weak signals and limited sensitivity across modalities. Inspired by Model Autophagy Disorder (MAD), we introduce MADreMIA, a model-agnostic framework that enhances white-, gray-, and black-box MIA and DI. Rather than relying on shadow model training -- often infeasible for large generative models -- our framework facilitates scalable inference by leveraging inherent signals through iterative trajectories. This process utilizes chained generations across diverse modalities, where each output serves as the subsequent input, to improve membership evidence at low FPR. We demonstrate that memorized training samples exhibit significantly higher coherence and slower degradation during iterative regeneration than non-member generations. Our results show that MADreMIA provides richer signals across diverse model families and modalities; we present comprehensive evaluations for IARs, diffusion, and language models, alongside preliminary results demonstrating its potential for audio models.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了稀疏自编码器（SAE）在扩散模型中用于概念擦除和引导的有效性，发现SAE虽能可靠检测语义概念，但直接干预其潜空间会导致分布外激活和严重伪影；提出将SAE仅作为语义检测器，通过检测到的区域替换patch embedding实现更干净的擦除效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设SAE提取的稀疏特征可作为可控干预点用于概念级操作（如擦除），但该假设缺乏系统验证。本文旨在评估SAE在扩散模型中用于概念检测与干预的实际效果，并探索更有效的擦除方法。

Method: 使用SAE激活作为语义检测器定位目标物体区域，然后将这些区域的patch embedding替换为不含目标物体的patch embedding，从而在保持扩散模型激活统计特性的前提下实现擦除，避免直接潜空间干预导致的分布外伪影。

Result: SAE能可靠检测和定位语义概念，但直接潜空间干预常导致严重视觉伪影；所提出的检测-替换方法相比潜空间引导产生更干净的擦除结果，揭示了概念检测与概念干预之间的根本差距。

Conclusion: 单语义或稀疏特征本质上不适合作为扩散模型的控制旋钮；SAE作为生成模型的可解释性分析工具具有强大潜力，但用于直接操作（如unlearning）时存在重要局限性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散模型中的概念擦除（unlearning）与引导（steering），属于生成模型的可控性与对齐问题，与 canonical subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: While the paper offers a rigorous evaluation of SAE limitations in diffusion models, it focuses on concept erasure and steering, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Sparse autoencoders for diffusion models, Concept detection vs. intervention gap, Unlearning and steering evaluation, Not aligned with priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have recently been proposed as interpretable tools for concept-level manipulation, under the assumption that isolated features can serve as controllable intervention points. In this work, we systematically evaluate this assumption in the context of object erasure and steering in diffusion models. We show that while SAEs reliably detect and localize semantic concepts within diffusion model activations, direct intervention in their latent space frequently induces out-of-distribution activations, resulting in severe visual artifacts. To disentangle detection from intervention, we use SAE activations purely as semantic detectors to identify image regions containing the target object, and replace those patch embeddings with the ones that do not contain it. This detection-based replacement preserves the diffusion model's activation statistics and produces significantly cleaner erasure results than latent steering. Our findings reveal a fundamental gap between concept detection and concept intervention in diffusion models: monosemantic or sparse features are not inherently suitable as control knobs for steering. These results position SAEs as powerful interpretability tools for analyzing generative models, but highlight important limitations when used for direct manipulation, such as unlearning.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文指出现有AI生成视频检测基准（如GenVidBench）的评测协议存在未控制的混淆因素，可能导致泛化性能被高估。作者提出六控制审计协议（Six-Control Protocol），并构建VidAudit工具包（含14个检测器），通过跨数据集审计揭示了简单分类器、CLIP基线等方法的真实性能，推荐使用AUC、above-floor margin、operating-point recall和calibration组成的审计元组替代单一指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频检测基准的评测协议存在未控制的混淆变量，导致报告的泛化性能可能被严重高估。例如，一个仅使用三个特征的片段长度分类器在GenVidBench上达到0.998 AUC，但实际上并未测量任何运动信息。20篇论文调查发现没有一篇应用了全部六项标准控制措施。

Method: 提出六控制审计协议（Six-Control Protocol），包含：1) 留一生成器评估（LOGO）；2) 跨数据集评估；3) 真实vs真实一致性检验；4) 基线消融；5) 操作点分析；6) 校准评估。构建VidAudit工具包，集成14个检测器（含3个已发布检测器、3个改造信号源、TemporalSpec白盒正控制等），并引入跨底物特征融合（XSFF）方法。

Result: 审计揭示：三特征片段长度分类器从0.998 AUC降至接近随机（0.529）；CLIP基线被发现携带数据集身份信息；2025年检测器WaveRep在分布外LOGO AUC达0.996且真实vs真实一致性为随机水平。在可部署的0.1% FPR下，多个高AUC方法降至个位数召回率，排行榜顺序发生改变。

Conclusion: 推荐使用审计元组（AUC、above-floor margin、操作点召回率和校准）替代单一指标。六控制审计协议和VidAudit工具包将评测从排行榜排名转向验证结果是否真正测量了其所声称的内容。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于AI生成视频检测的评测协议与基准，提出了六控制审计协议和VidAudit工具包，属于生成模型评测范畴，因此映射到multimodal_generation方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"。

Importance reason: Focuses on auditing AI video detection benchmarks, not on core generative model advancement or user's priority subtopics. Signals: Evaluation protocol audit, AI-generated video detection, Generalization confounds, Not generative model innovation, Low priority subtopic match

Abstract: AI-generated video detection benchmarks such as GenVidBench and AIGVDBench are the de facto leaderboards, yet most evaluation protocols leave uncontrolled confounds that can inflate reported generalization. As an existence proof, a three-feature clip-length classifier reaches a leave-one-generator-out (LOGO) AUC of 0.998 on GenVidBench under unaudited evaluation, while measuring nothing about motion. A 20-paper survey finds none applying all six standard controls that would catch this, so we combine them into an audited protocol and apply it to six representative feature sources (three published detectors and three repurposed signal sources), re-running it cross-dataset on AIGVDBench. The audit both debunks and certifies: the trivial classifier collapses to near chance (0.529), a CLIP baseline is caught carrying dataset identity, and the 2025 forensic detector WaveRep clears the floor at out-of-distribution LOGO AUC 0.996 with chance-level real-vs-real coherence. At a deployable FPR of 0.1%, multiple high-AUC methods fall to single-digit recall and the leaderboard order changes, so we recommend an audited tuple (AUC, above-floor margin, operating-point recall, and calibration) over a single number. As a white-box positive control, we add TemporalSpec (codec motion vectors); via cross-substrate feature fusion (XSFF), a second substrate adds genuine complementarity that survives the audit. We release VidAudit, to our knowledge the largest unified and audited detector collection for this task, providing 14 detectors behind one plugin API, a leaderboard, and Croissant metadata, available at . Together, the protocol and toolkit move evaluation from leaderboard rank toward whether a result measures what it claims.

</details>


### [77] [Arena-T2I Hard: Benchmarking and Improving Faithfulness with Dependency-Aware Checklist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31711)
*Yuanhao Ban, Tong Xie, Sohyun An, Yunqi Hong, Evan Frick, I-Hung Hsu, Wei-Lin Chiang, Ion Stoica, Cho-Jui Hsieh*

TL;DR: 本文提出 Arena-T2I Hard，一个包含310个复杂提示词的文生图忠实性压力测试基准，每个提示词分解为约30个细粒度约束。实验表明现有最强闭源系统在该基准上得分仅0.855，且公开竞技场排名与忠实性相关性弱。作者进一步提出依赖感知的检查表奖励（DAG结构）结合组解耦归一化（GDPO）的多奖励优化方法，在忠实性与美学之间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图忠实性评测基准过于简单（原子指令），顶级模型已接近满分，无法区分模型在复杂多约束场景下的真实表现。同时，公开竞技场的Bradley-Terry偏好评分更偏向美学而非细粒度忠实性，亟需一个能精细评估复杂提示词忠实性的基准和对应的训练信号。

Method: 1) 构建 Arena-T2I Hard 基准：从真实竞技场日志中筛选310个复杂提示词，每个提示词由VLM分解为约30个二元约束（覆盖空间关系、风格、文本渲染等6类），形成细粒度评测集。2) 提出依赖感知检查表奖励（Dependency-Aware Checklist Reward）：将每个提示词分解为有向无环图（DAG）形式的二元问题，失败父节点的子节点自动归零，将忠实性转化为每个约束的训练信号。3) 结合组解耦归一化（GDPO）美学奖励：将每个奖励在其生成组内标准化，避免单一奖励或简单加权求和导致的模式坍塌，实现忠实性与美学的严格帕累托改进。

Result: 最强闭源系统在 Arena-T2I Hard 上得分0.855，11个系统间存在33个百分点的性能差距，展示了强大的区分能力。在 SD3.5-Medium 和 FLUX.1-dev 上，所提方法在 MMRB2 成对比较中优于所有单奖励、朴素加权求和及4奖励BT集成基线，实现了忠实性与美学之间更优的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文证明了现有文生图忠实性评测的不足，并提出了更具区分度的压力测试基准。依赖感知检查表奖励结合GDPO的多奖励优化方法能有效提升模型忠实性而不牺牲美学质量，为文生图模型的细粒度对齐提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向文生图模型忠实性的压力测试基准 Arena-T2I Hard，属于生成模型评测范畴，因此映射到 multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）。同时该论文也涉及多模态对齐奖励方法（dependency-aware checklist reward + GDPO），与 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 multimodal_rl_alignment 相关。

Importance reason: Strong faithfulness benchmark and reward method for text-to-image generation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or world models. Signals: faithfulness benchmark, dependency-aware checklist reward, group-decoupled normalization, text-to-image generation, complex prompt evaluation

Abstract: Faithfulness -- how precisely a generated image aligns with its prompt -- is increasingly central to the real-world utility of text-to-image (T2I) models. Existing faithfulness benchmarks, however, rely on simple atomic instructions, on which top-tier systems already achieve near-perfect scores. As T2I models enter creative workflows, users issue multi-faceted requests combining intricate spatial relationships, stylistic constraints, and complex text rendering. In this setting, a single binary VLM-judge score no longer captures which specific constraints the model fails to satisfy. We introduce Arena-T2I Hard, a 310-prompt stress benchmark drawn from real arena T2I logs, with approximately 30 decomposed yes/no constraints per prompt spanning six categories, including text rendering. The strongest closed-source system we evaluate reaches 0.855 with a 33~pp performance gap across 11 systems, demonstrating substantial discriminative power. Moreover, high public-arena rankings fail to predict faithfulness, confirming that holistic Bradley-Terry (BT) preference scores prioritize aesthetics over fine-grained prompt adherence. We propose a dependency-aware checklist reward that decomposes each prompt into a DAG of yes/no questions and zeroes descendants of failed parents, turning faithfulness into a per-constraint training signal. Combined with a BT aesthetic reward via group-decoupled normalization (GDPO), which standardizes each reward within its rollout group so neither collapses, the recipe attains a strictly better faithfulness-aesthetics trade-off on SD3.5-Medium and FLUX.1-dev under MMRB2 pairwise comparisons than every single-reward, naive weighted-sum, or 4-reward BT-ensemble baseline.

</details>


### [78] [DataEvolver: Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Data Construction for Text-Rich Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31537)
*Siyu Yan, Yizhen Gao, Yilin Wang, Dongxing Mao, Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MA

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

TL;DR: DataEvolver 是一个自演化多智能体框架，通过检索器、验证器、评论者和生成器四个智能体的协作，将文本丰富图像生成中原本被丢弃的失败样本转化为反馈信号，迭代优化数据构建策略，在 PixArt-alpha 和 Show-o2 上显著提升了 OCR-F1 指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本丰富图像生成的数据构建流程采用静态的"爬取-过滤-冻结"范式，丢弃了大量包含有用失败信号（如OCR错误、语义不匹配）的样本，导致后续构建轮次重复相同的失败模式。需要一种能够利用失败样本反馈来自我改进的数据构建方法。

Method: 提出 DataEvolver 多智能体框架，包含四个角色：(1) Retriever 收集候选样本；(2) Verifier 分配质量分数并记录拒绝原因；(3) Critic 将轮次级反馈总结为语义反馈；(4) Generator 针对覆盖不足的区域进行目标合成。更新后的反馈记忆指导下一轮数据构建，形成自演化循环。

Result: 在 0.75M 数据规模下，基于 PixArt-alpha，DataEvolver 在 TextScenesHQ 上 OCR-F1 提升 85.3%，在 LongTextBench 上提升 35.3%。改进效果在 Show-o2 上也得到验证，表明方法不依赖于特定下游生成器。

Conclusion: 被拒绝的样本可以提供可操作的反馈信号，用于改进文本丰富图像的数据构建。DataEvolver 的自演化多智能体框架能够有效利用这些信号，在固定数据预算下生成比静态数据集更有用的训练数据。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DataEvolver，一个自演化的多智能体数据构建框架，用于生成文本丰富的图像训练数据。核心贡献在于数据构建流程的改进（而非生成模型本身），属于生成模型的数据集构建与评测范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Novel self-evolving multi-agent framework for text-rich image generation, but subtopic is not a priority. Signals: Self-evolving multi-agent, text-rich image generation, failure signal reuse, OCR-F1 improvement, PixArt-alpha

Abstract: Text-rich image generation is one of the most challenging settings in image generation, since models must simultaneously produce visually realistic images and render legible, semantically aligned, and layout-consistent text. Existing data pipelines usually follow a static crawl-filter-freeze paradigm. They collect candidate samples, filter them once, and freeze the accepted data for training. However, rejected samples are usually discarded, although they often contain useful failure signals such as OCR errors and semantic mismatches. As a result, later construction rounds may repeat the same failure modes. To address these limitations, we propose DataEvolver, a self-evolving multi-agent framework for text-rich image data construction. DataEvolver treats data construction as feedback-driven construction policy evolution. A Retriever collects candidate samples, a Verifier assigns quality scores and rejection causes, a Critic summarizes round-level feedback into semantic feedback, and a Generator completes under-covered regions through targeted synthesis. The updated feedback memory then guides the next construction round. Experiments on text-rich image generation benchmarks show that DataEvolver produces more useful training data than fixed-dataset baselines under matched data budgets. At the 0.75M scale on PixArt-alpha, DataEvolver improves OCR-F1 over the strongest baseline by 85.3 percent on TextScenesHQ and 35.3 percent on LongTextBench. The improvements are consistent across both evaluated benchmarks and also transfer to Show-o2, indicating that the benefit of DataEvolver is not tied to a single downstream generator. These results suggest that rejected samples can provide actionable feedback for improving text-rich image data construction.

</details>

*Dominika Woszczyk, Andreas Triantafyllopoulos, Jura Miniota, Éva Székely, Bjoern Schuller*

TL;DR: 本文研究了五个SOTA TTS系统在五个不同领域（AI助手、朗读、表演、动画角色、自发言语）中的自然度与适当性。结果表明，适当性随领域变化且独立于自然度；系统在朗读领域表现优异，但在表达性领域仍有挑战；自然度评分会惩罚风格化语音而奖励自发性；TTS性能并非"已解决"，而是依赖于目标领域，需要上下文感知的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前TTS评估主要关注"自然度"，但随着合成语音保真度的提升，研究者开始关注"适当性"——即语音是否适合其使用场景。然而，不同下游领域对语音的期望差异尚未被系统研究，现有评估指标可能存在盲区。

Method: 选取五个SOTA TTS系统（包括基于扩散模型和自回归模型的方法），在五个不同领域（AI助手、朗读、表演、动画角色、自发言语）上收集人类评分，分别评估自然度（human-likeness）和适当性（appropriateness），并分析两者之间的关系及跨领域差异。

Result: 适当性在不同领域间存在显著差异，且与自然度并非强相关；TTS系统在朗读领域表现最佳，但在需要表达性的领域（如表演、动画角色）仍存在挑战；优化某一领域的自然度可能会降低其他领域的表现；自然度评分倾向于惩罚风格化/表达性语音，而奖励自发性语音；现有自动评估指标在表达性领域存在盲区。

Conclusion: TTS性能并非"已解决"的问题，其表现高度依赖于目标应用领域。评估需要从单一的自然度指标转向上下文感知的多维度评估框架，充分考虑不同领域对适当性的差异化需求。

Classification reason: 该论文研究TTS系统的自然度与适当性评估，属于语音生成模型的评测问题，映射到多模态生成方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark），该子主题涵盖生成模型评测基准、自动评测指标与人类偏好评估。

Importance reason: Focuses on TTS evaluation methodology, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: TTS evaluation, naturalness vs appropriateness, domain-dependent performance, context-aware evaluation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Text-to-speech (TTS) evaluation is an open challenge. While the primary target was "naturalness," recent fidelity gains shifted focus toward "appropriateness" and whether speech is correct for its context. In this work, we examine how perception changes when the expected downstream use varies. We measure the appropriateness and human-likeness of five SOTA TTS systems across five domains: AI assistant, reader, actor, animated character, and spontaneous speaker. Results show appropriateness varies across domains independently of naturalness. While systems shine at reading, expressive domains remain challenging, and optimizing for one can degrade others. Furthermore, naturalness scores tend to penalize stylized speech while rewarding spontaneity. Finally, our study also highlights blind spots in one-size-fits-all evaluation metrics across more expressive domains. We demonstrate that TTS performance is not "solved" but depends on the target domain, requiring context-aware evaluation.

</details>

### [80] [Generative Lane Topology Reasoning via Autoregressive Model with Geometry Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31814)
*Jiahui Fu, Zehao Huang, Han Li, Naiyan Wang, Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 自回归视觉生成

Importance: 值得关注 (67.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TopoGPT 提出一种基于自回归序列建模的车道拓扑推理生成框架，通过车道 tokenizer 将车道图序列化为离散 token，预训练自回归车道序列 Transformer 学习几何先验，再通过感知适配器将 BEV 特征对齐到预训练条件，实现传感器端到端的车道图生成，在 OpenLane-V2 上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车道拓扑推理方法采用检测+关联范式，将每条车道实例独立处理，导致连接端点几何不一致，且因视觉遮挡产生不完整图结构。作者希望利用车道图结构中的几何先验，通过生成式方法解决这些问题。

Method: 构建包含330万场景的大规模地图数据集；设计车道 tokenizer 将车道图序列化为离散 token；场景上下文编码器将地图栅格化并提取全局特征作为场景 token；预训练自回归车道序列 Transformer 进行场景条件下的 next-token prediction 以学习几何先验；设计感知适配器将多视图图像的 BEV 特征对齐到预训练场景条件，迁移几何先验到传感器端车道图预测。

Result: 在 OpenLane-V2 基准上，TopoGPT 在车道级指标平均提升 +6.4，在点级指标平均提升 +11.6，生成的车道图具有几何一致性和结构完整性。

Conclusion: 自回归生成范式能够有效学习车道拓扑结构的几何先验，克服检测+关联范式的几何不一致和图不完整问题，为车道拓扑推理提供了新的生成式解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文使用自回归序列建模（next-token prediction）进行车道拓扑图生成，属于自回归视觉生成范式，与 [CANONICAL] 自回归视觉生成 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with autoregressive visual generation and geometry prior learning, directly matching priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: autoregressive lane graph generation, geometry prior via pretraining, BEV perception adapter, OpenLane-V2 SOTA, 3.3M scene dataset

Abstract: Lane topology reasoning aims to construct a lane graph from onboard sensor observations. Existing methods follow a detection and association paradigm that treats each lane instance independently, leading to geometric inconsistency at connected endpoints and incomplete graphs due to visual occlusions. To address these issues, we propose TopoGPT, a generative framework that learns the geometry prior from typical lane graph structures through autoregressive sequence modeling. Specifically, we construct a large-scale map dataset comprising 3.3M scenes. For each lane graph, a lane tokenizer serializes it into discrete tokens, while a scene context encoder converts it into a rasterized image and extracts global features as scene tokens. We pre-train an autoregressive lane sequence transformer via scene-conditioned next-token prediction, endowing the model with the geometry prior over lane graph structures. Building upon this prior, a perception adapter aligns BEV features from multi-view images with the pre-trained scene condition, transferring the learned geometry prior to sensor-based lane graph prediction. On the OpenLane-V2 benchmark, TopoGPT outperforms existing methods by an average of +6.4 on lane-level and +11.6 on point-level metrics, and produces geometrically consistent and structurally complete lane graphs.

</details>


### [81] [GEAR: Guided End-to-End AutoRegression for Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32039)
*Bin Lin, Zheyuan Liu, Chenguo Lin, Sixiang Chen, Yunyang Ge, Yunlong Lin, Jianwei Zhang, Miles Yang, Zhao Zhong, Liefeng Bo, Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 自回归视觉生成

Importance: 高优先级 (96.2) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: GEAR 提出了一种端到端联合训练 VQ tokenizer 和自回归生成器的方法，通过双路码本读出（hard one-hot 分支用于 AR 训练，soft 可微分支用于表征对齐损失回传）解决了 tokenizer 梯度无法到达的问题，使 AR 模型能够引导 tokenizer 生成更易预测的索引分布，在 ImageNet 上实现高达 10 倍的 gFID 收敛加速，并泛化到多种量化器及文生图任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成模型采用两阶段训练范式：先训练 tokenizer（仅优化重建损失），再冻结 tokenizer 训练生成器。这种解耦方式导致 tokenizer 不了解生成器对哪些特征更容易建模，tokenizer 的表示空间与生成器的预测需求之间存在不对齐。作者希望让 tokenizer 感知生成器的"偏好"，实现端到端联合训练。

Method: GEAR 的核心是双路码本读出机制：(1) hard one-hot 分支：对码本索引进行离散化，用于 AR 模型的 next-token 预测训练（不可微）；(2) soft 可微分支：保持码本分配的软概率，计算与 DINOv2 特征之间的表征对齐损失，该损失可微地回传到 tokenizer。通过这种方式，AR 模型的梯度信号（通过表征对齐损失）能够引导 tokenizer 生成更易于 AR 模型预测的索引分布。训练时两个分支同时进行，推理时仅使用 hard 分支。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256×256 上，GEAR 相比强基线 LlamaGen-REPA 实现高达 10 倍的 gFID 收敛加速；学习到显著更好的 patch 级和空间一致特征；泛化到 VQVAE、LFQ、IBQ 等多种量化器；在文本到图像生成任务上也有效。

Conclusion: GEAR 通过端到端联合训练 tokenizer 和自回归生成器，有效解决了两阶段训练中的不对齐问题。该方法将对齐负担从 tokenizer 转移到 AR 模型（tokenizer 特征变得不那么 DINOv2-like，而 AR 特征变得更 DINOv2-like），与扩散模型侧让 latent 本身语义化的做法相反，为自回归视觉生成提供了新的训练范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GEAR，一种联合训练 VQ tokenizer 和自回归生成器的方法，属于自回归图像生成范式。论文以 LlamaGen 为基线，聚焦于自回归视觉生成中 tokenizer 与 generator 的端到端联合训练，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 自回归视觉生成 (autoregressive_visual_generation)。

Importance reason: 端到端联合训练VQ tokenizer与自回归生成器，直接解决连续语言模型与自回归视觉生成的核心梯度阻断问题，且与用户高权重方向高度吻合。 Signals: 连续语言模型与自回归视觉生成联合训练, 双路码本读出解决非可微问题, 10倍gFID收敛加速, 泛化至多种量化器与文生图, 与用户priority_subtopics中连续语言模型、自回归图像生成、模型高效性直接相关

Abstract: Visual generative models are typically trained in two stages. A tokenizer is first trained for reconstruction and then frozen, after which a generator is trained on its discrete indices or continuous latents. This decoupling leaves the tokenizer unaware of what the generator finds easy to model. We present GEAR (Guided End-to-end AutoRegression), which trains a vector-quantized (VQ) tokenizer and an autoregressive (AR) generator jointly and end-to-end, guided by representation alignment. The key obstacle is that the VQ index fed to the AR model is non-differentiable, so gradients cannot reach the tokenizer, and a straight-through estimator collapses. GEAR resolves this with a dual read-out of the codebook assignment. A hard, one-hot branch trains the AR with next-token prediction, while a differentiable soft branch carries a representation-alignment loss that flows back to guide only the tokenizer. The AR model thereby steers its tokenizer toward an index distribution it can predict more easily. This shifts the alignment burden from the tokenizer to the AR: the tokenizer's own features become less DINOv2-like while the AR's become more so, the opposite of diffusion-side recipes that make the latent itself semantic. GEAR speeds up ImageNet gFID convergence by up to 10x relative to the strong LlamaGen-REPA baseline, learns markedly better patch-level and spatially-coherent features, and generalizes across quantizers (VQVAE, LFQ, IBQ) and to text-to-image generation.

</details>

### [82] [AVTok: 1D Unified Tokenization for Holistic Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30811)
*Kien T. Pham, I Chieh Chen, Qifeng Chen, Long Chen*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.CV, cs.MM, eess.AS

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 高优先级 (87.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AVTok 提出了一种统一的音视频分词器，采用双流Transformer架构与共享编解码器、模态特定可学习查询，将音视频对压缩为紧凑的一维潜空间表示，并通过分层训练策略解决异构信息不平衡问题，在音视频重建及下游生成任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频生成方法采用双分支设计，各模态独立分词和生成，忽略了模态间的表征鸿沟，且需要大量计算资源。作者希望设计一个统一的音视频分词器，将音视频对编码为紧凑的联合潜表示，为构建统一的音视频生成大模型奠定基础。

Method: AVTok 采用双流Transformer架构，包含共享的编码器-解码器和模态特定的可学习查询，将音频和视频对编码为紧凑的一维潜空间表示，并使用统一码本。为应对异构信息不平衡问题（音频和视频信息量差异大），设计了分层训练策略，逐步实现各模态的重建能力。

Result: 实验表明AVTok在音视频重建任务上表现优异，并成功集成到下游流水线中，支持音频到视频、视频到音频以及类别条件联合音视频生成等多种任务。

Conclusion: AVTok为联合音视频分词化这一挑战铺平了道路，为构建统一的音视频生成大模型提供了潜在方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出AVTok统一音视频分词器，用于音视频联合生成（audio-to-video, video-to-audio, joint audio-video generation），属于多模态生成方向下的[CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation），该子主题涵盖音视频联合生成与跨模态条件音频生成。

Importance reason: Proposes a unified audio-video tokenizer with 1D latent representation, directly advancing continuous multimodal models and unified multimodal generation. Signals: unified audio-video tokenization, 1D latent representation, hierarchical training for heterogeneous modalities, strong reconstruction and downstream generation, aligns with continuous language/multimodal model priority

Abstract: Audio-video generation has recently gained unprecedented research attention, aiming to synthesize high-quality sounding video content with fine-grained synchronization and semantic alignment between the auditory and visual components. The preceding methods predominantly adopt a dual-branch design with separate tokenization and generation modules per modality, neglecting the representation gap while necessitating intensive computational resources for proper training. Inspired by recent advancements in one-dimensional visual tokenization, we present \textbf{AVTok}, a novel unified tokenizer designated for holistic audio-video generation. AVTok features a dual-stream transformer-based architecture with shared encoder-decoder and modal-specific learnable queries to efficiently and effectively encode an audio-video pair into a compact one-dimensional latent representation with a unified codebook. To cope with the heterogeneous information imbalance that hinders AVTok from exploiting aligned audio-visual information, we devise a hierarchical training strategy to progressively realize reconstruction capabilities for each modality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AVTok excels both in audio-video reconstruction and when integrated into downstream pipelines for audio-to-video, video-to-audio, and class-conditional joint audio-video generation. AVTok paves the way for the challenge of joint audio-video tokenization and provides a potential direction to build unified large multimodal models for audio-video generation.

</details>


### [83] [SwiftAudio: Data-Efficient Caption-Only Distillation for One-Step Text-to-Audio Diffusion-based Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31259)
*Binh Mai, Tran Quoc Bao Le, Hung Dinh, Cong Tran*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.AI, cs.MM, eess.AS

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (51.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SwiftAudio 提出一种仅使用文本标题（无需配对音频数据）进行蒸馏的单步文本到音频生成框架，通过将变分分数蒸馏（VSD）适配到音频域并引入时序平滑正则化，在 AudioCaps 和 Clotho 上达到单步方法最优性能，显著缩小了与多步扩散系统的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式文本到音频（TTA）模型合成质量高但推理延迟大，已有的单步方法虽缓解了延迟问题，但仍依赖配对的文本-音频数据进行蒸馏，限制了数据效率和实用性。本文旨在实现无需配对音频数据的单步 TTA 蒸馏。

Method: 将变分分数蒸馏（VSD）从图像域适配到音频域，设计时序平滑正则化损失以鼓励连贯的潜在音频表征，使学生模型仅从预训练扩散教师模型继承生成先验，无需配对音频监督，仅需约 45K 文本标题即可有效训练。

Result: 在 AudioCaps 和 Clotho 数据集上，SwiftAudio 在严格单步方法中达到最优性能，显著缩小了与多步扩散系统之间的质量差距。

Conclusion: SwiftAudio 证明了仅使用文本标题进行音频蒸馏的可行性，为数据高效的单步 TTA 生成提供了新范式，有望推动音频生成模型的实用化部署。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本到音频生成（TTA），属于音频生成方向，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "音频与多模态联合生成" (audio_av_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on audio generation, which is not a priority subtopic, and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: one-step text-to-audio, diffusion distillation, VSD adaptation, temporal smoothness, data-efficient caption-only

Abstract: Diffusion-based text-to-audio (TTA) models achieve impressive synthesis quality but suffer from high inference latency due to iterative multi-step denoising. Existing one-step approaches alleviate this issue but still rely on paired text--audio data during distillation. To address these limitations, we propose SwiftAudio, a one-step TTA framework that performs audio-free distillation from a pretrained diffusion teacher using only text captions. Specifically, we adapt Variational Score Distillation (VSD) to the audio domain and introduce a temporal smoothness regularization objective to encourage coherent latent audio representations. This design enables the student model to inherit the teacher's generative prior without requiring paired audio supervision and allows effective training with only approximately 45K captions. Experiments on AudioCaps and Clotho demonstrate that SwiftAudio achieves state-of-the-art performance among strict one-step methods and substantially narrows the gap to multi-step diffusion systems. Project page:

</details>


### [84] [UniSAE: Unified Speech Attribute Editing on Speaker, Emotion and Low-Level Content via Discrete Phonetic Posteriorgram Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31128)
*Chuanbo Zhu, Wuyou Zhou, Rongxiu Zhong, Shilei Zhang, Kun Qian, Yike Guo, Wei Xue*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.AI, eess.AS, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 普通相关 (50.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniSAE 提出统一语音属性编辑框架，通过离散音素后验图（DPPG）表示将语音内容分解为音素身份、发音变体和时长等离散 token，支持从子音素到词级别的说话人、情感和内容编辑，使用自回归 Transformer 预测编辑后的 DPPG 序列，并由扩散声学解码器渲染语音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音编辑方法主要关注词级内容修改，且通常将内容、说话人和情感编辑视为独立任务，限制了编辑粒度和灵活性。作者希望在一个统一框架中实现多粒度、多属性的组合式语音编辑。

Method: 提出离散音素后验图（DPPG）表示，将语音内容分解为音素身份、发音变体和时长的离散 token；对于词级编辑，使用自回归内容 Transformer 预测编辑后的 DPPG 序列；最终由基于扩散的声学解码器在解耦的说话人和情感表征条件下渲染语音。

Result: 实验表明 UniSAE 支持精确的说话人和情感控制、多粒度内容编辑，以及在一个框架内联合修改所有三种属性。

Conclusion: UniSAE 首次实现了统一框架下的说话人、情感和内容的多粒度组合式语音编辑，验证了 DPPG 表示在语音属性解耦与编辑中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出统一语音属性编辑框架，涉及语音生成、说话人/情感/内容编辑，属于音频生成范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 音频与多模态联合生成 (audio_av_generation) 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on speech attribute editing, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: speech editing, discrete phonetic posteriorgram, autoregressive transformer, diffusion decoder, speaker/emotion/content editing

Abstract: Speech editing aims to modify specific portions of an utterance while preserving the remaining speech. Existing approaches primarily focus on word-level content modification and typically treat content, speaker, and emotion editing as separate tasks, limiting both editing granularity and flexibility. We propose UniSAE, a unified speech attribute editing framework which supports composable speaker, emotion and content editing from sub-phoneme to word level within a single architecture. UniSAE introduces a Discrete Phonetic PosteriorGram (DPPG) representation that factorizes speech content into discrete tokens encoding phoneme identity, pronunciation variants, and duration, enabling direct phoneme- and sub-phoneme-level editing. For higher-level modifications, an autoregressive content transformer predicts edited DPPG sequences for word-level content editing. The edited sequences are rendered into speech by a diffusion-based acoustic decoder, conditioned on disentangled speaker and emotion representations. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed unified framework supports precise speaker and emotion control, content editing at multiple granularities, and joint modification of all three attributes within a single framework.

</details>

### [85] [Harnessing Textual Refusal Directions for Multimodal Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31876)
*Moreno D'Incà, Massimiliano Mancini, Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MARS（Modality-Agnostic Refusal Steering），一种轻量级、无需训练的多模态安全对齐方法。通过从 LLM 骨干中提取文本拒绝方向并推广到图像/视频模态，结合激活重定心、几何信任区域自适应缩放和最优层选择，在五个 SOTA MLLM 上实现了安全性与实用性的平衡提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型的安全对齐通常需要大量不安全的多模态数据，收集成本远高于纯文本数据。本文旨在探索能否仅利用文本模态的拒绝方向来提升多模态安全性，从而降低对多模态不安全数据的依赖。

Method: 提出 MARS 方法：(1) 从 LLM 骨干中提取文本拒绝方向；(2) 通过激活重定心（activation re-centering）纠正模态错位，避免安全输入被错误引导；(3) 在几何定义的信任区域内自适应缩放引导强度；(4) 选择最优干预层，仅在第一个生成 token 处进行操作。整个过程无需训练，无需多模态安全数据。

Result: 在五个 SOTA MLLM（LLaVA、Video-LLaVA 等）上评估，涵盖安全性、实用性和视频越狱基准。MARS 在保持实用性的同时实现了持续的安全提升，验证了文本拒绝方向在跨模态安全对齐中的有效性。

Conclusion: 安全相关的结构在模态间是共享的，文本拒绝方向可以作为多模态对齐的强大且未被充分探索的基础。MARS 提供了一种高效、无需训练的多模态安全注入方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型的安全对齐问题，提出利用文本拒绝方向进行跨模态安全干预，属于多模态安全防御方向，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态安全与攻防 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal safety alignment, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: Training-free safety method, Textual refusal directions generalize to vision, Activation steering for MLLMs, No multimodal unsafe data needed, Evaluated on multiple MLLMs

Abstract: To improve safety in Large Language Models (LLMs) we can either perform post-training alignment or exploit refusal directions in the activation space. Both strategies are less feasible in Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) as they require unsafe multimodal data, harder to collect than their unimodal counterpart. In this work, we relax this constraint and investigate whether textual refusal directions, extracted directly from the LLM backbone, generalize across modalities (i.e., image, video). Preliminary findings confirm this ability, though effectiveness is conditioned by layer selection, steering strength, and cross-modal alignment, with the latter causing safe multimodal inputs to be spuriously steered toward refusal. Building on this, we introduce Modality-Agnostic Refusal Steering (MARS), a light-weight training-free approach that injects multimodal safety without the need for multimodal safety data. MARS corrects modality misalignment via activation re-centering, adaptively scales steering strength within a geometrically defined trust region, and selects the optimal intervention layer, operating at the first generated token. Evaluated on five SOTA MLLMs across safety, utility, and video jailbreak benchmarks, MARS achieves consistent safety gains while preserving utility. These results reveal that safety-relevant structure is shared across modalities and that textual refusal directions are a powerful and underexplored foundation for multimodal alignment.

</details>

### [86] [ADAPT: Attention Dynamics Alignment with Preference Tuning for Faithful MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31054)
*Zhiyuan Yao, Zheren Fu, Zhixiao Zheng, Jiajun Li, Yi Tu, Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MM, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 高优先级 (88.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT框架，通过干预文本到图像的交叉注意力动态来缓解MLLM的幻觉问题。核心包括：从早期解码中提炼交叉注意力视觉锚点提供稳定空间定位、在线检测并纠正注意力漂移的注意力监督推理机制、以及视觉注意力引导的DPO偏好对齐。在多个幻觉基准上降低40%-60%的幻觉率，同时保持通用多模态能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM存在严重的幻觉问题，生成内容与输入图像不一致。现有缓解策略大多是结果驱动的，没有直接针对模型内部的注意力退化这一根本原因。作者发现幻觉的内部标志是生成过程中文本到图像交叉注意力的逐步退化，导致注意力不聚焦或偏斜。

Method: ADAPT包含三个关键组件：(1) 交叉注意力视觉锚点——从早期解码阶段提炼稳定的空间注意力分布作为锚点；(2) 注意力监督推理——在推理时在线检测注意力漂移并进行纠正；(3) 视觉注意力引导DPO——将偏好对齐导向视觉上更忠实的响应。三者协同工作，直接干预交叉注意力动态。

Result: 在多个幻觉评测基准上，ADAPT将幻觉率降低40%-60%，在主流MLLM骨干网络上均取得最佳结果，同时保持通用多模态能力不下降。消融实验验证了每个组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文从模型内部交叉注意力行为的角度提供了缓解幻觉的新视角，证明了直接干预注意力动态比结果驱动的后处理方法更有效。ADAPT框架为构建更忠实可靠的多模态大模型提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于多模态大模型（MLLM）的幻觉缓解问题，通过注意力动态对齐与偏好微调提升生成内容的视觉忠实性，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses MLLM hallucination via attention dynamics, a core reliability issue for multimodal understanding. Signals: MLLM hallucination mitigation, cross-attention dynamics, preference tuning, 40-60% hallucination reduction, attention supervision inference

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are critically hampered by hallucination, generating content inconsistent with the provided image. In this paper, we identify an internal signature of hallucination: progressive degradation of text-to-image cross-attention during generation, leading to specific failure patterns like unfocused or biased attention. Existing mitigation strategies are largely outcome-driven and do not explicitly target this failure mode. To address this problem, we propose ADAPT (Attention Dynamics Alignment with Preference Tuning), an attention-based framework that intervenes directly on text-to-image cross-attention dynamics. We propose ADAPT with three key contributions: a cross-attention visual anchor refined from early decoding to provide stable spatial grounding, an attention-supervised inference mechanism that detects and corrects attention drift online, and a Visual Attention Guidance DPO that aligns preferences toward visually grounded responses. Experiments show that each component of ADAPT contributes to hallucination reduction, and the full framework achieves new best results across multiple hallucination benchmarks, reducing hallucination rates by 40%-60% across mainstream backbones while preserving general multimodal capabilities. Our work provides an attention-based perspective on mitigating hallucinations by exploring the model's internal text-to-image cross-attention behaviors. Code is available at

</details>


### [87] [CORTEX: Token-Level Hallucination Detection in RAG via Comparative Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31033)
*Kazuaki Furumai, Shuichiro Haruta, Kazunori Matsumoto, Daisuke Kamisaka*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CORTEX 提出一种基于LLM内部表征对比的token级幻觉检测方法，通过比较有无检索文档时模型的内部表征差异，并结合前文传播与平滑后处理，在RAG场景中实现细粒度的幻觉定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长文本RAG输出中，幻觉往往出现在局部片段而非整个回答中，现有方法缺乏token级别的细粒度检测能力。作者希望利用"基于检索文档的token应比幻觉token更受文档影响"这一直觉，通过对比内部表征来精确定位幻觉。

Method: CORTEX 包含三个核心组件：(1) 比较LLM在有/无检索文档两种条件下的内部表征，捕获文档对每个token的影响程度；(2) 利用文档信息通过前文token的传播效应，减少因证据已被吸收到上下文中而产生的误报；(3) 后处理平滑步骤，利用幻觉标签在连续片段中的持续性来降低局部噪声，鼓励片段一致的预测。

Result: 在两个RAG基准测试和三个LLM上的实验表明，CORTEX 显著提升了token级幻觉检测性能，每个组件均对性能提升有持续贡献。

Conclusion: CORTEX 通过对比内部表征实现了有效的token级幻觉检测，验证了文档对grounded token和hallucinated token影响差异的假设，为RAG系统的可靠性提升提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究RAG中的幻觉检测，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。虽然论文主要针对文本RAG场景，但幻觉检测方法可推广至多模态RAG，且该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解与可靠性提升，与本论文高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on RAG hallucination detection, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: token-level hallucination detection, RAG, internal representation comparison, fine-grained localization, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: In this paper, we propose CORTEX, a token-level hallucination detection method for Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). In long-form RAG outputs, hallucinations often arise in localized spans rather than throughout an entire response. CORTEX therefore identifies ungrounded content at the token level, enabling fine-grained localization of hallucinations. The key intuition behind CORTEX is that tokens grounded in retrieved documents should be more strongly influenced by those documents than hallucinated tokens. To capture this document-induced effect, CORTEX compares internal representations of a large language model (LLM) under two conditions: with and without the retrieved documents. Instead of relying solely on each token's immediate sensitivity to the retrieved documents, CORTEX also leverages the propagation of document-grounded information through preceding tokens, reducing false positives for tokens whose evidence has already been absorbed into the context. Finally, CORTEX applies post-processing smoothing step that models the tendency of hallucination labels to persist over contiguous spans, reducing local noise and encouraging span-consistent predictions. Experiments on two RAG benchmarks and three LLMs show that CORTEX substantially improves token-level hallucination detection, with each component consistently contributing to performance gains.

</details>


### [88] [Seeing Is Not Sharing: Some Vision-Language Models Overestimate Common Ground in Asymmetric Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31719)
*Nan Li, Albert Gatt, Massimo Poesio*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究VLM在非对称协作对话中能否区分"可能共享的信息"与"已通过交互建立共同理解的信息"。通过HCRC MapTask对话数据集上的解释匹配任务，发现提供地图图像虽提升整体性能，但导致模型过度预测对齐（将地图内容视为共同理解的证据），且该偏差源于任务相关的地图内容本身而非视觉通道。校准分析和指代链追踪表明模型依赖静态指代线索而非对话历史中的交互式建立过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作对话中，共享感知并不保证共享理解，共同理解需要通过交互逐步建立。现有VLM研究多关注单轮理解，缺乏对对话中共同理解建立过程的建模能力评估。本文旨在探究VLM能否区分"潜在可共享的信息"与"已通过交互建立共享的信息"。

Method: 基于HCRC MapTask对话数据集构建13,077条标注指代表达的解释匹配任务。系统控制对话上下文和地图信息访问（真实地图图像、文本描述、非信息性图像、无图像），评估5个VLM（Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct等）在不同条件下的对齐预测准确性，并进行校准分析和指代链追踪。

Result: 提供真实地图图像提升整体性能，但导致模型过度预测对齐（对非对齐案例准确率下降）。文本描述复现该偏差，非信息性图像则完全抑制对齐预测，表明偏差源于任务相关地图内容而非视觉通道。Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct表现最明显，其他4个模型也有不同程度体现。

Conclusion: VLM倾向于将地图内容（无论视觉还是文本形式）视为共同理解的证据，混淆了"潜在共同理解"与"已建立的共同理解"。模型依赖静态指代线索而非对话历史中的交互式建立过程，揭示了当前VLM在对话式共同理解建模中的根本性局限。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在非对称对话中是否混淆"潜在共同理解"与"已建立的共同理解"，即模型过度预测对齐（over-predicting alignment），属于多模态模型的可靠性/忠实性问题，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM common ground in dialogue, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM common ground, asymmetric dialogue, map task, alignment bias, calibration analysis

Abstract: In collaborative dialogue, shared perception does not guarantee shared interpretation. Mutual understanding must be established through interaction. We investigate whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish what could be shared from what has been shared between dialogue participants through grounding. We formulate this as an interpretation-matching task on 13,077 annotated reference expressions from HCRC MapTask dialogues, and evaluate VLMs under systematically controlled manipulations of dialogue context and map-information access. Our results show that providing authentic map images improves overall performance but shifts models toward over-predicting alignment. Textual descriptions of the same map content reproduce this bias, while non-informative images suppress alignment predictions entirely, indicating that the bias is driven by task-relevant map content, not the visual channel. This improvement comes at the cost of degraded accuracy on non-aligned cases. Calibration analysis and reference-chain tracking further suggest that models rely on static referential cues on the maps rather than tracking how grounding unfolds through dialogue history. We observe these patterns most clearly in Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct and, to varying degrees, in four additional models from two architecture families. In models that exhibit the bias, map content, whether presented visually or textually, is treated as evidence of mutual understanding, conflating potential with established common ground.

</details>


### [89] [Visual Semantic Entropy: Do Vision Language Models Recognize Visual Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31407)
*Ta Duc Huy, Trang Nguyen, Townim Chowdhury, Ankit Yadav, Minh-Son To, Zhibin Liao, Johan W. Verjans, Vu Minh Hieu Phan*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (59.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉语义熵（VSE），通过仅扰动图像输入来探测视觉语言模型对视觉模糊性的不确定性，解决了传统语义熵在过置信视觉嵌入下失效的问题，在多个VLM和VQA基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在视觉模糊输入上可能产生过度自信的错误回答，现有基于输出多样性的不确定性估计方法（如语义熵SE）因过置信视觉嵌入抑制了输出多样性而失效；而基于文本或图文联合扰动的方法引入的变异性主要来自文本变化而非视觉证据，无法准确反映视觉模糊性。

Method: 提出Visual Semantic Entropy (VSE)：仅对图像进行扰动（如数据增强），保持文本查询固定，探测视觉邻近变化；将生成的回答聚类为语义原型，计算质量加权分散度作为不确定性度量。

Result: 在5个现代VLM（LLaVA-NeXT、InternVL2等）和5个VQA基准上的实验表明，VSE有效捕捉视觉模糊性，在不确定性估计任务上达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 仅扰动图像而非文本的不确定性估计方法能更准确地反映视觉模糊性，VSE为VLM的可靠性评估提供了有效工具。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型在视觉模糊输入下的不确定性估计，属于多模态模型的可靠性/忠实性问题，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）这一规范主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses VLM uncertainty estimation, but is not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: VLM uncertainty, visual ambiguity, semantic entropy, input perturbation, VQA benchmarks

Abstract: Vision-language models can produce confident answers on visually ambiguous inputs, resulting in biased predictions. Common entropy-based methods, such as Semantic Entropy (SE), rely on output diversity. Yet our analysis shows that overconfident visual embeddings suppress output diversity under stochastic decoding, causing SE to underestimate uncertainty in such cases. Recent methods instead probe output diversity through input perturbations, including textual paraphrasing or joint text-image perturbations, and show improved performance. We study these approaches and reveals that the resulting variability is often dominated by textual changes rather than visual evidence, causing uncertainty estimates to reflect prompt sensitivity rather than visual ambiguity. We therefore propose Visual Semantic Entropy (VSE), which perturbs only the image to probe nearby visual variations while keeping the text query fixed. VSE measures uncertainty by clustering generated answers into semantic prototypes and computing the mass-weighted dispersion among them. Extensive evaluation across five modern vision-language models and five diverse VQA benchmarks demonstrates that VSE effectively captures visual ambiguity, establishing a new state-of-the-art for VLM uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [90] [CoMet: Context and Multiplicity Decomposition for Multimodal Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32012)
*Sanghyuk Chun, William Yang, Amaya Dharmasiri, Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (75.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CoMet 提出一种多模态大模型不确定性估计方法，通过将不确定性分解为上下文特定项和多样性特定项，并训练轻量级后验模块来高效估计，无需自回归生成或重复采样，在开放集多模态基准、幻觉检测和 VQA 任务上持续优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型的不确定性来源多样（不同模态及其关系），且开放集回答空间无界，现有不确定性估计方法效率低（需多次采样或自回归生成），亟需一种高效且能分解不确定性来源的方法。

Method: 提出 CoMet，将不确定性分解为上下文特定项（context-specific，由任务/提示引起的歧义）和多样性特定项（multiplicity-specific，给定上下文下与输入兼容的合理答案数量）。训练一个轻量级后验不确定性模块来估计这两项，无需自回归生成或重复采样。

Result: 在多个开放集多模态基准、幻觉检测和多项选择 VQA 任务上，CoMet 在保持高效性的同时，持续优于现有不确定性估计基线方法。

Conclusion: CoMet 通过分解不确定性来源并采用轻量级后验模块，实现了高效且有效的多模态不确定性估计，为提升 MLLM 的可靠性和可信度提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型的不确定性估计，旨在提升模型对自身不确定性的认知（"知道不知道"），这与多模态幻觉与可靠性（multimodal_hallucination）直接相关——不确定性估计是缓解幻觉、提升可靠性的核心方法之一。该 subtopic 是 [CANONICAL] 条目，优先选用。

Importance reason: Addresses MLLM uncertainty estimation, a niche reliability topic, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal uncertainty estimation, Post-hoc lightweight module, Hallucination detection, No autoregressive sampling needed, Open-ended VQA benchmarks

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation has been a long-standing challenge in AI models; it amounts to "knowing what you don't know," and metacognition is notoriously difficult even for humans (cf. the Dunning-Kruger effect). Although it is still far from solved even in simpler classification systems, tackling it in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is becoming increasingly important. Within MLLMs, uncertainty can stem from any of the diverse sources as well as from their relationships, and further can stem from the unbounded answers in the open-ended setting. To tackle the issues, we propose CoMet, an MLLM uncertainty estimation method by decomposing uncertainty into a context-specific term and a multiplicity-specific term. The former captures ambiguity induced by the given context (e.g., task or prompt), while the latter captures how many plausible answers determined by the context remain compatible with the given input. We train a lightweight post-hoc uncertainty module to estimate these quantities, which enables efficient uncertainty estimation without autoregressive answer generation or repeated sampling. Experiments on various open-ended multimodal benchmarks, hallucination detection, and multiple-choice visual question answering benchmarks show that CoMet consistently improves uncertainty estimation over existing baselines while remaining efficient in practice. Code is available at

</details>
