### [91] [ReGRPO: Reflection-Augmented Policy Optimization for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31392)
*Binjie Zhang, Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (60.28) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ReGRPO 提出了一种反思增强的组相对策略优化框架，通过构造 Reflection-of-Thought 三元组（错误类型、证据、修复计划）进行 SFT 预热，再结合局部轨迹的组相对优势优化反思 token 与纠正动作，并引入反思代价项减少不必要的反思，在 GTA 和 GAIA 基准上取得了最佳开源结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型 VLM 存在两个问题：1) SFT 主要基于成功轨迹，对工具调用失败后的恢复信号不足；2) 稀疏的轨迹级 RL 奖励无法定位具体哪一步失败以及如何修复。因此需要一种能提供细粒度反思与纠正信号的学习框架。

Method: 1) 构建反思数据引擎：执行 near-miss 动作收集失败观测，构造 Reflection-of-Thought 三元组（ErrorType, Evidence, FixPlan）配对纠正动作，用于 SFT 预热。2) 在局部轨迹内使用组相对优势（group-relative advantages）联合优化反思 token 和纠正动作。3) 引入反思代价项（reflection-cost term）抑制不必要的反思。

Result: 在 GTA 和 GAIA 基准上，使用相同骨干网络和工具套件，ReGRPO 一致优于强开源基线，在对比的开源控制器中取得了最佳结果。

Conclusion: ReGRPO 通过反思增强的组相对策略优化有效提升了工具增强型 VLM 在失败场景下的恢复能力，验证了结构化反思数据与细粒度 RL 优化相结合的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ReGRPO，一种基于反思增强的组相对策略优化方法，用于工具增强型 VLM 的强化学习训练。核心是使用 GRPO 对 VLM 进行策略优化，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"。

Importance reason: Strong RL alignment method for tool-using VLMs, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Reflection-augmented RL, tool-using VLM, group relative policy optimization, SFT warm-start, GTA/GAIA benchmarks

Abstract: Tool-augmented vision-language models (VLMs) can solve multimodal, multi-step tasks by calling external tools, yet they remain fragile in practice. Existing works have two common gaps. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is built mostly on successful trajectories and offers little signal for recovery after tool failures, while sparse trajectory-level RL rewards provide limited guidance on which step failed and how to repair it. We introduce ReGRPO (Reflection-augmented Group Relative Policy Optimization), a framework that learns reflection-guided correction in tool-using agents. ReGRPO starts with a structured reflective data engine: we execute near-miss actions to collect grounded failure observations, then build Reflection-of-Thought triplets (ErrorType, Evidence, FixPlan) paired with corrected actions for warm-start SFT. We then optimize reflection tokens and corrective actions jointly within local trajectories using group-relative advantages, and include a reflection-cost term to reduce unnecessary reflection. Experiments on GTA and GAIA show that, under the same backbone and tool suite, ReGRPO consistently outperforms strong open-source baselines and achieves the best results among the compared open-source controllers. Code and RoT data are available at .

</details>


### [92] [One Reflection Is Not Enough: Self-Correcting Autonomous Research via Multi-Hypothesis Failure Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31478)
*Jie Ma, Binfei Chu, Jie Gao, Jinlu Zhang, Yiwei Ma, Yi Tan, Jiayi Ji, Xiaoshuai Sun, Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (83.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（Self-correcting, Autonomous, Grounded Experimenter），一种自主科研智能体，通过多假设失败归因（MHFA）机制在实验失败时进行结构化因果诊断，将失败根因路由到正确的干预层级（假设、实验设计或实现），并采用基于实际测量值的约束报告机制防止幻觉。在12个主题5个领域的基准上，SAGE将指标产出率从42%提升至92%，人工评分从5.00提升至6.75/10，并超越AI-Scientist-v2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主科研智能体在实验失败时通常依赖单一的自由形式反思（free-form reflection），将丰富的轨迹信息压缩为一条文字批评，导致要么陷入局部试错，要么丢弃有用上下文进行大幅转向。这种脆弱的失败恢复机制严重限制了自主科研的可靠性。

Method: SAGE的核心是Multi-Hypothesis Failure Attribution（MHFA）机制，它将失败恢复视为结构化因果诊断：通过分析动态轨迹特征，系统性地生成多个基于证据的失败解释，独立评估其严重性，并将验证后的根因确定性地路由到正确的干预层级（假设、实验设计或实现）。此外，SAGE采用基于实际测量值的约束报告机制，明确限制草稿结果只能使用实际测量值，删除幻觉数字。

Result: 在12个主题5个领域的基准上，SAGE将指标产出率从42%提升至92%，人工评分的工件质量从5.00提升至6.75/10，并盲评超越AI-Scientist-v2（52.0 vs 48.2），增益主要集中在代码开发和执行环节。

Conclusion: 通过将结构化恢复与显式约束报告相结合，SAGE显著优于单一反思范式，为未来自主科研建立了更可靠的信任基础。完全自主的科研写作和生成会议级论文仍然是整个领域的公开难题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SAGE自主科研智能体，核心是Multi-Hypothesis Failure Attribution（多假设失败归因）机制，用于在实验失败时进行结构化因果诊断和自我纠正。虽然论文本身不直接涉及多模态对齐或RLHF，但其核心关注的是自主科研智能体的可靠性、自我纠正与对齐（确保结果真实可信），这与多模态强化学习与对齐方向下的"对齐"主题（确保模型行为符合预期、可靠）有合理的关联。此外，论文也涉及多模态理解与生成方向下的自主科研能力。

Importance reason: Strong structured failure recovery for autonomous research agents, but only tangentially touches user's priority subtopics. Signals: Multi-hypothesis failure attribution, autonomous research agent, self-correction, grounded reporting, AI scientist improvement

Abstract: Autonomous research agents can now draft hypotheses, write code, run experiments, and produce papers, but they remain brittle when experiments fail. Under the prevailing paradigm, failure recovery is usually delegated to a single free-form reflection: a rich trajectory of metrics, logs, and design choices is compressed into one verbal critique, which often leads either to localized trial-and-error or to hard pivots that discard useful context. We propose SAGE, a Self-correcting, Autonomous, Grounded Experimenter, to tackle this failure-recovery bottleneck. Its core mechanism, Multi-Hypothesis Failure Attribution (MHFA), treats recovery as a structured causal diagnosis. By analyzing dynamic trajectory features, MHFA systematically generates multiple evidence-grounded explanations for a failure, independently evaluates their severity, and deterministically routes the verified root cause to the correct intervention level (hypothesis, experimental design, or implementation). To guarantee scientific honesty, SAGE further employs a grounded reporting mechanism that explicitly constrains drafted results to actual measured values, redacting hallucinated numbers. On a 12-topic, 5-domain benchmark, SAGE increases metrics-bearing outputs from 42% to 92% over a reflection baseline, improves artifact quality from 5.00 to 6.75/10, and blindly outscores AI-Scientist-v2 (52.0 vs. 48.2), with gains concentrated in code development and execution. While fully autonomous scientific writing and generating conference-ready papers remain notoriously difficult open problems for the entire field, SAGE successfully produces significantly more reliable and higher-quality scientific artifacts. Ultimately, by coupling structured recovery with explicit grounding constraints, SAGE significantly outperforms monolithic reflection paradigms, establishing a highly trustworthy foundation for future autonomous research.

</details>


### [93] [UniCoder: Unified Visual-to-Code Generation via Symbolic Rewards and Reference-Guided Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31732)
*Yaozhi Zheng, Yilei Jiang, Manyuan Zhang, Yuxuan Wan, Kaituo Feng, Tianshuo Peng, Bo Zhang, Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniCoder 是一个统一的强化学习框架，通过符号属性对齐（Symbolic Attribute Alignment）和参考引导代码优化（Reference-Guided Code Optimization）解决视觉到代码生成中奖励粗糙和探索停滞的问题，在多个基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 MLLM 通过 SFT 难以实现像素级精确的视觉到代码生成，而 RL 的应用面临奖励粗糙（语义指标无法惩罚细粒度元素偏差）和探索停滞（稀疏异构代码搜索空间导致策略无法启动有效轨迹）两大障碍。

Method: 提出 UniCoder 框架：(1) 符号属性对齐（SAA），使用轻量辅助 LLM 将生成代码解析为离散视觉属性（如十六进制颜色、坐标范围），实现密集的逐元素奖励计算；(2) 参考引导代码优化（RGCO），动态将真实轨迹注入低性能 rollout 组，将盲探索转化为引导式策略改进。

Result: 在 ChartMimic、UniSVG、Design2Code 和 ScreenBench 基准上，8B 参数模型超越所有开源基线，达到与闭源模型相当的 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: UniCoder 通过符号奖励和参考引导优化有效解决了视觉到代码生成中的 RL 训练难题，为通用视觉到代码合成建立了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 UniCoder 框架，使用强化学习（RL）和符号奖励来优化 MLLM 的视觉到代码生成任务，核心是多模态 RL 与对齐方法，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）。

Importance reason: Strong on code generation but not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: visual-to-code generation, reinforcement learning, symbolic reward, reference-guided optimization, MLLM fine-tuning

Abstract: Visual-to-Code generation, which transforms scientific plots, vector graphics, and webpages into executable scripts, demands a level of pixel-precise alignment that standard Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) fail to achieve through Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) alone. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers a theoretical pathway to bridge this gap, its application is hindered by two fundamental obstacles: (1) \textit{Reward Coarseness}, where semantic metrics like CLIP scores fail to penalize fine-grained element deviations, and (2) \textit{Exploration Stagnation}, where the sparse, heterogeneous code search space prevents the policy from bootstrapping valid trajectories. To overcome these limitations, we introduce UniCoder, a unified RL framework that integrates two novel mechanisms. First, we propose \textbf{Symbolic Attribute Alignment}, which employs a lightweight auxiliary LLM to parse generated code into discrete visual attributes (e.g., hex colors, coordinate limits), enabling dense, element-wise reward computation. Second, to escape local optima, we devise \textbf{Reference-Guided Code Optimization}, a strategy that dynamically injects ground-truth trajectories into low-performing rollout groups, transforming blind exploration into guided policy improvement. Extensive experiments on ChartMimic, UniSVG, Design2Code and ScreenBench benchmarks demonstrate that our 8B-parameter model not only surpasses all open-source baselines but also achieves state-of-the-art performance comparable to proprietary models, establishing a new paradigm for generalized visual-to-code synthesis.

</details>

### [94] [ViTL: Temporal Logic-Guided Zero-Shot Natural Language Navigation via Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30696)
*Kaier Liang, Hengde Dai, Cristian-Ioan Vasile*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ViTL提出一个零样本长时序自然语言导航框架，利用LLM将指令编译为LTL公式并转换为DFA，结合VLM的方向评分机制，在HM3D上实现了带时序约束的多目标导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航方法仅支持单目标任务，无法处理包含时序和逻辑约束的多目标自然语言指令（如"先清洁椅子或沙发，然后打开电视"），亟需一种能理解并执行长时序多目标导航任务的零样本框架。

Method: ViTL采用双层架构：任务层使用LLM将自然语言指令编译为LTL公式，再转换为DFA来协调多通道价值图并触发动态重规划；导航层引入方向评分机制，在观测图像上标注前沿方向并从VLM中提取每个方向的分值，实现方向感知的导航决策。

Result: 在HM3D数据集上的实验表明，ViTL框架能够零样本完成带时序约束的长时序自然语言导航任务，且方向评分机制相比基线方法提升了单目标导航的准确率和效率。

Conclusion: ViTL通过结合LLM的时序逻辑编译能力和VLM的视觉导航能力，首次实现了零样本长时序多目标自然语言导航，为机器人理解复杂指令并执行多步任务提供了有效框架。

Classification reason: 该论文使用VLM和LLM将自然语言指令编译为LTL公式，实现零样本长时序多目标导航，涉及多模态推理（视觉+语言+时序逻辑推理），属于多模态推理与思维链范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics navigation with temporal logic, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: zero-shot navigation, temporal logic, vision-language model, long-horizon tasks, HM3D benchmark

Abstract: Enabling robots to follow natural language commands to complete zero-shot long-horizon tasks remains challenging. It requires extracting implicit temporal and logical constraints from natural language commands and executing multiple sub-tasks accordingly. Recent zero-shot object navigation methods use vision-language models (VLMs) to guide frontier-based exploration in unknown environments, but they are limited to single-target tasks. Real-world commands such as "Clean either the chair or the couch, then turn on the tv." require navigating to multiple targets in a temporally constrained order, which no existing zero-shot system can handle. We present ViTL, a framework that addresses this gap at two levels. At the task level, we use a large language model (LLM) to compile natural language commands into Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas, which are then converted into Deterministic Finite Automata~(DFA) that coordinate multi-channel value maps and trigger dynamic replanning when new objects are detected. At the navigation level, we introduce directional score: rather than producing a direction-agnostic value across the entire field of view, we label frontier directions on the observation image and extract per-direction scores from the VLM. Experiments on Habitat-Matterport 3D (HM3D) show that the full framework enables zero-shot long-horizon completion of natural language navigation tasks with temporal constraints, and that directional score improves single-target navigation accuracy and efficiency over the baseline.

</details>


### [95] [Knowledge Distillation from Large Reasoning Models to Compact Student Models: A Case Study on the John O Bryan Mathematics Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31048)
*Gaurab Baral, Aaditya Khanal, Yangyang Tao, Junxiu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究从大推理模型（DeepSeek-R1）向紧凑学生模型（Qwen2.5-7B）进行知识蒸馏。利用John O'Bryan数学竞赛历史题目构建CoT训练语料，通过LoRA微调。学生模型准确率从64.67%提升至69.43%，并在MATH-500上达到73.1%。同时发现响应长度与推理质量呈正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1）虽然推理能力强，但计算成本高、部署困难。如何将大模型的推理能力高效蒸馏到紧凑模型中，使其在保持较低计算开销的同时获得显著的推理能力提升，是实际部署中的关键问题。

Method: 使用双智能体框架（DeepSeek-R1作为教师生成CoT推理过程，GPT-4o作为验证器）构建数学竞赛题目的CoT训练语料。基于MLX框架在Apple Silicon上使用LoRA对Qwen2.5-7B进行参数高效微调。通过多轮独立训练评估结果稳定性，并设计六个推理级别（R1-R6）研究响应长度对推理质量的影响。

Result: 基础Qwen2.5-7B准确率64.67%，教师DeepSeek-R1为91.40%。微调后学生模型平均准确率69.43%（提升4.76个百分点），在MATH-500上达73.1%。响应长度从R1（均值220词）到R6（均值31.2词）时，准确率从69.43%降至41.9%，表明响应长度是数学推理质量的关键因素。

Conclusion: CoT蒸馏能有效提升紧凑学生模型的数学推理能力，响应长度与推理质量呈正相关。LoRA微调在Apple Silicon上可行且高效。研究为将大推理模型能力迁移到可部署的小模型提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 论文研究从大推理模型（DeepSeek-R1）向小模型（Qwen2.5-7B）蒸馏Chain-of-Thought推理能力，聚焦数学竞赛推理任务，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴下的推理蒸馏与数学推理。

Importance reason: Focuses on math reasoning distillation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Knowledge distillation, Chain-of-Thought, LoRA fine-tuning, Math reasoning, Response length analysis

Abstract: This paper investigates knowledge distillation from a large reasoning model (DeepSeek-R1) to a compact student model (Qwen2.5-7B). Using historical problems from the John O'Bryan Mathematics Competition at Northern Kentucky University (2011-2025), we build a Chain-of-Thought (CoT) training corpus through a dual-agent framework. The dataset is used to fine-tune the student model with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on Apple Silicon hardware using the MLX framework. The base Qwen2.5-7B model achieves 64.67% accuracy on competition problems, while the DeepSeek-R1 teacher achieves 91.40%. An initial 1,000-iteration training run revealed severe overfitting, with validation loss reaching a minimum at iteration 200 before rising steadily. Based on this finding, we ran five independent training runs each limited to 200 iterations with varied random seeds to assess result stability. Across these five runs, the fine-tuned student model achieves a mean accuracy of 69.43% (std dev 0.17%) on the competition dataset, a 4.76 percentage-point improvement over the base model, and generalizes to 73.1% (std dev 0.18%) on the MATH-500 benchmark. We further study how response length affects answer quality across six reasoning levels (R1-R6): accuracy declines consistently from 69.43% at R1 (mean 220 words) to 41.9% at R6 (mean 31.2 words), with the two-person speed section most sensitive to token reduction. These results demonstrate that CoT distillation improves compact student models and that response length is a critical factor in mathematical reasoning quality.

</details>


### [96] [Spatial Reasoning via Modality Switching Between Language and Symbolic Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31285)
*Shreya Rajpal, Tanawan Premsri, Parisa Kordjamshidi*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可信度与复杂度的切换度量，让 LLM 在空间推理任务中动态选择使用自然语言推理还是网格/布局等结构化符号表示，实验表明切换到网格表示可提升最多 42% 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在复杂推理时往往借助外部符号（如画图、画网格）来辅助思考，而现有 LLM 通常仅依赖自然语言推理。作者希望探索在空间推理中引入结构化模态（如网格/布局）是否能提升推理效果，并让模型自主决定何时切换模态。

Method: 提出一种基于可信度（trustworthiness）和复杂度（complexity）信号的切换度量（switching metric），用于估计何时将空间故事（spatial story）转换为网格/布局等结构化表示更有利于推理。在多种空间推理任务上，对比纯语言推理与切换到网格表示后的性能差异。

Result: 在空间推理任务中，从自然语言推理切换到网格表示可提升 LLM 性能最高达 42%，验证了模态选择对推理结果的重要影响。

Conclusion: 模态选择在 LLM 推理中至关重要，结构化符号表示能显著提升空间推理能力，为未来实现原则性的模态选择提供了初步探索。

Classification reason: 该论文研究空间推理中的模态切换（语言 vs 符号/网格表示），属于多模态推理范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度相关。

Importance reason: Explores modality switching for spatial reasoning, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: spatial reasoning, modality switching, grid representation, LLM reasoning, trustworthiness metric

Abstract: Human reasoning is inherently multimodal: when problems become difficult, we rarely think in words alone. We often externalize our reasoning by sketching diagrams or drawing grids to understand the underlying conceptual structure and avoid mistakes. Building on this premise, our research investigates: (a) whether grounding multi-hop textual-spatial stories into geometry-aware modalities, such as layouts or grids, improves reasoning compared to natural language-based inference; and (b) whether a model can decide when to rely on natural language reasoning and when to switch to a structured modality. We address these questions by introducing a switching metric based on trustworthiness and complexity signals, which estimates when grounding a spatial story into structure is likely to improve performance. This takes a first step toward principled modality selection in Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning. Across our settings, switching from natural language-based reasoning to a grid-based representation improves LLM performance by up to 42\%, highlighting the importance of modality choice in shaping reasoning outcomes.

</details>


### [97] [Decodable Is Not Grounded: A Vision-Ablation Arbiter for VLM Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31257)
*Chih-Ting Liao, Fei Shen, Xin Cao, Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (91.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单的空白图像对照方法（Vision-Ablation Arbiter），揭示线性探针和 steering recovery 会系统性地高估 VLM 对图像的空间推理 grounding。在 14 个 VLM 上发现水平轴是 grounded（依赖视觉且正确），垂直轴是 prior（不依赖视觉），深度轴是 inverted（可解码但符号错误导致低于随机水平），并展示了不同几何轴所需的修正复杂度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（线性探针、steering recovery）声称能从 VLM 的 latent 中解码出空间知识，但无法区分模型是真正从图像中感知到的（grounded），还是仅依赖语言先验（prior），甚至是符号反转的（inverted）。作者希望设计一种简单、无需训练的对照方法，来严格区分这三种 grounding 状态。

Method: 提出 Vision-Ablation Arbiter：将图像替换为灰色空白，比较正常图像与空白图像下探针解码的正确率。如果解码正确率在空白图像下显著下降，则为 grounded；如果保持不变，则为 prior；如果空白图像下反而上升（从低于随机变为随机或更高），则为 inverted。在 14 个 VLM（涵盖 6 个语言模型家族，2B-27B）上对水平、垂直、深度三个空间轴进行系统评测。

Result: 水平轴在所有模型上均为 grounded（依赖视觉且正确）；垂直轴为 prior（不依赖视觉，解码正确率来自语言先验）；深度轴为 inverted（可解码但符号错误，模型得分低于随机水平）。inversion 现象在模型规模增大时出现。训练无关的旋转操作可在最干净的模型上修正 inversion，而分布式 inversion 需要低秩编辑修正。

Conclusion: 线性探针和 steering 方法会系统性地高估 VLM 对图像的 grounding，空白图像对照是一种廉价、自校准的检验方法，应作为 VLM latent knowledge 和 steering 研究的默认控制实验。该工作揭示了 grounding、prior 和 inverted 三种不同的感知状态，对 VLM 空间推理能力的评估方法提出了重要修正。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLM 的空间推理能力，通过空白图像对照实验揭示 probing/steering 方法高估了模型对图像的真正 grounding，属于多模态视觉推理范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "多模态推理与思维链" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong methodological contribution for VLM spatial reasoning evaluation, but not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: VLM spatial reasoning, grounding evaluation, linear probe overestimation, vision-ablation arbiter, inverted perception

Abstract: The standard way to read latent knowledge out of a model, a linear probe confirmed by a steering recovery, can systematically overstate what a vision-language model (VLM) actually grounds in the image. We show this on spatial reasoning, where the error is invisible to both probing and steering yet exposed by a one-line causal control: replacing the image with a gray blank. Probes decode the within-axis answer at 73--97% across axes, and a training-free projection lifts a near-chance axis from 59% to 79%, exactly the signature of unlocking latent knowledge. The blank-image arbiter refutes it, revealing three grounding regimes that probing conflates: an axis can be grounded (vision-dependent, correct), a prior (vision-independent, with its decode and its apparent recovery a directional default rather than perception), or, surprisingly, inverted: decodable, causally controllable, but deployed with the wrong sign, so the model scores below chance and the error requires looking. The taxonomy holds across the studied VLMs: in fourteen models spanning six language-model families and 2B--27B, horizontal is grounded, vertical is a prior, and depth is inverted, with the inversion emerging at scale within families. The decode-versus-deploy inversion replicates on seven of eight models across five families, and the minimal edit that re-deploys it varies with geometry: a training-free rotation matches a trained edit on the cleanest model, while distributed inversions need a trained low-rank edit, tracing a per-model correction-complexity spectrum. The cheap, self-calibrating arbiter cleanly separates grounded perception, inverted perception, and prior substitution; we argue it should be a default control for latent-knowledge and steering claims in VLMs.

</details>


### [98] [Evo-PI: Aligning Medical Reasoning via Evolving Principle-Guided Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31800)
*Xianda Zheng, Huan Gao, Meng-Fen Chiang, Michael Witbrock, Kaiqi Zhao, Shangyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (70.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Evo-PI 提出一种原则驱动的学习框架，通过可演化的语言化原则作为监督信号，替代静态奖励/规则，使 MLLM 的推理能力在动态对齐循环中持续提升。在医学 VQA 的 8 个基准上取得最高 24.6% 的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 的推理能力受限于静态监督（固定提示、规则或奖励模型），这些信号无法自适应地指导模型的推理过程，导致泛化能力脆弱且在复杂决策任务中性能饱和。需要一种能够动态适应模型推理缺陷的监督范式。

Method: Evo-PI 将推理原则视为显式的、语言化的可演化监督信号。框架包含一个共演化循环：原则指导模型推理，模型行为反过来优化监督原则。具体包括原则生成、原则评估和原则迭代演化三个核心步骤，在医学 VQA 场景中实现结构化视觉-文本推理的对齐。

Result: 在 8 个医学 VQA 基准和多个模型骨干上，Evo-PI 一致提升了推理准确率，最高提升达 24.6%。

Conclusion: 可演化的原则引导监督为训练 MLLM 的专家对齐推理提供了一种可扩展且通用的范式，能够有效克服静态监督的局限性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Evo-PI 框架，通过可演化的原则引导监督来提升 MLLM 的推理能力，并在医学 VQA 任务上验证。核心关注多模态推理（尤其是视觉-文本推理）的改进，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical reasoning alignment, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: medical VQA, principle-guided supervision, MLLM reasoning, static supervision limitation, co-evolutionary loop

Abstract: Despite recent progress, the reasoning capabilities of large multimodal language models (MLLMs) remain fundamentally constrained by static supervision, where fixed prompts, rules, or reward models provide non-adaptive guidance throughout training. Such static signals are often sufficient to enforce output formats, but fail to shape the underlying reasoning process, leading to brittle generalization and performance saturation in complex decision-making tasks. We propose Evo-PI, a principle-centric learning framework that treats reasoning principles as explicit, language-based supervision signals that can be generated, evaluated, and iteratively evolved. Instead of relying on fixed rewards, Evo-PI enables a co-evolutionary loop in which principles guide model reasoning, while model behaviors in turn refine the principles that supervise them. This dynamic alignment mechanism allows supervision to progressively adapt to the model's reasoning deficiencies. We instantiate Evo-PI in medical visual question answering as a high-stakes testbed requiring structured visual-textual reasoning. Across eight benchmarks and multiple model backbones, Evo-PI consistently improves reasoning accuracy, achieving gains of up to 24.6%. Our results suggest that evolving principle-guided supervision offers a scalable and general paradigm for training expert-aligned reasoning in MLLMs. Code is available at .

</details>


### [99] [Token-Sparse Medical Multimodal Reasoning via Dual-Stream Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31599)
*Kaitao Chen, Weiqian Zhao, Jiamin Wu, Qihao Zheng, Shangquan Sun, Chunfeng Song, Xiaosong Wang, Mu Zhou, Mianxin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (42.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ViToS，一个双流强化学习框架，通过视觉 token 剪枝（VTP）和跨反馈顺序优化，在医学多模态推理中实现 token 稀疏化，将视觉 token 减少至原长的 77%，同时在多个医学基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像通常包含极其稀疏的视觉证据，大量无关视觉 token 会干扰 VLM 的推理。现有方法缺乏统一的强化学习框架来同时实现视觉 token 剪枝和医学多模态推理。

Method: 提出 ViToS 双流强化学习框架：一个策略模型包含两个任务分支——一个专注于 grounding（定位相关区域），另一个在 VTP 后进行 token 稀疏推理。通过跨反馈顺序优化（cross-feedback sequential optimization）解决耦合策略学习问题，避免梯度冲突并促进共享策略模型收敛。

Result: 在七个医学基准上，视觉 token 减少至原序列长度的 77%，在 Lingshu-7B 上达到 108.27% 的相对性能，在 HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B 上达到 104.16% 的相对性能，同时实现推理加速。

Conclusion: ViToS 通过 token 稀疏化实现了医学多模态推理的性能提升和推理加速，为医学多模态推理建立了高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出双流强化学习框架 ViToS，通过视觉 token 剪枝增强医学多模态推理，属于多模态推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" (multimodal_reasoning_cot)。

Importance reason: Medical domain focus and token pruning technique are not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Medical multimodal reasoning, Token pruning for efficiency, Dual-stream RL, Cross-feedback optimization, Performance improvement on medical benchmarks

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) combining reinforcement learning (RL) ignite remarkable progress in multimodal reasoning, yet still struggle with medical images, which typically exhibit extremely sparse visual evidence to inform clinical decision-making. We recognize that pruning visual tokens outside the grounding region greatly enhances medical reasoning. However, a united RL framework for active visual token pruning (VTP) and medical multimodal reasoning remains unestablished. Here, we propose a dual-stream RL framework, ViToS, to fulfill token pruning and question answering. ViToS trains one policy model with two task branches, where one focuses on grounding while the other conducts token-sparse reasoning after VTP. Furthermore, we solve the coupled policy learning problem by introducing the cross-feedback sequential optimization, avoiding gradient conflict and facilitating convergence of the shared policy model. Evaluated on seven medical benchmarks, our method reduces visual tokens to 77% of the original sequence length while achieving a 108.27% relative performance on Lingshu-7B and 104.16% relative performance on HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B. Overall, ViToS delivers superior performance and inference speedup, establishing an efficient paradigm for medical multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [100] [Breaking Failure Cascades: Step-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Medical Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31825)
*Junha Jung, Minbyul Jeong, Suhyeon Lim, Sungwook Jung, Jaehoon Yun, Taeyun Roh, Mujeen Sung, Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (62.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MRPO（Medical Reasoning-aware Policy Optimization），一种面向医学多模态推理的步骤感知强化学习算法。通过为推理过程中的每个步骤分配过程奖励，并对早期错误步骤施加指数级惩罚，有效阻断级联错误传播。在多个 MLLM 骨干上，MRPO 显著优于 GRPO 等基线，将早期推理失败率从 64.0% 降至 13.0%，并在 Qwen3-VL-8B 上超越 HuatuoGPT-Vision-34B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 后训练管线主要依赖最终答案正确性或序列级偏好进行优化，存在稀疏奖励分配问题，难以优化临床应用中至关重要的推理过程。作者发现早期推理阶段的级联错误是医学 VQA 中错误预测的主要原因，因此需要一种能够细粒度地识别和纠正推理过程中错误步骤的方法。

Method: 提出 MRPO 算法，在强化学习框架中引入步骤级过程奖励。当最终答案错误时，MRPO 对早期无效推理步骤中的 token 分配指数级增大的惩罚，从而阻断失败级联而不影响成功的推理路径。该方法结合了过程奖励建模与策略优化，实现了对推理过程的细粒度监督。

Result: 在三个多模态 LLM 骨干上，MRPO 一致优于标准 GRPO 和近期 RL 基线。在 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 上，MRPO 甚至超越更大的医学 MLLM（如 HuatuoGPT-Vision-34B）2.79 分。早期推理失败率从 64.0% 降至 13.0%，显著提升了推理质量和最终答案准确率。

Conclusion: 步骤感知的强化学习能够有效缓解医学多模态推理中的级联错误问题，通过细粒度的过程奖励分配，可以同时提升推理质量和最终答案准确性。该方法为临床应用中需要可靠推理过程的场景提供了有效解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向医学多模态推理的步骤级过程奖励强化学习方法（MRPO），核心是优化多模态推理过程、缓解级联错误，属于多模态推理范畴。映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot），该 subtopic 涵盖多模态 CoT、视觉推理等方向，与本文的医学 VQA 推理优化高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical domain and outcome-centric RL, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Medical multimodal reasoning, Step-aware RL, Process reward, Error cascade, Clinical VQA

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models have shown great promise in clinical image reasoning, but existing post-training pipelines remain predominantly outcome-centric, relying on final answer correctness or sequence-level preferences. This suffers from sparse credit assignment, making it difficult to optimize the reasoning process essential for clinical applications. Our analysis reveals that cascading errors from early-stage reasoning failures are a leading cause of incorrect predictions in medical visual question answering (VQA) benchmarks. Motivated by this, we propose Medical Reasoning-aware Policy Optimization (MRPO), an RL algorithm that incorporates step-wise process rewards. When the final answer is incorrect, MRPO assigns exponentially larger penalties to tokens in earlier invalid reasoning steps, breaking failure cascades without compromising successful paths. Across three multimodal LLM backbones, MRPO consistently outperforms standard GRPO and a recent RL baseline, and on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct even surpasses substantially larger medical MLLMs such as HuatuoGPT-Vision-34B by 2.79 points. Moreover, MRPO reduces early-stage reasoning failures from 64.0% to 13.0%, showing that targeted mitigation of cascading failures improves both reasoning quality and final answer accuracy. Our code is available at

</details>

### [101] [Thinking Before Retrieving: Robust Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval via Strategic Planning and Self-Criticism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31222)
*Gunho Jung, Jeong-Woo Park, Seon Bin Kim, Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 PEC-CIR，一个无需训练的零样本组合图像检索框架，通过 Planner-Executor-Critic 多阶段推理流水线将查询构建分解为显式约束提取、候选描述生成和约束合规评估，替代传统的单次生成策略，从而减少语义失真并提升检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本组合图像检索方法采用单次生成策略，将参考图像上下文和修改文本融合为统一描述，导致参考属性保留与文本需求整合相互干扰，难以检测或纠正生成过程中的语义失真和遗漏，检索精度受限。

Method: 提出 PEC-CIR 框架，采用 Planner-Executor-Critic 三阶段架构：Planner 从参考图像和修改文本中提取显式约束；Executor 基于约束生成多个候选目标描述；Critic 评估候选描述对约束的符合程度，选择最优描述用于检索。整个过程在冻结的视觉-语言嵌入空间中完成，无需训练。

Result: 在多个组合图像检索基准上，PEC-CIR 显著优于现有零样本方法，检索精度提升明显，且通过多候选生成与自批评机制有效减少了语义失真和错误传播。

Conclusion: 将查询构建重构为分阶段推理过程而非单次输出，能够显式评估候选查询后再进行检索，从而减少生成错误传播，提升检索稳定性与精度，为零样本组合检索提供了有效的新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究组合图像检索（Composed Image Retrieval），属于跨模态/多模态检索范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on composed image retrieval, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions like continuous models or video generation. Signals: zero-shot composed image retrieval, multi-stage reasoning pipeline, training-free framework, retrieval-oriented query construction, constraint compliance evaluation

Abstract: Composed image retrieval requires identifying a target image from a gallery by integrating a reference image with a textual modification instruction. In a training-free zero-shot setting, this task relies on constructing a retrieval-oriented textual query within a frozen vision--language embedding space at inference time. Existing approaches predominantly rely on a single-pass generation strategy that fuses the reference context and modification text into a unified description. This strategy makes it difficult to detect or correct semantic distortions and omissions during generation. Consequently, the preservation of reference attributes and the integration of textual requirements interfere with each other, which degrades retrieval precision. To address these challenges, we introduce PEC-CIR, a training-free framework that structures query construction as a multi-stage reasoning pipeline. The framework operates through a Planner--Executor--Critic architecture where the Planner extracts explicit constraints, the Executor generates multiple candidate target descriptions, and the Critic evaluates these candidates based on constraint compliance. By reframing query construction as a staged inference process instead of a single-pass output, PEC-CIR reduces the propagation of generative errors by explicitly evaluating candidate queries before retrieval, thereby improving retrieval stability.

</details>


### [102] [ALM2Vec: Learning Audio Embeddings for Universal Audio Retrieval with Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30682)
*Fengjie Lu, Chenang Jiang, Jiarui Hai, Helin Wang, Aaron Yee*

Main category: cs.SD, cs.AI, eess.AS

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ALM2Vec 利用预训练的大音频语言模型（LALM）提取通用音频嵌入，实现跨域、跨任务的统一音频检索，支持文本-音频检索、音频问答和方面条件检索等指令感知检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频检索嵌入主要针对音频-字幕匹配优化，难以支持多样化的检索目标和可控检索行为。作者希望利用LALM的音频理解、指令跟随和推理能力，构建一个统一的音频嵌入框架，实现跨域、跨任务和跨用户意图的通用音频检索。

Method: 基于预训练的大音频语言模型（LALM），通过将自然语言指令融入嵌入过程，学习统一的音频嵌入空间。ALM2Vec 不仅支持传统的文本-音频检索，还支持指令感知检索，如音频问答和方面条件检索。

Result: 在标准音频和语音检索基准上取得了有竞争力的性能，同时展示了有前景的组合检索和可控检索能力，验证了其作为统一音频嵌入模型的潜力。

Conclusion: ALM2Vec 证明了从大音频语言模型中提取的嵌入可以有效支持跨域、跨任务和跨用户意图的统一音频检索，为构建通用音频检索系统提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出ALM2Vec，利用大音频语言模型（LALM）学习通用音频嵌入，实现文本-音频检索、指令感知检索等跨模态检索任务，属于多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）范畴，该子主题为[CANONICAL]条目。

Importance reason: Focuses on audio retrieval embeddings, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics of continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: Audio retrieval, Large audio-language model, Instruction-aware retrieval, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Recent advances in language--audio retrieval have been largely driven by contrastive dual-encoder architectures that align audio and text in a shared embedding space. While effective, existing retrieval embeddings are primarily optimized for audio--caption matching, limiting their ability to support diverse retrieval objectives and controllable retrieval behaviors. We present ALM2Vec, a universal audio embedding framework derived from pretrained large audio--language models (LALMs). By transferring the audio understanding, instruction-following, and reasoning capabilities acquired through large-scale multimodal training, ALM2Vec learns a unified embedding space for retrieval across audio domains and task types. Beyond conventional text--audio retrieval, ALM2Vec incorporates natural-language instructions into the embedding process, enabling instruction-aware retrieval for scenarios such as audio question answering and aspect-conditioned retrieval. Experimental results show that ALM2Vec achieves competitive performance on standard audio and speech retrieval benchmarks while exhibiting promising compositional and controllable retrieval capabilities, highlighting its potential as a unified audio embedding model for retrieval across domains, tasks, and user intents.

</details>


### [103] [Unsupervised Data-Efficient Cross-Modal Retrieval with Global-Neighborhood Alignment Hashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31517)
*Runhao Li, Xiaoxu Ma, Zhenyu Weng, Yue Zhang, Guibo Luo, Huiping Zhuang, Zhiping Lin, Yap-Peng Tan*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.IR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 GNAH，一种数据高效的无监督跨模态哈希方法，通过原型锚定全局对齐和对比随机邻域对齐，在少量图像-文本对下学习紧凑二值哈希码，实现高效跨模态检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督跨模态哈希方法通常依赖大规模图像-文本对，数据收集成本高。本文旨在解决数据受限场景下的无监督跨模态哈希检索问题。

Method: 提出 Global-Neighborhood Alignment Hashing (GNAH)，包含两个核心模块：(1) Prototype-Anchored Global Alignment (PAGA) 模块，从连续潜空间中捕获全局结构信息并迁移到二值汉明空间；(2) Contrastive Stochastic Neighborhood Alignment (CSNA) 模块，通过建模随机邻域关系扩展传统成对对比学习，缓解对稀疏成对相关性的过拟合。

Result: 在多个跨模态检索基准上，GNAH 在数据受限设置下一致优于现有无监督跨模态哈希方法，展示了数据高效性。

Conclusion: GNAH 通过全局-邻域对齐策略有效解决了数据受限场景下的无监督跨模态哈希问题，为实际应用提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究无监督跨模态哈希检索（image-text retrieval），属于多模态检索方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索" (multimodal_retrieval)。

Importance reason: Focuses on cross-modal retrieval hashing, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key interests in generation, world models, or continuous representations. Signals: Unsupervised cross-modal hashing, data-efficient retrieval, global-neighborhood alignment, not generative, not continuous language/multimodal

Abstract: Compared to supervised cross-modal hashing (CMH), unsupervised CMH reduces the reliance on manual labeling by learning binary codes from unlabeled image-text pairs. However, existing unsupervised CMH methods often rely on large-scale image-text pairs, which are costly to collect. To address this limitation, we propose Global-Neighborhood Alignment Hashing (GNAH), a novel approach that preserves the semantic structure of vision-language foundation models within a compact binary Hamming space using only a limited number of image-text pairs. Specifically, GNAH captures global structural information from the continuous latent space and transfers it into the binary Hamming space through a Prototype-Anchored Global Alignment module. In addition, GNAH extends conventional pairwise contrastive learning by modeling stochastic neighborhood relationships via a Contrastive Stochastic Neighborhood Alignment module, thereby alleviating overfitting to sparse pairwise correlations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GNAH consistently outperforms existing unsupervised cross-modal retrieval methods under data-constrained settings, offering a practical solution for real-world CMH applications.

</details>

### [104] [RCT: A Robot-Collected Touch-Vision-Language Dataset for Tactile Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31694)
*Jingbo He, Michael Färber, Roberto Calandra*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 RCT，一个机器人采集的触觉-视觉-语言数据集，包含 122 种工业参考材料的 29,279 帧触觉数据。论文揭示了现有评测中因 contact-sequence 重叠导致的数据泄露问题，并证明在 held-out-material 设置下触觉表征泛化性能急剧下降，强调了新材料泛化是触觉感知的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作开放世界物体时，触觉表征需要泛化到未见过的材料。现有触觉数据集和评测协议存在数据泄露问题（同一物理交互的近似重复帧被分到训练和测试集），导致对触觉泛化能力的评估过于乐观。因此需要更严格的评测基准来暴露真实挑战。

Method: 作者使用三个 DIGIT 触觉传感器，对 122 种工业参考材料（7 大类）进行全机器人按压，采集了 29,279 帧触觉数据。每个按压过程被保存为 contact sequence（接触序列）。论文系统分析了 frame-random split 与 contact-sequence-aware split 对评测结果的影响，并设计了 held-out-material 评测协议来评估对未见材料的泛化能力。

Result: 1) 帧级随机划分因 contact-sequence 重叠导致 Recall@1 虚高 17.7 个百分点；2) Held-out-material 设置下 Recall@1 仅 25.1±6.1%；3) 公开的 TVL/HCT 划分同样存在数据泄露，测试序列全部出现在训练集中；4) 均匀采样按压帧可改善对比学习训练效果；5) RCT 训练的嵌入在未见材料上的类别探测性能有所提升。

Conclusion: RCT 数据集和 contact-sequence-aware 评测协议揭示了触觉感知中新材料泛化的核心挑战。现有评测因数据泄露而高估了触觉表征的泛化能力，RCT 为更严格的触觉泛化研究提供了标准化评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个机器人采集的触觉-视觉-语言数据集（RCT），并重点分析了数据泄露（contact-sequence overlap）对评测结果的影响，提出了更严格的 held-out-material 评测协议。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，因此复用该 canonical subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on tactile generalization for robotics, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models and video generation. Signals: tactile dataset, contact-sequence data leakage, held-out-material evaluation, robotics, multimodal understanding

Abstract: For robots manipulating open-world objects, tactile representations must generalize to unseen materials. We introduce RCT (Robotic Contact Tactile), a robot-collected touch-vision-language dataset with 29,279 tactile frames from full robot presses on 122 industrial reference materials in 7 categories, recorded with three DIGIT sensors at multiple contact positions. RCT preserves each press as a contact sequence, enabling held-out evaluation across materials, categories, sensors, contact positions, and contact sequences. Frames from one press are strongly correlated: frame-random splits can place near-duplicate observations of the same physical interaction in both training and test. With the encoder held fixed, removing contact-sequence overlap reduces tactile-to-text Recall@1 by 17.7 percentage points. When materials are additionally held out at training time, performance drops sharply, leaving held-out-material Recall@1 at 25.1 +/- 6.1% averaged over three held-out draws. The public TVL/HCT split shows the same structure: every test contact sequence appears in training, and raw-pixel nearest neighbors recover the correct sequence in 98.3% of cases. Uniformly sampling a press improves contrastive training, and RCT-trained embeddings improve category probes on unseen materials. RCT makes contact-sequence-aware, held-out-material evaluation reproducible and exposes novel-material generalization as a central challenge for robotic tactile perception. The RCT dataset is open-sourced at

</details>


### [105] [PPT-Eval: A Benchmark for Computer-Use Agents on PowerPoint Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31154)
*Apurva Gandhi, Vishwas Suryanarayanan, Raja Hasnain Anwar, Firoz Shaik, Shubhang Desai, Thong Q. Nguyen, Muhammad Taqi Raza, Vishal Chowdhary, Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PPT-Eval是一个包含120个PowerPoint任务的评测基准，用于评估计算机使用智能体在幻灯片创建和编辑场景中的表现。论文设计了基于评分标准的评估框架，支持部分得分和自然语言反馈，与人类判断的Kendall's τ-b相关性达0.77。现有最强模型Claude-4.5-Opus仅取得45%的成功率和57%的平均部分得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻灯片创建和编辑是专业和教育场景中普遍的多模态活动，是测试计算机使用智能体能力的理想场景。然而，现有评测缺乏针对这类复杂多模态任务的标准化基准，且二元成功指标无法捕捉智能体仅部分完成任务的中间进展。

Method: 构建了包含120个PowerPoint任务（跨12个文件）的基准，涵盖内容创建和演示编辑场景并按难度分级。设计了基于评分标准的评估框架，为每个任务创建特定评分标准，对中间步骤给予部分得分，惩罚不必要的更改和不良美学，并提供自然语言反馈。

Result: 该评估框架与人类判断的Kendall's τ-b相关性达0.77。现有前沿智能体在PowerPoint任务上表现不佳，Claude-4.5-Opus仅取得45%的成功率和57%的平均部分得分。

Conclusion: PPT-Eval为计算机使用智能体在复杂多模态任务上的评测提供了有效基准，揭示了现有智能体在幻灯片处理任务上的显著不足，为未来研究提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了PPT-Eval，一个针对PowerPoint任务的计算机使用智能体评测基准，属于多模态智能体评测范畴。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）涵盖多模态评测、新基准与评测协议，与该论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on a narrow benchmark for PowerPoint agents, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: multimodal agent benchmark, partial credit evaluation, low frontier model performance, not generative or continuous, no video/image generation

Abstract: Creating and editing slides is a rich, multimodal activity that is ubiquitous in professional and educational settings, making it an ideal testbed for real-world computer-use agents. Microsoft PowerPoint is among the most widely adopted and feature-rich environments for presentation creation. We introduce PPT-Eval, a benchmark of 120 PowerPoint tasks across 12 files that cover both content creation and presentation editing scenarios, organized by difficulty. A central challenge in this domain is evaluation: tasks are complex, multimodal, and often admit many valid solutions. Moreover, today's agents frequently make only partial progress, which binary success metrics fail to capture. To address this, we design a robust evaluation framework to help create task-specific rubrics for PowerPoint tasks, taking inspiration from and building on past works for rubric-based evaluation. These rubrics award partial credit for intermediate steps, penalize unnecessary changes and poor aesthetics, and provide natural language feedback. This nuanced approach proves highly effective, achieving a Kendall's {\tau}-b correlation of 0.77 with human judgments. We find that existing frontier agents still struggle with solving PowerPoint tasks, with strong models like Claude-4.5-Opus achieving only a 45% success rate and an average partial score of 57%. The benchmark is located at: .

</details>


### [106] [HealthAgentBench: A Unified Benchmark Suite of Realistic Agentic Healthcare Environments for Challenging Frontier AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31179)
*Qianchu Liu, Sheng Zhang, Guanghui Qin, Jeya Maria Jose Valanarasu, Maximilian Rokuss, Mingyu Lu, Timothy Ossowski, Juan Manuel Zambrano Chaves, Cliff Wong, Peniel Argaw, Yashna Hasija, Mu Wei, Wen-wai Yim, Qin Liu, Zilin Jing, Jason Entenmann, Naoto Usuyama, Tristan Naumann, Hoifung Poon*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HealthAgentBench 是一个包含54个医疗智能体任务的统一评测基准，覆盖患者全流程的7个类别，涉及多模态数据。评测发现最强智能体（Codex GPT-5.5）仅达到约42%的成功率，表明该基准具有挑战性，尤其在医学影像和大搜索空间组合推理任务上表现困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体评测缺乏面向真实医疗场景的、涵盖多模态数据与复杂长程推理的统一基准，难以衡量前沿智能体在临床工作流中的实际能力。

Method: 构建了54个端到端医疗智能体任务，覆盖患者就诊全流程的7个类别（如研究建模、医学影像分析等），每个任务提供最小指令，智能体需自主探索原始医疗数据、在复杂环境中操作并执行多步解决方案，最终以任务成功率作为统一评测指标。

Result: 最强且最具成本效益的智能体 Codex GPT-5.5 仅达到约42%的整体成功率。智能体在EHR数据研究建模任务上表现较好，但医学影像任务对Claude Code模型尤其困难，而Codex GPT-5.5展现出初步能力。大搜索空间与组合推理结合的任务对所有当前智能体均具挑战性。

Conclusion: HealthAgentBench 是一个具有挑战性和现实意义的医疗智能体评测基准，当前前沿智能体表现有限，尤其在医学影像和复杂组合推理方面存在显著提升空间，为未来研究提供了明确方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向医疗场景的AI智能体评测基准（HealthAgentBench），包含54个任务、7个类别，涉及多模态数据（EHR、医学影像等）和复杂推理。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，因此直接复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on healthcare agent benchmarks, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Medical domain, benchmark suite, low relevance to core topics, no continuous language or video generation, negative keyword "medical diagnosis" applies

Abstract: As AI agents become increasingly capable of complex, long-horizon reasoning, rigorous and holistic evaluation is essential for measuring progress toward real-world healthcare applications. We introduce HealthAgentBench, a suite of 54 agentic healthcare tasks across 7 categories each with its unique environment. The benchmark suite spans diverse workflows throughout the patient journey and a broad range of modalities. Each task is designed to replicate an end-to-end clinical workflow: given minimal instructions, an agent must explore raw healthcare data, operate within a complex environment, and execute multi-step solutions that go beyond naive prompting. A final task success rate is reported to provide a single, interpretable metric for HealthAgentBench overall performance for each agent. Evaluating frontier agents on HealthAgentBench, we find that overall task success rate remains low, underscoring the difficulty of the suite. The strongest and the most cost effective agent, Codex GPT-5.5, achieves only approximately 42% success rate. Beyond aggregate performance, HealthAgentBench reveals nuanced strengths and weaknesses across task categories. Frontier agents show promise in automatically developing research modeling pipelines over EHR data, but medical imaging remains especially challenging, particularly for Claude Code models, while Codex GPT-5.5 shows emerging capability. Tasks that combine large search spaces with compositional reasoning requirements remain difficult for all current agents. Together, these results suggest that HealthAgentBench provides a challenging and realistic benchmark with substantial room for future progress. We release our benchmark at .

</details>


### [107] [MECoBench: A Systematic Study of Multimodal Agent Collaboration in Embodied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31966)
*Qingyun Liu, Jiwen Zhang, Jingyi Hu, Siyuan Wang, Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.MA, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (73.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MECoBench 是一个面向多模态大模型在具身环境中协作能力的系统性评测基准，涵盖多种真实世界任务、两种协作结构和三种协作模式，实验发现协作通常能提升任务完成度但受协调复杂度制约，通信是协作增益的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在具身智能体方面潜力巨大，但它们在视觉化环境中协作的能力尚未被系统研究，缺乏统一的评测基准来理解多模态具身协作的机制与局限。

Method: 构建了 MECoBench 评测平台，包含多样化的真实世界任务、两种协作结构（并行/层级）和三种协作模式（无通信、共享观察、共享观察+通信），并在多种 MLLM 上进行了系统实验。

Result: 协作通常提升具身任务完成度，但收益取决于协作增益与协调复杂度的平衡；通信对协作增益至关重要，最佳协作模式取决于团队规模和模型能力；协作在噪声先验和探索条件下能提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MECoBench 为系统理解多模态具身协作的机制与局限提供了标准化测试平台，揭示了协作收益的关键因素和边界条件。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多模态具身协作评测基准 MECoBench，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal agent collaboration benchmarks, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous models or generation. Signals: multimodal agent collaboration, embodied benchmark, MLLM cooperation, communication analysis, robustness under noise

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have strong potential as embodied agents, but their ability to collaborate in visually grounded environments remains underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce MECoBench, a multimodal embodied cooperation benchmark with an evaluation platform spanning diverse real-world tasks, two cooperation structures, and three collaboration modes. Through extensive experiments across various MLLMs, we summarize three key findings: (i) Collaboration generally improves embodied task completion, but its benefits depend on balancing collaborative gains against coordination complexity. (ii) Communication is essential to collaboration gains, while the best collaboration mode depends on team size and model capability. (iii) Moreover, collaboration improves robustness under noisy priors and exploration conditions. Generally, MECoBench provides a systematic testbed for understanding the mechanisms and limits of multimodal embodied collaboration. Code and dataset are available at .

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Single Character: Evaluating MLLMs for Sentence-Level Oracle Bone Inscription Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31169)
*Ziqi Li, Zijian Chen, Tingzhu Chen, Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (68.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 S-OBI 基准，通过合成清晰的句子级甲骨文图像和设计语义匹配、语义槽提取、上下文推理三类任务，系统评估 MLLM 在甲骨文句子级理解上的能力。实验表明当前 MLLM 在句子级理解上表现不佳，视觉感知错误会沿推理链传播，模型仍高度依赖单字识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有甲骨文识别研究仅关注单字级别，忽略了完整占卜辞中的长程文本连贯性和上下文依赖关系。作者希望评估 MLLM 是否能超越孤立字符识别，实现结构化的句子级甲骨文理解。

Method: 从 95 张经专家验证的原始拓片出发，用清晰的字形样本替换原始拓片中的字符，同时保留整体铭文结构和语义组织，合成清晰的句子级甲骨文图像。基于此构建 S-OBI 基准，包含语义匹配、语义槽提取和上下文推理三类任务，共 695 个问答对。

Result: 当前 MLLM 在句子级甲骨文理解上表现不佳。视觉感知错误会沿推理链传播，导致对掩码字符的预测错误，表明模型仍高度依赖单字识别而非结构化铭文理解。

Conclusion: S-OBI 提供了一个诊断性基准，用于评估 MLLM 能否超越孤立字符识别，实现结构化的铭文级理解。当前模型在句子级甲骨文理解方面仍有显著不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 S-OBI 基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在甲骨文句子级理解上的表现，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on a niche historical script benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Oracle bone inscription, sentence-level MLLM benchmark, low alignment with user's priority subtopics, no boost keywords matched, negative keywords not triggered

Abstract: Existing AI-assisted oracle bone inscription (OBI) visual recognition and understanding studies mainly focus on character-level, ignoring the long-form textual coherence and contextual dependencies embedded in complete divination charges. Recently, the powerful visual perception capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have opened new possibilities for OBI information processing. In this work, we introduce S-OBI, a novel benchmark for evaluating MLLMs in Sentence-level OBI understanding. Instead of using noisy and incomplete rubbings as the visual input, S-OBI synthesizes clear and standardized sentence-level OBI instances through glyph substitution and composition. According to 95 original rubbings with translations that have been identified, corrected, and verified by experts, we replace characters in the original rubbings with corresponding clean glyph samples sourced from existing OBI datasets while preserving the overall inscriptional structure and semantic organization. This mitigates the influence of low-level distortions and enables a more focused evaluation of sentence-level OBI understanding. Based on this, we design semantic matching, semantic slot extraction, and contextual reasoning tasks and obtain 695 question-answer pairs. Experiments reveal the inferiority of contemporary MLLMs on sentence-level OBI understanding. In particular, visual perception errors in unmasked regions propagate through the reasoning chain, leading to erroneous predictions for masked characters, which indicates that sentence-level OBI understanding in current models remains strongly dependent on character-level recognition. Overall, S-OBI provides a diagnostic benchmark for evaluating whether MLLMs can move beyond isolated character recognition toward structured inscription-level understanding.

</details>


### [109] [Learning to Deny: Action Denial in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31187)
*Raiyaan Abdullah, Shehreen Azad, Yogesh Singh Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF101-AD基准数据集，用于评估多模态大语言模型在视频中识别"动作缺失"（action denial）的能力。实验发现20个SOTA MLLM在动作存在识别上超过85%准确率，但在动作缺失识别上低于50%，暴露出模型倾向于"肯定合理动作"而非"验证动作是否真实发生"的盲点。作者提出CausalAct因果图框架，通过自然语言提示引入因果线索，显著减少误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在视频理解基准上表现优异，但缺乏对"动作缺失"（即上下文强烈暗示某动作但实际并未发生）的识别能力评估。作者希望揭示MLLM在因果推理上的盲点，并推动模型从"模式匹配"向"因果验证"发展。

Method: 1) 构建UCF101-AD基准：从UCF101中为每个动作视频配对"动作存在"和"动作缺失"两个版本，缺失版本保留相同的人物、物体、场景和运动线索，但关键动作被移除。2) 提出CausalAct因果图公式，通过自然语言提示表达场景结构（上下文、交互、运动），引导模型进行因果推理。

Result: 20个SOTA MLLM在动作存在识别上准确率>85%，但在动作缺失识别上<50%。引入CausalAct因果线索后，误报率显著降低，证明动作缺失识别是可学习的推理技能。

Conclusion: MLLM在视频理解中存在严重的"动作确认偏差"——倾向于根据上下文线索肯定动作存在而非验证动作是否真实发生。UCF101-AD为诊断和改进多模态模型的因果推理能力提供了新的评测视角。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了UCF101-AD基准数据集，专门评估MLLM在视频理解中识别动作缺失（action denial）的能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM action denial evaluation, not aligned with user's priority on continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: MLLM evaluation, action denial benchmark, causal reasoning, low priority subtopic, no generation or world model focus

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have rapidly advanced video understanding, achieving strong zero-shot and few-shot recognition across standard benchmarks. Yet their ability to deny an action by recognizing when an activity is not happening despite strong contextual cues remains largely unexplored. We introduce UCF101-AD, a large-scale benchmark consisting of paired Action-Presence and Action-Denial clips, designed to evaluate this capacity for denial. Each negative video in UCF101-AD preserves the same contextual and motion cues, including persons, objects, and locations, as its positive counterpart, but the defining action itself is explicitly absent. Evaluating 20 state-of-the-art MLLMs reveals a consistent failure: models that exceed 85% accuracy on the positive action classes collapse below 50% on their action-denial counterparts, indicating a strong inclination to affirm plausible actions rather than verify that they truly occur. This exposes a critical blind spot in modern video understanding: the inability to reason causally about whether a motion actually happens. To probe this issue, we explore a causal graph formulation, CausalAct, which expresses scene structure through natural-language prompts linking context, interaction, and motion. Incorporating such causal cues substantially reduces false positives, demonstrating that denial is a learnable reasoning skill. UCF101-AD provides a new lens for diagnosing and improving causal reasoning in multimodal models. Dataset and relevant code: .

</details>


### [110] [CooperScene: Multi-Modal Cooperative Autonomy Benchmark with C-V2X Communication Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31219)
*Bo Wu, Ruoshen Mo, Justin Yue, Yanyu Zhang, Janice Nguyen, Guoyuan Wu, Amit Roy-Chowdhury, Matthew J. Barth, Hang Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (74.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CooperScene是一个面向C-V2X协作自动驾驶的高保真多模态数据集与基准，包含多车多模态传感器数据、真实C-V2X通信特性、全局一致的3D标注（59K帧、344K物体），用于评估多智能体协作感知、预测与规划在实际部署条件下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作自动驾驶数据集忽略了真实部署中的复杂性，如有限且动态变化的通信带宽、异构感知模态以及多智能体协作的可扩展性。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个真实C-V2X通信条件下的高保真协作自动驾驶基准。

Method: 构建了一个包含3辆联网自动驾驶车辆（CAV）和1个路侧单元（RSU）的数据集，所有智能体配备多模态传感器和商用C-V2X通信模块。数据集涵盖交叉口、高速匝道和停车场等场景，提供10Hz全局一致的3D标注，并实现了严格的传感器/智能体同步、厘米级定位与空间对齐、精确跨模态标定以及符合3GPP标准的C-V2X通信特性表征。

Result: 数据集包含59K帧、344K个3D标注物体。通过基准测试评估了多智能体扩展性和实际部署条件下的性能，揭示了通信带宽动态变化对协作感知性能的影响。

Conclusion: CooperScene为协作自动驾驶研究提供了一个更贴近真实部署条件的高保真基准，有助于推动C-V2X协作感知、预测与规划在实际场景中的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向C-V2X协作自动驾驶的高保真多模态数据集与评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on cooperative autonomous driving with C-V2X, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: cooperative autonomy, C-V2X communication, multi-modal dataset, autonomous driving benchmark, real-world deployment

Abstract: Cellular vehicle-to-everything (C-V2X) enables cooperative perception, prediction, and planning beyond the field of view of individual agents. However, existing datasets often overlook the complexities of real-world deployment, such as limited communication bandwidth and its dynamics, heterogeneous sensing modalities, and scalability beyond a single cooperative partner. In this paper, we introduce CooperScene, a high-fidelity cooperative autonomy dataset with real-world C-V2X communication characterization. The dataset is organized into diverse scenes, including intersections, highway ramps, and parking lots. These scenes involve three connected and autonomous vehicles (CAVs) and one infrastructure roadside unit (RSU), all equipped with multi-modal sensors and commercial off-the-shelf C-V2X communication radios. All scenes are annotated with globally consistent 3D labels at 10 Hz, totaling 344K objects across 59K frames, underpinned by tight sensor- and agent-synchronization, centimeter-level localization and spatial alignment, precise cross-modality calibration, and 3GPP-standard-compliant C-V2X communication. CooperScene establishes a rigorous benchmark for evaluating multi-agent scaling and actual performance in real-world deployable settings. Project website for data and benchmark:

</details>


### [111] [Localized Conformal Prediction for Image Classification with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31577)
*Clément Fuchs, Tim Bary, Benoît Macq*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统性地将局部化共形预测方法应用于VLM图像分类任务，发现直接使用余弦相似度无法改善非局部基线，并提出一种简单的非线性变换方法，在保持边际覆盖保证的同时显著降低了平均预测集大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测具有强边际覆盖保证，但完全的条件保证不可实现。现有局部化方法在回归任务中表现优异，但在图像分类中尚未充分探索。本文旨在系统评估局部化共形预测在VLM图像分类中的表现，并寻找有效的改进方案。

Method: 基于开源实现的局部化共形预测算法，在VLM图像分类任务上进行广泛基准测试。提出对测试样本与校准样本之间的余弦相似度进行简单的非线性变换（如指数变换），以更好地反映样本间的局部相似性，同时保持边际覆盖保证。

Result: 直接使用余弦相似度的局部化方法未能优于非局部基线；而提出的非线性变换方法在多个VLM和数据集上实现了统计显著的预测集大小缩减。

Conclusion: 局部化共形预测在VLM图像分类中有效，但需要合适的相似度变换才能发挥优势。非线性变换是一种简单且有效的改进策略。

Classification reason: 该论文对VLM图像分类任务中的局部化共形预测方法进行了广泛的基准测试（benchmarking），属于多模态评测范畴，因此映射到[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on conformal prediction for VLM classification, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: VLM uncertainty quantification, localized conformal prediction, image classification, cosine similarity transformation, marginal coverage guarantees

Abstract: Conformal predictions have attracted significant attention in the field of uncertainty quantification, mainly because of their strong marginal coverage guarantees. Full conditional guarantee is not an attainable goal, a well known fact in conformal predictions literature. As a result, several approaches have tried to approximate this behavior by adapting the conformal sets of test-time samples according to their similarity to calibration examples. Although the latter has gained traction and shown impressive performances for regression problems, its application to image classification remains under-explored. We conduct an extensive benchmarking on natural image classification tasks with vision-language models (VLMs), using our open source implementation of a recent localized conformal prediction algorithm. We show that straightforward usage of the cosine similarity between test-time and calibration visual features, an intuitive choice for VLMs, is not sufficient to improve over the non-local baselines. In response, we propose a simple non-linear transformation of the cosine similarities, which conserves marginal coverage guarantees and achieves statistically significant mean set sizes reduction. Code is available at .

</details>


### [112] [JL1-CC&QA: Extending the JL1-CD Benchmark with Change Captioning and Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31745)
*Ziyuan Liu, Ruifei Zhu, Ouqiao Ma, Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

TL;DR: 本文在 JL1-CD 遥感变化检测数据集基础上扩展了变化描述（CC）和变化问答（QA）两个新标注层，构建了 JL1-CC&QA 多任务基准，包含 17,021 条变化描述和 20,060 个问答对，采用多模态大模型生成+视觉LLM评判+人工验证的三阶段标注流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥感变化检测仅关注像素级二值分割，能检测"哪里"发生了变化，但无法回答"发生了什么变化"以及"为什么变化"。为了弥合这一语义鸿沟，作者希望构建一个统一的多任务基准，将变化检测、变化描述和变化问答整合在同一数据集上。

Method: 基于吉林一号卫星 0.5-0.75m 分辨率的 5,000 对双时相遥感图像，在原有 JL1-CD 变化检测数据集基础上，通过三阶段标注流程扩展：(1) 多模态大模型（LLM）自动生成变化描述和问答对；(2) 视觉增强的 LLM 评判过滤低质量样本；(3) 人工专家验证确保标注质量。最终得到 17,021 条变化描述（JL1-CC）和 20,060 个问答对（JL1-QA，覆盖 8 种问题类型）。

Result: 构建了 JL1-CC&QA 多任务基准数据集，统一了二值变化掩码、变化描述和变化问答三种任务，为遥感变化理解提供了更丰富的评测资源。

Conclusion: JL1-CC&QA 作为首个在同一遥感图像集上统一变化检测、变化描述和变化问答的基准，有望推动遥感领域多任务变化理解研究的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个遥感变化检测的多任务基准（JL1-CC&QA），包含变化描述（CC）和变化问答（QA）两个新标注层，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Remote sensing benchmark is outside user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Remote sensing change detection, Change captioning & QA, Multimodal LLM annotation pipeline, Low priority subtopic match, Negative keyword "remote sensing"

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection (CD) traditionally focuses on pixel-level binary segmentation, which identifies where changes occur but neither what nor why. To bridge this semantic gap, we introduce JL1-CC&QA, a multi-task benchmark that extends the JL1-CD dataset with two complementary annotation layers: change captioning (CC) and change question answering (QA). Built upon 5,000 bi-temporal image pairs acquired by the Jilin-1 satellite at 0.5-0.75m ground sample distance, the benchmark comprises: (i) JL1-CC, providing 17,021 quality-verified captions that describe diverse land-cover transformations; and (ii) JL1-QA, offering 20,060 question-answer pairs across eight question types, enabling fine-grained, interactive interrogation of surface changes. All annotations are produced via a three-stage pipeline consisting of multi-modal large language model (LLM) generation, vision-grounded LLM judging, and human expert verification. We hope that JL1-CC&QA, as a benchmark unifying binary change masks, change captions, and change-oriented QA over the same image set, will serve as a valuable resource for the community to advance multi-task change understanding in remote sensing. The dataset is available at .

</details>


### [113] [RESOLVE: A Multi-Resolution and Multi-Modal Dataset for Roadside Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31895)
*Shaozu Ding, Linan Song, Marco De Vincenzi, Dajiang Suo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: RESOLVE 是一个大规模真实世界路侧协同感知基准数据集，包含多分辨率 LiDAR 和同步相机-LiDAR 数据，用于系统评估路侧 3D 检测与跟踪中单模态和融合架构在不同点云稀疏度下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路侧协同感知数据集缺乏对 LiDAR 点云稀疏度变化（由传感器配置和场景依赖的感知条件引起）下单模态与相机-LiDAR 融合架构行为的系统研究。

Method: 构建了 RESOLVE 数据集，包含超过 10 万张图像和 2.6 万帧点云，22 万个手动标注的边界框，覆盖 10 类交通参与者，在真实城市交叉口采集，涵盖多种光照和天气条件。数据集支持三种 LiDAR 分辨率级别的受控评估，同时保持其他感知和环境因素不变。

Result: 通过大量基准实验揭示了多模态融合如何补偿 LiDAR 点云稀疏性，以及点云分布偏移（由分辨率变化、感知距离、训练-推理分辨率不匹配引起）对架构性能的影响。

Conclusion: RESOLVE 为设计成本高效的路侧多模态感知系统提供了有价值的见解，并可作为公平比较不同架构在点云稀疏度变化下表现的标准化基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向路侧协同感知的多分辨率多模态基准数据集 RESOLVE，包含相机-LiDAR 数据，用于系统评估 3D 检测与跟踪架构。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Dataset is solid for roadside perception but far from user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Roadside cooperative perception, Multi-resolution LiDAR, Camera-LiDAR fusion, 3D detection benchmark, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: LiDAR has increasingly been integrated into traffic cameras to expand coverage and mitigate occlusion in roadside cooperative perception. However, how unimodal and camera-LiDAR fusion architectures behave under variations in LiDAR point sparsity induced by sensor configurations and scene-dependent sensing conditions remains underexplored. We introduce RESOLVE, a large-scale real-world benchmark dataset featuring multi-resolution roadside LiDAR and synchronized camera-LiDAR sensing for systematic evaluation of unimodal and fusion-based architectures in roadside 3D detection and tracking. RESOLVE contains over 100k images and 26k point cloud frames with 220k manually annotated bounding boxes, captured at a real-world urban intersection across diverse lighting and weather conditions and spanning 10 classes of traffic participants. In particular, RESOLVE enables controlled evaluation across three LiDAR resolution levels while keeping all other sensing and environmental factors fixed. This allows fair cross-architecture comparisons under point cloud distribution shifts resulting from resolution variations, sensing distance, and training-inference resolution mismatches. Results from extensive benchmark experiments reveal insights into how multimodal fusion can compensate for LiDAR point sparsity, offering clues for designing cost-efficient roadside multimodal perception. The dataset and benchmark codes are available at .

</details>


### [114] [No Place to Hide: Benchmarking Video Hallucination with Background-Controlled Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31933)
*Haojian Huang, Harold Haodong Chen, Meng Luo, Junjia Du, Shanqing Xu, Ziheng Chen, Yanxiang Huang, Yinchuan Li, Ying-Cong Chen*

TL;DR: 本文提出 VidPair-Halluc，一个通过背景控制视频对来评估大视频模型（LVM）视频幻觉的新基准。利用 PairFlow 流水线（结合文生图和视频生成技术）构建具有相似背景但不同前景语义的视频对，包含 1K 高质量对抗视频对和 11K 时空 QA 对，覆盖 10 个语义方面。评测显示主流 LVM 在对抗性设置下仍难以进行鲁棒的细粒度视频理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频幻觉基准主要依赖文本扰动或对抗性问题，忽略了视觉背景一致性的控制，导致模型错误难以归因于真正的幻觉。作者希望构建一个在严格控制背景条件下评估视频幻觉的基准，以更精确地诊断模型问题。

Method: 提出 PairFlow 流水线，利用文生图和视频生成技术系统性地构建故事、生成连贯视频片段并组装成对抗性视频对。视频对具有高度相似的背景但不同的前景语义，覆盖空间和时间推理的 10 个语义方面。构建了 1K 高质量对抗视频对和 11K 时空 QA 对。

Result: 在主流 LVM 上的评估显示，模型在对抗性设置下仍存在持续的细粒度视频理解困难，表明现有模型在视频幻觉问题上仍有显著不足。

Conclusion: VidPair-Halluc 通过背景控制视频对提供了更精确的视频幻觉评估方法，揭示了当前 LVM 在对抗性视频理解中的局限性，为未来视频幻觉缓解研究提供了可靠的评测基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 VidPair-Halluc，一个用于评估大视频模型（LVM）视频幻觉的新基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该基准通过背景控制视频对来精确评估幻觉，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on video hallucination benchmarking, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: Video hallucination benchmark, Background-controlled pairs, LVM evaluation, Not aligned with priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: We introduce VidPair-Halluc, a new benchmark for evaluating video hallucination in large video models (LVMs) under rigorous and controlled conditions. Unlike previous benchmarks that primarily rely on text-based perturbations or adversarial questions while neglecting the consistency of visual backgrounds, VidPair-Halluc features video pairs with highly similar backgrounds but distinctly different foreground semantics, enabling precise attribution of model errors to genuine hallucination rather than background variation. The benchmark is constructed through PairFlow, a pipeline that leverages recent advances in text-to-image and video generation to systematically compose stories, generate coherent video clips, and assemble them into adversarial pairs. Covering both spatial and temporal reasoning across ten semantic aspects, VidPair-Halluc comprises 1K high-quality adversarial video pairs and 11K spatio-temporal QA pairs with control over background and foreground variations. Evaluations on mainstream LVMs show persistent difficulty with robust fine-grained video understanding in adversarial settings, and code and data are available at the .

</details>

### [115] [Reasoning-aware Speculative Decoding for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31160)
*Anh Dung Dinh, Simon Khan, Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 普通相关 (60.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出推理感知的推测解码方法（FlatRoPE + AARL），通过将VLA模型的因果链推理拆分为常规推理器（关注轨迹历史）和审慎推理器（关注视觉证据），在自动驾驶场景下实现约4倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的VLA规划器在生成轨迹前需要自回归地执行因果链推理（CoC），该推理步骤主导了推理延迟，而轨迹头是并行的且计算成本低。延迟是自动驾驶的操作性约束，因此加速推理步骤是核心问题。

Method: 将CoC推理拆分为两条专用路径：常规推理器（routine reasoner）处理可预测的延续部分，关注轨迹历史；审慎推理器（deliberative reasoner，即未修改的VLA目标模型）处理需要新视觉证据的意外情况。采用推测解码框架让两者协作。提出FlatRoPE（1D旋转位置编码）打破目标模型3D M-RoPE的旋转对称性，将注意力从视觉token重定向到轨迹历史token；提出Action-aware RL（AARL）后训练阶段，使用动作质量奖励和静态参考KL锚点。

Result: 在Alpamayo规划器上，推理步骤运行时间减少约4倍。

Conclusion: 通过将推理拆分为专用化的常规推理器和审慎推理器，结合FlatRoPE和AARL，可以有效加速VLA模型的推理，满足自动驾驶的延迟约束。

Classification reason: 该论文针对自动驾驶中的VLA模型（视觉-语言-动作模型）提出推理感知的推测解码加速方法，属于多模态理解与生成方向中的高效化问题。论文核心是加速VLM/VLA推理中的自回归推理步骤，与[多模态高效化与轻量化]（efficient_multimodal）这一CANONICAL子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving and speculative decoding, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks direct alignment with continuous language models or video generation. Signals: speculative decoding, autonomous driving, VLA model, inference acceleration, chain-of-causation reasoning

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) planners for autonomous driving emit a chain-of-causation (CoC) reasoning step \emph{before} producing a trajectory. The reasoning is autoregressive and dominates inference latency, while the trajectory head is parallel and cheap. Latency is an operational constraint in autonomous driving, so accelerating the reasoning step is the central problem we address. We observe that CoC reasoning has two qualitatively different needs: most tokens continue routine setup that follows naturally from the ego-trajectory history, and a small fraction encode commitments that require fresh visual evidence about an unexpected situation. We split this reasoning into two specialized paths: a \emph{routine reasoner} that handles the predictable continuation by attending to trajectory history, and a \emph{deliberative reasoner} (the unmodified VLA target) that handles novel cases by attending to current visual evidence, using the speculative decoding framework as the architectural template for how the two paths cooperate. Unlike standard speculative decoding, our routine reasoner is not a smaller replica of the target; the two reasoners are deliberately specialized to read different parts of the prompt. We propose two techniques to realize this. First, we introduce \textbf{FlatRoPE}, a 1D rotary positional embedding in the draft that breaks the rotational symmetry of the target's 3D M-RoPE, redirecting attention away from visual tokens and onto trajectory-history tokens. Second, we introduce \textbf{Action-aware RL (AARL)}, a post-training stage that uses an action-quality reward together with a static-reference KL anchor. Together, our two-reasoner system reduces the reasoning-step running time by approximately $4\times$ relative to the original Alpamayo planner.

</details>


### [116] [MS-Resampler: Multi-Scope Visual Resampling for Efficient Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31383)
*Zhongyang Li, Yaqian Li, Faming Fang, Rinyoichi Takezoe, Zi-Hao Bo, Cheng Qian, Mo Guang, Guixu Zhang, Kaiwen Long*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (84.25) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MS-Resampler 是一种多范围视觉重采样框架，通过注入显式空间范围先验到重采样注意力中，使多个分支分别以不同粒度聚合视觉信息，再自适应融合输出，在保持极低计算开销的同时提升 MLLM 的视觉理解与多模态推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 的重采样投影器通常采用单一固定的全局交叉注意力聚合范围，这容易模糊细粒度局部证据，难以在固定 token 预算内同时捕获局部细节与全局上下文。

Method: MS-Resampler 实例化多个范围特定的重采样器，通过向重采样注意力中注入显式空间范围先验（如局部/全局位置编码），使每个分支以特定粒度聚合视觉信息，最后通过自适应融合机制将各分支输出合并为最终视觉表征。

Result: 在十个公开多模态基准上，MS-Resampler 相比传统单范围重采样器在视觉理解与多模态推理上取得一致提升，且仅引入极小的额外计算开销。

Conclusion: 多范围视觉重采样是一种简单有效的 MLLM 投影器改进策略，能够在固定 token 预算下更好地平衡局部细节与全局上下文，为高效多模态大模型提供了新的设计思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MS-Resampler，通过多范围视觉重采样框架减少视觉 token 数量并提升效率，属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Directly improves MLLM efficiency and visual understanding with minimal overhead, aligning with user's priority on model efficiency and unified multimodal models. Signals: multimodal large language model, efficient inference, visual resampling, multi-scope aggregation, lightweight architecture

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically employ resampling-based projectors to transform dense visual features into a compact token sequence for language modeling. Most existing resamplers adopt a single, fixed aggregation scope via global cross-attention, which can blur fine-grained local evidence and limit the ability to capture both local details and global context within a fixed token budget. In this work, we propose MS-Resampler, a multi-scope visual resampling framework for MLLMs. MS-Resampler instantiates multiple scope-specific resamplers by injecting explicit spatial scope priors into the resampling attention, enabling each branch to aggregate visual information at a particular granularity from local to global. The outputs of these scope-specific resamplers are then adaptively fused to produce the final visual representations for language modeling. Extensive experiments on ten public multimodal benchmarks show that MS-Resampler consistently improves visual understanding and multimodal reasoning over conventional single-scope resamplers, while introducing only minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [117] [Attend, Transform, or Silence: Operator-Level Visual Skipping for Efficient Multimodal LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31903)
*Zhaoyang Luo, Runmin Dong, Miao Yang, Fan Wei, Yushan Lai, Bin Luo, Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种算子级视觉 token 跳过框架，通过分析发现视觉 token 在 Transformer 的注意力或 FFN 算子中存在"答案沉默冗余"，从而选择性地跳过冗余算子计算，在保持 99.5% 性能的同时减少 33.7% 的 TFLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 加速方法通常粗粒度地移除视觉 token 或跳过整层更新，可能丢弃细粒度证据或同时抑制有用和冗余的算子。作者希望从更细粒度的算子级别识别并跳过冗余计算，实现更好的效率-精度权衡。

Method: 将每个 Transformer 层分解为注意力（Attention）和前馈网络（FFN）两个算子，基于"答案沉默冗余"观察（即视觉 token 的更新对答案 token 表示影响很小），为每个算子独立决定是否跳过视觉 token 的计算。框架保留完整视觉 token 序列，仅选择性跳过冗余的注意力、FFN 或两者。

Result: 在三种 MLLM 架构（Qwen3-VL 等）和 10 个 VQA 基准上验证，方法在 Qwen3-VL 上减少 33.7% TFLOPs，同时保留 99.5% 的原始模型性能，实现了强效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 算子级别的视觉 token 跳过是一种比粗粒度层跳过更有效的 MLLM 加速策略，能够在不显著损失性能的前提下大幅降低推理计算量。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种算子级别的视觉 token 跳过框架，用于加速多模态大语言模型推理，属于 MLLM 推理效率优化。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态高效化与轻量化 (efficient_multimodal) 高度匹配，该 subtopic 涵盖 token 压缩、稀疏推理等高效方法。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for MLLMs, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel operator-level skipping method that achieves strong efficiency-accuracy trade-offs. Signals: efficient inference, multimodal large language model, visual token skipping, operator-level acceleration, Qwen3-VL

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly process long visual-token sequences, increasing the overall inference computation. Existing acceleration methods usually remove visual tokens or skip visual-token updates in entire layers, but these coarse strategies may discard fine-grained evidence or suppress useful operators together with redundant ones. In this paper, we study visual-token computation from an answer-observable perspective and find that late visual-token updates can remain large while having little effect on answer-token representations. Motivated by this answer-silent redundancy, we decompose each Transformer layer into attention and FFN operators and show that useful visual computation is often operator-dominant and layer-dependent. We propose an operator-level visual-token skipping framework that preserves the full visual-token sequence while selectively bypassing redundant attention, FFN, or both. Experiments across three MLLM architectures and 10 VQA benchmarks show that our method achieves strong efficiency-accuracy trade-offs, reducing \textbf{33.7\%} TFLOPs on Qwen3-VL while retaining \textbf{99.5\%} of the vanilla model performance.

</details>


### [118] [ERA: Entropy-Guided Visual Token Pruning with Rectified Attention for Efficient MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31982)
*Yuhao Wang, Mu Qiao, Haiwen Diao, Yunzhi Zhuge, Pingping Zhang, Xindong Zhang, Lei Zhang, Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ERA 提出了一种基于熵引导的视觉 token 剪枝框架，通过双视角熵剪枝、偏置感知 token 回收和对数保持注意力矫正三个组件，在保持模型性能的同时大幅降低 MLLM 推理开销，适用于单图、多图和视频场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 中长视觉 token 序列导致推理成本高昂，现有训练无关的 token 缩减方法会扭曲注意力分布，导致"注意力对数坍塌"现象，亟需一种既能高效压缩 token 又能保持注意力分布正确性的方法。

Method: ERA 包含三个核心组件：1) 双视角熵剪枝（DEP），通过联合建模视觉多样性和头级显著性识别代表性锚点 token；2) 偏置感知 token 回收（BTR），将剪枝 token 回收至对应锚点并估计聚类级对数偏置；3) 对数保持注意力矫正（LAR），将估计偏置注入注意力对数以矫正 token 缩减导致的坍塌。

Result: 在多个 MLLM 上验证，ERA 在单图、多图和视频设置下均能实现显著加速（如 token 压缩率可达 75%+），同时保持甚至提升下游任务性能，有效缓解了注意力对数坍塌问题。

Conclusion: ERA 将对数保持的视觉 token 剪枝建立为高效 MLLM 的原则性框架，统一了理论基础、算法设计与实际部署，为训练无关的 token 压缩提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视觉 token 剪枝框架以加速 MLLM 推理，属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题，具体涉及 token 压缩与注意力矫正。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient MLLM inference via token pruning, aligning with user's model efficiency priority and boosting keywords. Signals: efficient inference, visual token pruning, MLLM acceleration, attention logit collapse, entropy-guided pruning

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) incur prohibitive inference costs due to long visual token sequences. Training-free visual token reduction provides an efficient solution. However, existing methods distort attention distributions, giving rise to a phenomenon we term Attention Logit Collapse. To address this issue, we propose ERA, an Entropy-guided visual token pruning framework with Rectified Attention for efficient MLLMs. Specifically, ERA comprises three crucial components: Dual-view Entropy Pruning (DEP), Bias-aware Token Recycling (BTR), and Logit-preserving Attention Rectification (LAR). First, DEP identifies representative anchor tokens by jointly modeling visual diversity and head-wise saliency. BTR then recycles pruned tokens into their corresponding anchors while estimating a cluster-level logit bias. Building upon this, LAR injects the estimated bias into attention logits, effectively rectifying the collapse induced by token reduction. Together, these components preserve visual evidence even under aggressive compression, enabling robust performance across single-image, multi-image, and video settings on a wide range of MLLMs. Beyond delivering practical acceleration, ERA establishes logit-preserving visual token pruning as a principled framework for efficient MLLMs, unifying theoretical foundation, algorithmic design, and practical deployment. The code is at .

</details>
