### [119] [AeroVerse-SatAgent: UAV-Satellite Collaborative Spatial Reasoning Inspired by the Dual Visual Pathway Theory of Cognitive Neuroscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31467)
*Wenyi Zhang, Fanglong Yao, Youzhi Liu, Peng Hu, Zhengqiu Zhu, Chen Gao, Xian Sun, Kun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 受人类视觉双通路机制启发，提出SatAgent——无人机与卫星协同空间推理模型，通过几何感知3D重建编码器、多视图拓扑-语义对齐模块和一致性损失，在复杂城市环境中实现鲁棒的空间推理，并构建了首个大规模无人机-卫星协同多视图空间推理数据集SatAgent-SR130K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机空间推理方法受限于单视角感知，易受遮挡和透视畸变影响；同时大多数VLM缺乏显式几何建模，依赖语义线索，在视角和尺度变化下推理不一致。亟需一种融合多视角几何信息与语义信息的空间推理方法。

Method: 1) 几何感知3D重建编码器：将无人机2D特征提升为显式3D空间表征；2) 多视图拓扑-语义对齐模块：在统一BEV坐标系中融合跨视角特征；3) 多视角一致性损失：鼓励视角不变的表征；4) 构建SatAgent-SR130K大规模无人机-卫星协同多视图空间推理数据集。

Result: 在多种任务上，SatAgent相比通用基础模型和专用空间推理模型分别提升25.91%和11.69%，在复杂几何关系推理中达到特别高的准确率。

Conclusion: 融合卫星与无人机双视角的显式几何建模能显著提升空间推理的鲁棒性和准确性，验证了认知神经科学双视觉通路理论在航空具身智能中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究无人机-卫星协同空间推理，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与3D多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）子主题，该子主题为[CANONICAL]条目，涵盖空间推理、几何推理等，与论文内容高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel cognitive neuroscience-inspired dual-pathway spatial reasoning for UAV-satellite collaboration, but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: UAV-satellite spatial reasoning, dual visual pathway inspiration, geometric-aware 3D reconstruction, multi-view topology-semantic alignment, new dataset SatAgent-SR130K

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of aerospace embodied intelligence, enabling Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) to autonomously understand and reason about complex environments has become increasingly important. However, existing UAV-based spatial reasoning approaches face critical limitations: single-view perception renders them vulnerable to occlusions and perspective distortions, while most VLMs lack explicit geometric modeling, relying on semantic cues and yielding inconsistent reasoning under viewpoint and scale variations. To address these challenges, we propose SatAgent, a UAV-Satellite collaborative spatial reasoning model inspired by the dual-pathway mechanism of the human visual system. By jointly leveraging satellite and UAV perspectives, SatAgent enables robust, accurate reasoning in complex urban environments. We first introduce a Geometric-Aware 3D Reconstruction Encoder that elevates 2D UAV features into explicit 3D spatial representations. Next, we design a multi-view topology-semantic alignment module integrating cross-view features within a unified BEV coordinate system. We further introduce a multi-view consistency loss encouraging viewpoint-invariant representations. Finally, we construct SatAgent-SR130K, the first large-scale UAV-Satellite collaborative multi-view spatial reasoning dataset. Experiments show SatAgent outperforms state-of-the-art general-purpose foundation models and specialized spatial reasoning models by 25.91\% and 11.69\%, respectively, across diverse tasks, achieving particularly high accuracy in complex geometric relationship reasoning.

</details>


### [120] [Think While You Map: Asynchronous Vision-Language Agents for Incremental 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31471)
*Deniz Bickici, Michael Pabst, Shohei Mori, Dieter Schmalstieg*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种异步架构，将轻量级在线3D建图与重量的VLM语义细化解耦，实现增量式开放词汇3D场景图构建。在线概率体素骨干网络维护稳定的物体身份，后台VLM代理逐步丰富语义属性、空间关系和闭环检测，并通过多目标帧调度器摊销VLM成本。该方法在语义分割和视觉定位基准上达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D场景图方法通常采用"先重建、后语义丰富"的两阶段流水线，导致探索过程中场景图不可查询。本文认为这种顺序耦合是不必要的，希望实现建图与语义细化异步并行，使场景图在探索过程中即可查询并随时间不断丰富。

Method: 提出异步架构：(1) 在线轻量级概率体素骨干网络（基于Voxblox++）增量式维护物体身份和几何信息；(2) 后台VLM代理（使用GPT-4o）异步执行语义细化，包括语义闭环检测消除重复物体、附加细粒度视觉属性、推导物体间空间关系；(3) 多目标帧调度器选择少量信息量大的帧，联合覆盖多个目标以摊销VLM推理成本。场景图在探索过程中即可查询，语义丰富度随时间增长。

Result: 在ScanNet和Replica数据集上，语义分割性能匹配或超越现有开放词汇3D场景图方法；在Sr3D+、Nr3D、ScanRefer三个视觉定位基准上，以A@0.25指标分别超越先前最优方法15.3、18.8和16.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 异步架构有效解耦了在线建图与离线语义细化，使得3D场景图在探索过程中即可查询并渐进式丰富，同时通过帧调度显著降低VLM计算成本。该方法在语义分割和视觉定位任务上均取得优异性能，验证了异步设计在3D场景理解中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出异步架构进行增量式3D场景图构建，结合VLM进行语义细化、空间关系推理和视觉定位，属于空间与3D多模态推理方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "空间与 3D 多模态推理" (spatial_3d_reasoning)。

Importance reason: Asynchronous VLM-3D mapping is novel but tangential to user's core focus on continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: asynchronous architecture, incremental 3D scene graph, open-vocabulary, VLM semantic refinement, visual grounding benchmarks

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D scene graph methods typically operate in two stages: first reconstruct, then enrich with vision-language models, leaving the graph unqueryable during exploration. We argue that this sequential coupling is unnecessary and propose an asynchronous architecture in which lightweight online mapping runs concurrently with heavyweight semantic refinement. A probabilistic voxel-based backbone maintains stable object identities incrementally, while background VLM agents progressively enrich the graph. This framework resolves duplicate object tracks through semantic loop closure, attaches fine-grained visual attributes and derives spatial relations between objects. A multi-target frame scheduler amortizes VLM cost by selecting a small set of informative frames that jointly cover multiple targets. The resulting scene graph is queryable during exploration and grows in semantic richness over time. Our method matches or outperforms existing open-vocabulary 3D scene graph methods on semantic segmentation (ScanNet, Replica) and surpasses the prior state-of-the-art across three visual grounding benchmarks (Sr3D+, Nr3D, ScanRefer) by 15.3 to 18.8 A@0.25. Project page:

</details>


### [121] [MV-GEL: Language-Driven Multi-View Geometric Entity Localization on Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31533)
*Kartik Bali, Roland Aydin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MV-GEL 提出了一种语言驱动的多视图几何实体定位框架，通过 GELviews 视角排序模块选择最优视角，结合 VLM 推理分割和几何感知光线投射，将2D分割掩码提升到3D网格上，实现细粒度CAD几何实体的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在图像域的指代分割已取得进展，但将语言驱动的定位扩展到结构化3D几何实体（如边、面、曲面）仍面临挑战，主要因为3D物体外观对视角高度敏感，单一视角可能因遮挡或透视缩短导致目标不可见。

Method: 提出 MV-GEL 框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) GELviews 视角排序模块，基于语言提示对CAD几何实体的可观测性进行排序，选择最优视角；(2) VLM 推理分割骨干网络，在选定视角上生成2D分割掩码；(3) 几何感知光线投射模块，将2D掩码提升到对应的3D网格上。整个框架不依赖CAD模型，仅使用3D网格。

Result: 在面级IoU上相比基线提升1.7倍，在边级F1上提升超过4.5倍，显著优于基于CLIP和随机视角采样的方法，尤其对薄和视角敏感的几何实体效果突出。

Conclusion: MV-GEL 通过智能视角选择和VLM推理分割，有效解决了语言驱动的3D几何实体定位难题，为CAD、机器人操作和科学仿真等应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于自然语言在3D网格上定位几何实体（面、边、曲面），属于3D视觉定位与空间推理范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "空间与 3D 多模态推理" (spatial_3d_reasoning) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D geometric entity localization, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: 3D mesh localization, language-driven, VLM-based, CAD agnostic, view selection

Abstract: Identifying and grounding precise geometric entities, such as edges, planar regions, and curved surfaces within 3D objects, is foundational to computer-aided design (CAD), robotic manipulation, and scientific simulation. Although modern Vision Language Models (VLMs) have advanced referring segmentation (RIS) in the image domain, extending such language-driven localization to structured 3D geometry is substantially harder. The 3D object appearance is highly sensitive to viewpoints; a single perspective may render a target entity clearly observable, while another may suffer from severe occlusion or foreshortening. In this work, we attempt to solve these challenges with MV-GEL (Multi-View Geometric Entity Localization), a framework for localizing fine-grained geometric entities on polygon meshes from natural language queries. Our key insight is that reliable CAD entity (i.e., faces, edges or solids) localization depends on selecting views that make the queried entity maximally interpretable. We introduce GELviews, a prompt-conditioned ranking module that prioritizes viewpoints based on language prompted observability of geometric CAD entities. Selected views are processed by a VLM-based reasoning segmentation backbone, and predicted masks are lifted to the corresponding meshes via geometry-aware ray casting. Our framework is completely CAD agnostic and relies only on 3D meshes. Experiments show up to a 1.7X improvement in face-level IoU and over 4.5X gains in edge-level F1 compared to vanilla baselines, substantially outperforming CLIP-based and random view sampling, particularly for thin and view-sensitive dataset, code and trained checkpoints are available at .

</details>


### [122] [Technical Report of RoboSpatial Challenge at CVPR 2026: Selective Reasoning Activation and Reference-Frame Disambiguation for Embodied Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31645)
*Yuxiang Xie, Qi Lv, Jianming Xing, Zijian Hong, Xiang Deng, Weili Guan, Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RoboSpatialBrain 是 RoboSpatial Challenge (CVPR 2026) 的冠军方案，基于 RoboBrain2.5-8B-NV，通过两种免训练推理时机制——强制<think>前缀激活+任务后提示词（提升上下文与兼容性任务的推理能力）和显式参考系重定向（解决相机中心与物体中心的歧义）——显著提升了具身空间推理能力，在 RoboSpatial-Home 上达到 80.9% 的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在通用感知上表现强劲，但在具身任务所需的空间推理（如物体相对位置、参考系理解）上仍存在明显不足，亟需提升模型在空间场景中的推理能力。

Method: 提出 RoboSpatialBrain，包含两个核心机制：(1) 强制<think>前缀激活策略 + 任务特定后提示词，引导模型在上下文和兼容性任务上进行深思熟虑的推理；(2) 显式参考系重定向流水线，解决相机中心与物体中心之间的歧义问题。此外还探索了在兼容性数据上微调 RoboBrain2.5 并与提示策略结合的分析。

Result: 在 RoboSpatial Challenge 中获得第一名，在 RoboSpatial-Home 数据集上总体成功率达到 80.9%。

Conclusion: 通过免训练的推理时机制（强制思考激活和参考系消歧）可以有效提升 VLM 在具身空间推理任务上的表现，为后续研究提供了简单有效的改进思路。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦具身空间推理（embodied spatial reasoning），涉及参考系消歧和选择性推理激活，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与3D多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied spatial reasoning with inference-time tricks, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Embodied spatial reasoning, Inference-time prompting, Reference-frame disambiguation, RoboBrain2.5, CVPR challenge winner

Abstract: Vision-language models achieve strong general perception but often struggle with the spatial reasoning required for embodied tasks. We present RoboSpatialBrain, our submission to the RoboSpatial Challenge at the Embodied Reasoning in Action Workshop, CVPR 2026, built on RoboBrain2.5-8B-NV. RoboSpatialBrain combines two training-free, inference-time mechanisms: a forced <think> prefix activation strategy paired with a task-specific post-prompt that elicits deliberate reasoning on context and compatibility tasks, and an explicit reference-frame redirection pipeline that resolves camera-centric and object-centric ambiguity for context tasks. We additionally explore fine-tuning RoboBrain2.5 on compatibility data and present a detailed analysis of its interaction with prompting. RoboSpatialBrain achieved first place in the RoboSpatial Challenge, with an overall success rate of 80.9\% on RoboSpatial-Home. Code is available at .

</details>

## 统一多模态模型 [[Back]](#toc)

理解与生成一体化、统一 token 表征、Any-to-Any 多模态模型。

### [123] [UniTac: A Unified Multimodal Model for Cross-Sensor Tactile Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31451)
*Jiahang Tu, Fengyu Yang, Chenyang Ma, Xihang Yu, Ziyao Zeng, Shaokai Wu, Hanbin Zhao, Zhi Tao, Chao Zhang, Hui Qian, Alex Wong*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniTac 是首个面向跨传感器触觉理解与生成的统一多模态模型，通过双层级表征建模触觉过程（从非接触到接触），在触觉理解（物体属性描述、传感器识别）和触觉生成（两阶段训练+传感器先验采样）上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMM）在视觉、语言等模态上取得了成功，但触觉模态尚未被纳入这一范式。触觉信号同时受物体属性和传感器配置影响，现有方法缺乏对这种跨传感器、跨物体信息的统一建模能力。

Method: UniTac 将触觉过程建模为从非接触到接触的转变，设计双层级表征分别编码传感器属性和物体属性。理解任务包括物体属性描述和传感器识别；生成任务采用两阶段训练（重建+对齐），并引入基于传感器先验的采样策略来模拟真实触觉接触信号。模型在大规模多传感器数据集上训练。

Result: 在触觉理解任务上达到 SOTA 性能，并能跨传感器生成逼真的触觉信号。

Conclusion: UniTac 成功将统一多模态模型范式扩展到触觉领域，验证了双层级表征和两阶段训练策略在触觉理解与生成中的有效性，为触觉智能的进一步发展奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出首个面向触觉模态的统一多模态模型（UMM），同时支持触觉理解与生成，属于"统一多模态模型"这一现有子主题的范畴。

Importance reason: Tactile modality is outside user's core focus on vision-language and video generation. Signals: unified multimodal model, tactile understanding and generation, cross-sensor, dual-level representation, sensor-prior sampling

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) have shown great promise in integrating understanding and generation across diverse modalities. However, existing research rarely extends this paradigm to the tactile domain, where both object-level semantics and sensor-level configurations jointly determine the meaning of touch. To address this gap, we propose UniTac, the first UMM designed for tactile understanding and generation. UniTac models the tactile process as a transition from non-contact to contact, capturing the physical interaction between sensors and objects through a dual-level representation that encodes both sensor and object attributes. For tactile understanding, UniTac introduces two tasks, object property description and sensor identification, to enhance reasoning over physical and cross-sensor information. For tactile generation, we design a two-stage training paradigm consisting of reconstruction and alignment, together with a sensor-prior-based sampling strategy that simulates realistic tactile contact. Trained on large-scale multi-sensor datasets, UniTac achieves state-of-the-art performance in tactile understanding and generates realistic tactile signals across sensors.

</details>


### [124] [Bridging Video Understanding and Generation in a Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31326)
*Yuqi Wang, Runyi Li, Ruoyu Feng, Renjie Chen, Wenfeng Lin, Mingyu Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.75) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Vega是一个统一视频理解与生成的框架，采用共享词汇表联合建模文本与视觉表征，结合自回归预测关键帧语义token与扩散模块渲染高分辨率视频帧的混合架构，在VBench和VideoMME等基准上取得良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像领域的理解与生成统一建模已被广泛探索，但视频领域的统一建模仍未被充分研究。视频理解需要紧凑的判别性语义表征，而视频生成需要保留视觉细节和时间一致性的密集信号，两者之间存在根本性矛盾。视频天然包含空间语义和时间动态，比静态图像更适合统一多模态建模，因此作者希望弥合视频理解与生成之间的鸿沟。

Method: Vega采用共享词汇表联合建模文本与视觉表征，使用混合架构：自回归（AR）模型预测关键帧的语义视觉token，提供结构化表征；扩散模块基于AR输出的引导渲染密集的高分辨率视频帧。AR负责语义理解层面的紧凑表征，扩散负责生成层面的细节渲染，两者协同工作。

Result: 在视频生成基准VBench和视频理解基准VideoMME上均取得了强性能，验证了统一框架在视频理解和生成两个任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: Vega证明了视频理解与生成可以在统一框架下有效桥接，AR+扩散的混合架构能够同时满足理解所需的紧凑语义表征和生成所需的密集细节渲染需求，为视频领域的统一多模态建模提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Vega统一框架，桥接视频理解与视频生成，属于理解与生成一体化方向。该论文同时匹配多模态理解与生成（primary）和多模态生成两个方向。在subtopic层面，该论文属于"统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model），该subtopic在multimodal_understanding_generation方向下为[CANONICAL]条目，在multimodal_generation方向下也有同名非canonical条目，直接复用。

Importance reason: Directly addresses unified multimodal modeling with a hybrid AR-diffusion architecture, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: unified multimodal, autoregressive video generation, diffusion-based rendering, shared vocabulary, video understanding and generation

Abstract: Recently, unified image generation and understanding have been extensively explored. However, extending such unified modeling paradigms to the video domain remains largely underexplored. A central challenge is that video understanding favors compact, discriminative semantic representations, whereas video generation requires dense signals that preserve visual details and temporal coherence. Videos naturally capture both spatial semantics and temporal dynamics, making them a more suitable modality for unified multimodal modeling compared to static images. In this paper, we propose Vega, a unified framework that bridges video understanding and generation. Vega leverages a shared vocabulary to jointly model text and visual representations and employs a hybrid architecture combining autoregressive (AR) prediction with diffusion-based rendering. Specifically, the AR model focuses on predicting semantically meaningful visual tokens for keyframes, providing a structured representation that guides the diffusion module in rendering dense, high-resolution video frames. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Vega achieves strong performance on video generation benchmarks such as VBench and video understanding benchmarks like VideoMME.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-vlm_mllm_general'></div>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)

通用视觉语言模型、MLLM 架构、多模态指令微调与对话。

### [125] [Learning from Failure: Inference-Time Self-Improvement for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31270)
*Xueqiao Sun, Xiaohan Wang, Ludwig Schmidt, Serena Yeung-Levy, Yuhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.AI, cs.CY

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种失败驱动的自改进循环范式，利用 LLM 诊断计算机使用智能体在任务中的失败模式，生成推理时解决方案和代码补丁，从而在不进行额外训练的情况下提升智能体性能。在 OSWorld 基准上，将 OpenCUA-72B 模型的成功率从 42.3% 提升至 48.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机使用智能体自改进方法仅利用成功轨迹进行微调，丢弃了大量包含丰富失败信息的失败轨迹。本文旨在探索如何利用失败轨迹来改进智能体，实现更高效的数据利用。

Method: 提出失败驱动的自改进循环：1) 收集智能体在可验证环境中的失败轨迹；2) 使用 LLM 诊断失败模式并提出推理时解决方案；3) 生成代码补丁（经人工轻量验证）来升级智能体；4) 在推理时应用补丁提升性能，无需额外训练。

Result: 在 OSWorld 基准上，OpenCUA-72B 模型成功率从 42.3% 提升至 48.9%（+6.6 个百分点），无需额外训练成本，仅增加少量推理开销。

Conclusion: 失败驱动的自改进是成功驱动管线的有效补充，能够更高效地利用数据提升计算机使用智能体的性能，为智能体改进提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于 MLLM 的计算机使用智能体（computer-use agent），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉语言模型与 MLLM 应用。论文提出的失败驱动自改进方法属于 MLLM 智能体的训练与推理优化，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on agent self-improvement from failures, not directly on core priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: MLLM agent improvement, failure-driven self-improvement, inference-time solutions, OSWorld benchmark, no additional training cost

Abstract: Computer-use agents, which leverage multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to operate computers and complete tasks, have attracted significant attention for their utility and versatility. A major challenge in developing these agents is collecting large-scale, high-quality trajectories. The standard approach generates synthetic data through a self-improving loop: an agent is placed in a verifiable environment and iteratively fine-tuned on its successful trajectories. Despite its effectiveness, this paradigm exploits only successful trajectories and discards the failed ones, even though failures carry rich information about a model's weaknesses. In this work, we explore a complementary failure-driven self-improvement loop, a data-centric paradigm that turns failed trajectories into agent improvements. Specifically, we employ an LLM to diagnose failure modes, propose inference-time solutions, and generate code patches -- lightly verified by humans -- that upgrade the agent. We validate this approach with the state-of-the-art OpenCUA-72B model on the OSWorld benchmark, improving the success rate from 42.3% to 48.9%, a gain of 6.6 percentage points, without any additional training cost and with only modest inference overhead. Our results demonstrate that failure-driven self-improvement is a viable complement to success-based pipelines, enabling more efficient agent improvement.

</details>


### [126] [Agentic RAG-VLM: Affordance-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Self-Reflective Planning for Robotic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31200)
*Tao Chen, Lizheng Liu, Jiaxu Wang, Ziyue Jiang, Ruiqi Tian, JiGuang Huo, Zhongxue Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agentic RAG-VLM，一个将检索增强生成（RAG）与视觉语言模型（VLM）及自反思规划相结合的框架，用于杂乱环境中的机器人抓取。通过层次化功能感知 RAG、场景图约束推理器和自反思流水线，在12任务基准上达到78.3%的成功率，比纯VLM基线提升53.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的抓取方法仅依赖视觉相似性进行物体匹配，忽略了物理功能属性（如手柄可抓性、材料脆弱性），且以开环方式运行，缺乏空间推理和失败恢复能力，在物体密集或物理属性多样的环境中效果有限。

Method: 提出三个紧密耦合的组件：(1) 层次化功能感知RAG（HAA-RAG），编码四维功能描述符（类型、材料、脆弱性、可抓取区域），按功能兼容性而非视觉外观检索策略；(2) 场景图约束推理器，从VLM感知构建空间关系图，将邻近、遮挡、支撑约束转化为具体抓取参数调整；(3) 自反思流水线，包含14类失败分类法和三级自适应重试机制，实现闭环抓取优化。

Result: 在包含单次抓取、交互式和长程场景的12任务基准上（每配置360次试验），Agentic RAG-VLM达到78.3%的总体成功率，相比纯VLM基线绝对提升53.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 功能感知检索、场景图推理和智能体恢复机制对于鲁棒的机器人操作是共同必要的，该框架有效弥合了VLM语义理解与物理抓取执行之间的鸿沟。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Agentic RAG-VLM，将 VLM 与检索增强生成（RAG）及自反思规划相结合用于机器人抓取。核心是 VLM 的扩展应用，属于通用视觉语言模型（VLM）的范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general 子主题。

Importance reason: Combines RAG with VLM for robotic grasping, but focuses on affordance and manipulation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: RAG-VLM, robotic grasping, affordance-aware retrieval, self-reflective planning, scene graph reasoning

Abstract: Generalizable robotic grasping in cluttered environments is essential for deploying manipulators in unstructured human spaces, yet existing VLM-based methods rely on visual similarity for object matching, neglecting physical affordances such as handle graspability and material fragility, and operate open-loop without spatial reasoning or failure recovery, limiting their effectiveness when objects are densely packed or physically diverse. We present Agentic RAG-VLM, a unified framework that bridges VLM-based semantic understanding and physically grounded grasp execution by integrating retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with vision-language models (VLMs) and agentic self-reflective planning. Agentic RAG-VLM introduces three tightly coupled components: (1) a Hierarchical Affordance-Aware RAG (HAA-RAG) that encodes four-dimensional affordance descriptors, including type, material, fragility, and graspable region, and retrieves strategies by functional affordance compatibility rather than visual appearance; (2) a Scene Graph Constraint Reasoner that constructs spatial relationship graphs from VLM perception and translates proximity, occlusion, and support constraints into concrete grasp parameter adjustments; and (3) an Agentic Self-Reflective Pipeline with a 14-type failure taxonomy and three-level adaptive retry for closed-loop grasp refinement. Evaluated on a 12-task benchmark spanning single-grasp, interactive, and long-horizon scenarios with 360 trials per configuration, Agentic RAG-VLM achieves 78.3 percent overall success, a 53.3 percentage-point absolute gain over VLM-only baselines, demonstrating that affordance-aware retrieval, scene graph reasoning, and agentic recovery are jointly essential for robust manipulation.

</details>


### [127] [Embodied CAD: Solver-Grounded LLM Agents for Parametric B-Rep Assembly Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31252)
*Fumin Liu, Haoyu Zhou, Fei Hao, Lin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出Embodied CAD框架，利用求解器接地（solver-grounded）的LLM智能体进行参数化B-Rep装配建模。智能体通过分层技能库（L0-L4）迭代选择动作，在CAD后端执行并利用求解器反馈进行规划、修复和学习，结合动作语法约束、确定性参数解析和求解器奖励进行GRPO风格优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM可以生成语法上合理的CAD脚本，但工业级CAD建模要求每个特征、位置和装配关系必须被精确的几何内核接受，同时保持参数化边界表示几何的可编辑性。一次性生成完整脚本的方法难以满足这些严格约束。

Method: 提出Embodied CAD框架，包含：(1) 分层L0-L4 CAD技能库，从基础几何操作到高级装配约束；(2) 动作语法约束确保生成的操作合法；(3) 确定性参数解析将动作解析为精确的几何操作；(4) 在CAD后端执行并获取求解器反馈；(5) 利用求解器奖励进行监督预热和GRPO风格强化学习优化。

Result: 在多步机械、工业设备和模具装配任务上，求解器接地的规划方法在现有基准上执行了所有强规划器工作流；学习控制器达到了高可执行率，揭示了有效工具调用与精确长时域策略预测之间的差距。

Conclusion: 求解器接地（solver-grounded）的LLM智能体方法能够有效处理参数化CAD装配建模中的精确几何约束，迭代式动作选择与求解器反馈机制显著提升了脚本的可执行率和任务完成成功率。

Classification reason: 该论文使用LLM智能体进行参数化CAD装配建模，属于多模态理解与生成方向中视觉语言模型（MLLM）在工程/设计领域的应用。虽然不直接处理图像，但LLM通过CAD技能库与几何求解器交互，属于MLLM在结构化几何建模中的延伸应用，映射到[CANONICAL]的vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与MLLM）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on CAD assembly modeling with LLM agents, which is not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: CAD assembly modeling, solver-grounded LLM agents, parametric B-Rep, GRPO-style refinement, not in user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Large language models can write plausible CAD scripts, but reliable industrial CAD modeling requires more than syntactically valid code: every feature, placement, and assembly relation must be accepted by an exact geometric kernel while remaining editable as parametric boundary representation geometry. We present Embodied CAD, solver-grounded LLM agents for parametric B-Rep assembly modeling. Instead of generating a complete script in one pass, the agent iteratively selects actions from a stratified L0-L4 CAD skill library, resolves them into typed geometric operations, executes them in a CAD backend, and uses solver feedback to plan, repair, and learn. The framework combines action grammar constraints, deterministic parameter resolution, and solver-derived rewards for supervised warm-up and GRPO-style refinement. We evaluate Embodied CAD on multi-step mechanical, industrial equipment, and mold-oriented assembly tasks using solver-aligned metrics: executable rate, skill accuracy, operation-family accuracy, exact policy accuracy, and task completion success. The results show that solver-grounded planning executes all strong-planner workflows in the current benchmark, while learned controllers reach high executable rates and expose the remaining gap between valid tool calls and exact long-horizon policy prediction.

</details>


### [128] [Xiaomi-GUI-0 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31410)
*Wanxia Cao, Chengzhen Duan, Pei Fu, Pengzhi Gao, Niu Lian, Fazhan Liu, Hui Liu, Heng Qu, Qinzhuo Wu, Zhehao Yu, Tongbo Chen, Shiqi Cui, Anan Du, Shukai Jia, Yuanfa Li, Yike Liu, Wenchao Lu, Haoyuan Sun, Jiatong Sun, Cheng Tan, Yajie Wang, Changqiao Wu, Tao Xiong, Jiahui Yang, Yuxuan Yuan, Ruoceng Zhang, Shaojie Zhang, Jian Zhu, Jian Luan, Cong Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (83.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 小米提出 Xiaomi-GUI-0，一个面向真实移动环境的原生多模态 GUI Agent，采用真实设备主导的混合基础设施，通过多源训练数据、错误驱动数据飞轮和三阶段渐进训练（SFT→步级RL→智能体RL），在 RealMobile 和 AndroidWorld 上分别达到 72.0% 和 78.9% 的成功率，显著提升了真实场景的执行稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI Agent 主要在离线轨迹、模拟环境和标准化基准上训练与评测，与真实应用在界面布局、交互逻辑和异常状态分布上差异巨大，导致基准分数与真实可用性之间存在持续差距。为弥合这一差距，作者提出在真实设备闭环中训练和评估的原生多模态 GUI Agent。

Method: 提出 Xiaomi-GUI-0，核心包括：(1) 真实设备主导的混合基础设施，物理设备为主、沙箱为辅；(2) 多源训练数据，涵盖高频头部任务、长尾意图的高泛化数据、以及反思与记忆的能力增强数据；(3) 错误驱动数据飞轮，将失败轨迹转化为纠正动作、反思解释和恢复演示；(4) 渐进式三阶段训练：SFT、步级强化学习、智能体强化学习。

Result: 在自建 RealMobile 基准上达到 72.0% 成功率，在 AndroidWorld 上达到 78.9% 成功率，在真实世界任务中显著提升了执行稳定性和异常状态识别能力。

Conclusion: Xiaomi-GUI-0 通过真实设备闭环、多源数据构建和渐进式强化学习训练，有效弥合了 GUI Agent 在基准评测与真实部署之间的差距，验证了真实设备主导训练范式的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Xiaomi-GUI-0，一个面向真实移动环境的原生多模态 GUI Agent，基于视觉语言模型构建，涉及 SFT、强化学习训练与真实设备闭环评测。属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 vlm_mllm_general。

Importance reason: Strong GUI agent work with real-device training and RL pipeline, but not directly aligned with top priority continuous multimodal or video generation subtopics. Signals: GUI agent, real-device closed loop, error-driven data flywheel, step-level and agentic RL, AndroidWorld 78.9% success

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents build on vision-language models to complete user tasks end-to-end in real applications through interface actions such as tapping, swiping, text entry, and navigation. However, existing GUI agents are trained and evaluated largely on offline trajectories, simulated environments, and standardized benchmarks. These differ substantially from real applications in interface layout, interaction logic, and abnormal-state distribution, and cannot faithfully characterize execution stability in real-world use, where account states, permission dialogs, payment authentication, and risk control continually reshape the state distribution and open a persistent gap between benchmark scores and real usability. To close this gap, we propose Xiaomi-GUI-0, a native multimodal GUI agent for real mobile environments, trained and evaluated within a real-device closed loop. At its core is a real-device-dominant hybrid infrastructure, where physical devices are the primary execution environment and sandboxes provide auxiliary support, so that data collection, training, rollout, and evaluation share an execution distribution close to real deployment. We construct multi-source training data spanning high-frequency head tasks, high-generalization data for long-tail intents, and capability-enhancement data for reflection and memory, and introduce an error-driven data flywheel that turns failure trajectories into corrected actions, reflective explanations, and recovery demonstrations. The model is trained through a progressive three-stage pipeline of supervised fine-tuning, step-level reinforcement learning, and agentic reinforcement learning. Evaluated on public benchmarks and our in-house RealMobile, Xiaomi-GUI-0 achieves 72.0% success on RealMobile and 78.9% on AndroidWorld, while substantially improving execution stability and abnormal-state recognition in real-world tasks.

</details>

### [129] [LOPA: Enhancing Spoken Language Assessment via Latent Ordinal Prototype Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31310)
*Hong-Yun Lin, Fu-An Chao, Bi-Cheng Yan, Berlin Chen*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (67.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LOPA 提出了一种基于潜空间序数原型对齐的口语语言评估方法，通过原型正则化器在潜空间中施加序数几何先验，结合语义锚定层路由（SALR）自适应提取冻结 Whisper 编码器的多层表示，以 0.361 RMSE 的性能媲美十亿参数级系统，无需 LLM 微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 MLLM 的口语评估方法忽视了语言习得固有的序数结构（如从初级到高级的渐进式能力），且依赖大规模模型微调，计算成本高昂。本文旨在设计一种轻量级、序数感知的替代方案。

Method: 提出 LOPA（Latent Ordinal Prototype Alignment），一种原型正则化器，在潜空间中强制施加序数几何先验；同时提出 SALR（Semantic-Anchored Layer Routing），自适应地从冻结的 Whisper 编码器中提取多层深度表示，两者协同工作实现高效的口语评估。

Result: 在口语评估任务上达到 RMSE 0.361，性能媲美十亿参数级系统，且无需 LLM 微调。分析表明 SALR 与 LOPA 的协同提供了可解释的、与评估标准对齐的偏好。

Conclusion: LOPA 证明了通过潜空间序数原型对齐，可以在不依赖大规模 MLLM 微调的情况下实现高效、序数感知的口语语言评估，为当前以规模为中心的建模范式提供了一种替代方案。

Classification reason: 论文研究口语语言评估（Spoken Language Assessment），属于多模态理解中的语音+文本问答任务，与视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）在任务范式上最为接近，均涉及多模态输入下的理解与评估。

Importance reason: Focuses on spoken language assessment, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: spoken language assessment, ordinal prototype alignment, efficient alternative to MLLMs, no LLM fine-tuning, RMSE 0.361

Abstract: Fueled by increasing model scale and multimodal inputs, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for Spoken Language Assessment (SLA). While effective, this paradigm often overlooks the intrinsic ordinal structure of language acquisition. This paper works around the necessity of large-scale MLLMs by introducing Latent Ordinal Prototype Alignment (LOPA) for SLA, a prototype-based regularizer that enforces an ordinal geometric prior directly on the latent space. Coupled with Semantic-Anchored Layer Routing (SALR), which adaptively harvests multi-depth representations from a frozen Whisper encoder, our framework achieves an RMSE of 0.361. This performance rivals billion-parameter systems without the need for LLM-based fine-tuning. Further analysis reveals that SALR's synergy with LOPA offers interpretable, criterion-aligned preferences, thereby supporting an efficient and ordinal-aware modeling alternative to current scaling-centric models for SLA.

</details>


### [130] [HyperVLP: Enhancing Hierarchical Surgical Video-Language Pre-training in Hyperbolic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31245)
*Yaojun Hu, Kun Yuan, Nassir Navab, Haochao Ying, Jian Wu, Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HyperVLP 提出在双曲空间中建模手术视频-语言预训练中的层次结构（动作→步骤→阶段），通过缓解结构假负例和保持父子阶段语义一致性，在零样本和少样本手术阶段识别任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视觉语言模型将多层次的语义信号（细粒度动作、中层级关键步骤、全局过程上下文）压缩到单一平坦嵌入空间，忽略了跨层级的语义包含关系（如动作属于步骤、步骤组成阶段），导致长程依赖建模能力弱。

Method: 提出双曲空间中的手术视频-语言预训练框架 HyperVLP：1）利用双曲空间的层次几何特性显式建模手术知识的层级结构；2）设计结构假负例缓解策略，避免过程上下文导致的错误负样本；3）引入父子阶段语义一致性约束，确保父阶段与其子步骤的嵌入对齐。

Result: 在多个手术基准数据集上的零样本和少样本阶段识别任务中，HyperVLP 一致优于现有方法，跨手术流程和机构的泛化能力显著提升。

Conclusion: 双曲空间能有效建模手术视频-语言预训练中的层次语义结构，缓解平坦嵌入空间的局限性，为手术视频理解提供了一种新的预训练范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向手术视频的语言-视觉预训练框架，属于医学/手术领域的视频理解与问答任务。虽然领域特殊，但核心是视频语言预训练与视频理解，与多模态理解与生成方向下的"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）中的视频理解、VQA任务高度相关，且该[CANONICAL] subtopic可覆盖手术视频理解这一应用场景。

Importance reason: Hyperbolic space for surgical video-language pre-training is niche and not aligned with your priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: surgical domain, hyperbolic embedding, hierarchical video-language, zero-shot phase recognition, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Surgical vision-language foundation models typically adopt educational materials, such as surgical lecture videos, to transfer surgical knowledge encoded in language into visual representations. These knowledge are multi-dimensional and hierarchical: fine-grained action cues appear in narration, mid-level key steps are summarized in subsection headings, and global procedural context, such as patient history and surgical strategy, is described in abstract texts. Prior work largely collapses these heterogeneous signals into a single flat embedding space, implicitly assuming independence across hierarchy levels. However, this is suboptimal because it ignores cross-level semantic containment, e.g., actions belong to steps, steps compose phases, weakens long-range dependency modeling. To this end, we propose a hyperbolic surgical video-language pre-training framework that explicitly preserves the hierarchical structure by mitigating structural false negatives induced by procedural context and enforcing semantic consistency between parent phases and their constituent child steps. Extensive experiments on multiple surgical benchmarks show consistent gains in zero- and few-shot phase recognition across procedures and institutions.

</details>

## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [131] [CoLT: Teaching Multi-Modal Models to Think with Chain of Latent Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31986)
*Lianyu Hu, Shengqian Qin, Zeqin Liao, Qing Guo, Liang Wan, Wei Feng, Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 高优先级 (91.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CoLT 提出了一种让多模态大模型在连续潜空间中进行链式推理（Chain of Latent Thoughts）的框架，仅需3步潜状态即可完成复杂视觉推理，相比文本 CoT 推理速度提升10倍以上，同时通过前向/后向解码器监督和内部一致性损失来稳定潜空间推理训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态 CoT 推理依赖生成冗长的自然语言中间步骤，推理速度慢且受限于自然语言的表达能力。作者希望让模型在连续潜空间中直接进行推理，以更少的步骤实现更高效的推理，同时避免潜空间推理中常见的语义丢失和训练不稳定问题。

Method: CoLT 框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 前向解码器——将当前潜状态解码为下一步的文本推理，提供前向监督；(2) 后向解码器——给定前文文本上下文，将解码器隐状态与模型潜状态对齐，提供后向监督；(3) 内部一致性损失——鼓励相邻潜状态之间的平滑过渡。训练时使用解码器和内部监督，推理时移除解码器，仅保留潜状态链进行高效推理。

Result: 在8个基准上，CoLT 超越了现有潜推理方法（如 CODI、SIM-CoT）以及依赖辅助图像的潜视觉推理方法。相比文本 CoT，推理时间减少10.1倍，文本解码时间减少22.6倍。

Conclusion: CoLT 证明了在连续潜空间中进行链式推理的可行性和高效性，为多模态模型提供了一种替代文本 CoT 的轻量级推理范式，在保持推理质量的同时大幅提升推理速度。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CoLT（Chain of Latent Thoughts），让多模态模型在连续潜空间中逐步推理，而非生成冗长的文本思维链。这直接对应 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续多模态推理与潜空间思维"（continuous_multimodal_reasoning），该 subtopic 专门涵盖多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、chain-of-continuous-thought 等工作。同时，该方法也涉及连续语言/潜空间建模，因此也匹配 continuous_language_multimodal 方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances the user's top-priority continuous multimodal reasoning with latent thoughts, achieving massive inference speedup. Signals: continuous latent reasoning, chain of latent thoughts, 10x inference speedup, step-level latent supervision, multimodal reasoning efficiency

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has enabled multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) to tackle complex visual reasoning tasks by generating explicit intermediate reasoning steps in natural language. However, this text-based reasoning paradigm is inherently slow at inference time with even thousands of tokens and fundamentally constrained by the expressiveness of natural language. In this paper, we propose CoLT, (Chain of Latent Thoughts), a novel framework that teaches multi-modal models to reason through a chain of latent thought representations instead of verbose text tokens, which can perform thinking with as few as 3 steps. Naively forcing the model to think with latent states easily produces meaningless semantics and makes training unstable. To effectively regulate the latent reasoning process, we introduce a lightweight external decoder that provides step-level supervision for each latent reasoning step in two complementary directions: a forward mode that decodes latent thoughts into the textual reasoning of the next step, and a backward mode that aligns decoder hidden states with the model's latent thoughts given preceding textual context. We further incorporate internal supervision that encourages coherent step-by-step latent transitions. The decoder and internal supervision are removed during inference to maintain high efficiency of latent reasoning. Extensive experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate that CoLT not only outperforms existing latent reasoning methods such as CODI and SIM-CoT, but also surpasses latent visual reasoning approaches that rely on auxiliary images with costly annotation requirements. Compared to text CoT methods, CoLT can notably reduce the inference time by 10.1$\times$ and text decoding time by 22.6$\times$. Code is released at .

</details>
